If a fortunate Jupiter <note>original: "Iuppiter fortunatus"</note> beholds the Moon with a quartile aspect, the newborn will be well-sensated, of healthy children, and of full understanding, provided the Lord Jupiter is not in the Midheaven. When Mercury is fortunate in the Midheaven and beholds Venus with a quartile aspect, the newborn will be sensible and intelligent. When Mercury is with Saturn in the house of Saturn, and in a good aspect with the Moon while it is increasing in number and light, the newborn will be well-sensated, of beautiful faith, and an organizer of things. When Mercury, Venus, and the Sun behold the Moon with a good aspect, the newborn will be well-sensated and grave in his actions. When Mars and Mercury are fortunate and free from retrograde motion and combustion, and behold the Moon increased in number and light, the newborn will be of good intellect. When Mars and Venus are fortunate and one of them beholds the Sun, the newborn will be grave in his actions, of good intellect, and a warrior. When Jupiter is in the Ascendant and Venus is in the Descendant, the newborn will be of subtle intellect and complete in sense. When Saturn is in any of his dignities in the Descendant or Midheaven with Mercury, the newborn will be of good intellect, of a powerful mind in battle, and like a wise man.

### On those born to foolishness. Chapter 31.

**W**hen the Moon is diminished in light and number, and also separated from Mars and "wild" <note>original: "feralis" - a term for a planet that does not make aspects to others</note>, the newborn will be a fool. When the Moon is separated from the opposition of the Sun in the 7th house and Mars beholds it with an unfortunate aspect, the newborn will be a fool. When the Part of Fortune or of Faith is in the 9th or 3rd house and an ill-fortune <note>a malefic planet like Saturn or Mars</note> beholds them, the newborn will be a fool. When Venus is with Saturn, and Mars and Mercury are in the place of the Moon, the newborn will be a fool and, because of his foolishness, will name himself first, and men will mock him. When Saturn and Mercury are "pilgrims" <note>original: "peregrini" - planets in a sign where they have no essential dignity</note> in the Ascendant and Jupiter is in their opposition, the newborn will be a fool and of base foolishness.

### On those born to be actors. Chapter 32.

**W**hen Mercury is in his own house with Mars, the newborn will be an actor <note>original: "hystrio" - can mean a stage actor, minstrel, or jester</note>. When Mars and Mercury are in a royal sign and the Sun beholds that sign with a good aspect, he will be an actor for kings and lords, and they will rejoice in him and his words. When Mars and Mercury are in the house of Saturn and falling from the angles, and the fortunate Moon increasing in light beholds them, he will be an actor for the common people and will play and joke with the crowd. When Venus beholds Mercury or is joined with him, he will be such a subtle actor that men will marvel at his wonderful deeds. When Mars, Mercury, and the Moon are joined together in the house of Venus, the newborn will be an actor of much and beautiful speech.

### On the faithfulness of the newborn. Chapter 33.

**W**hen Venus is in the Midheaven without an evil aspect from Mercury, the newborn will be faithful and will faithfully keep his promises, especially if it is a nocturnal birth. When Mercury is with Jupiter, the newborn will be faithful and high and sublime men will trust in him. When Jupiter beholds Saturn and Mars with a good aspect, the newborn will be good and faithful and will faithfully keep the promises made to him. When Venus is in the Ascendant and in her exaltation, the newborn will be a faithful preserver of promises. When Jupiter is in the 3rd house, strangers will offer their money to the newborn for safekeeping, and men will trust in him. When Mercury is in the 5th house and in his own face <note>a minor dignity</note>, the newborn will be a faithful scribe and will have honor because of his faithfulness. When Mercury is oriental and in the 2nd house, the newborn will be faithful to strangers.

### On those born wicked and their unfaithfulness. Chapter 34.

**W**hen unfortunate Mercury from the Midheaven beholds Mars and the Moon is in the 4th house, the newborn will be wicked and unfaithful, denying men what was promised to them. When Mars is a pilgrim and retrograde, and the Moon is with him in the 5th house, the newborn will be wicked, unfaithful, a seizer of others' goods, and one who withholds what was promised. When Mercury is in the term of Mars and in the 3rd house with the Moon, while being a pilgrim and retrograde, the newborn will be of little faithfulness, especially if Mercury is the Lord of the Ascendant.

### On those born to be thieves. Chapter 35.

**W**hen Mars, Mercury, and the Moon are with any angle and are unfortunate without an aspect from a fortune <note>a benefic planet like Jupiter or Venus</note>, the newborn will be a thief. When Saturn is a pilgrim and "wild" in the 7th house, as well as retrograde and in an unfortunate aspect of Mars, Mercury, and the Moon, the newborn will be a hidden thief and his end will be hanging. When Mars is without the aspect of any fortune in any angle and is retrograde, pilgrim, and weak, the newborn will be a thief, especially if Saturn beholds him with an evil aspect. When Saturn, Mars, and Mercury are in the Descendant, retrograde and "wild," and the Moon, diminished in light and number and without any of their dignities, beholds them by conjunction, aspect, or opposition, the newborn will be a thief and will end his days by a bad death because of his thievery. When Mars and Mercury are joined in any angle and are "wild" or stand without any dignity, and Saturn beholds them with an evil aspect, the newborn will steal the shrouds and clothes of the dead.

### On those born as fornicators. Chapter 36.

**W**hen Venus is in the Descendant or Midheaven and Mercury is with her in the term of Mars, the newborn will be a fornicator and will have many people in fornication. When Venus and Mars are without the aspect of Jupiter in the Midheaven or Descendant, the newborn will be a fornicator and of ill fame. When Saturn, Venus, and Mercury are in the house or exaltation of Venus, and also in the Descendant or Midheaven without the aspect of Jupiter, he will be a fornicator and will spend whatever he has on fornication. When Mars and Venus are without the aspect of Jupiter in the Midheaven or in the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house</note>, the newborn will be a fornicator and will rejoice in this. When Venus is in the Midheaven and the Moon beholds Saturn and Saturn beholds Venus, the newborn will be a pimp <note>original: "Leno"</note> and a follower of harlots. When Saturn and Mars are in the 6th house and Venus beholds them with a good aspect without the aspect of Jupiter, the newborn will be a pursuer of harlots. When Venus is in her own house in a diurnal birth and Mars radiates toward her from his own house with a good aspect, the newborn will frequent harlots. When Venus is in the house of Mars and Mars is in the house of Venus, the newborn will be a manifest fornicator without shame. When Venus is joined with Mars or in a quartile or opposite aspect, the newborn will commit evil and base fornications. If Mars and Venus are in a masculine sign, the newborn will be a fornicator. If Venus is effeminate, the newborn will be very effeminate and will love fornications beyond nature.

### On those born to sodomy and its natures. Chapter 37.

**W**hen the 6th sign is masculine and Mars and Venus are in it without the aspect of Jupiter, the newborn will be a sodomite. And if this Venus is in the house of Mercury and in a falling sign and unfortunate, you shall judge so. And if Mercury is the Lord of the [missing word], he will be a sodomite. And if the Part of Fortune is with Mercury in any angle and in a masculine sign, you shall judge so. If Mercury from a masculine sign beholds the house of marriage without the aspect of Jupiter, and Venus is in a masculine sign and unfortunes Mercury, the newborn will be a sodomite. If Mercury is tied to the Part of Marriage, or the Lord of that part is masculine and in a masculine sign or place, the newborn will be a sodomite. When the Moon is in a masculine sign and the Sun and Venus behold them from a masculine sign, the newborn will be a sodomite. When Mercury and Venus are joined in the 7th house, the newborn will be a lord of suitors and a sodomite. If Mercury is in the 7th house and Venus is falling from him and is the Lady of the Ascendant, the newborn will be a sodomite. If Mercury is in the 7th house with Mars and Venus is a pilgrim and beholds them with an evil aspect from a masculine sign, the newborn will be a sodomite. When Venus is in the house and term of Mars and in an evil place, the newborn will be a sodomite. When Mercury, Mars, and Saturn are in the house of his body, the newborn will be a sodomite, especially if Venus is with Mercury in a masculine sign. When the Part of Marriage is in the house of Mercury and in a masculine sign and in any of the angles, the newborn will be a sodomite. When Venus and the Moon are in the 8th house in a woman's birth, she will be a sodomite. When Venus is in the 7th house and in a masculine sign and the Moon is in the Ascendant, she will be a sodomite. When feminine planets are in masculine signs and places, she will be [born] of adultery and will desire to lie with women.

### On the chastity or prohibition of sodomy in those born. Chapter 38.

**W**hen Venus is in the house of Jupiter and free from the evil aspect of Mars, the newborn will be of good complexion and chaste regarding the sin of sodomy. When Venus is in the Ascendant or Midheaven with Jupiter, or from the places from which she receives him...

# ¶ On the effeminacy of the newborn. Chapter 39.

When Mercury and Saturn are with Venus in the ascendant or in the midheaven: the newborn will be effeminate. ¶ When the Sun and Moon are in the 3rd house: the newborn will be effeminate and his manners like the manners of a woman: and he will acquire his living from that effeminacy. ¶ When Venus is in Aries, Taurus, Leo, or Capricorn: and Mars and the Sun have cast their rays upon Saturn: the newborn will be effeminate: and will desire men. ¶ When the Moon is in the house of Saturn and Saturn is in the house of Venus or in Scorpio and in the <term>angle of the earth</term><note>the 4th house or Imum Coeli</note> or in the 6th or 12th house, the newborn will have womanly manners. ¶ When Venus is with a <term>misfortune</term><note>a malefic planet like Mars or Saturn</note> existing in a fixed sign: the newborn will be as if a woman and will act womanly: and furthermore will desire men as if he were a woman.

# ¶ On the liberality of the newborn. Chapter 40.

When the Sun and Venus look upon the Moon extended in the midheaven, the newborn will be generous, liberal, and easily spending or giving away his goods. ¶ When Mars looks upon the Sun with a 4th aspect: the newborn will be so liberal that little or nothing of his substance will remain. ¶ When Mars is the lord of the <term>Part of Fortune</term> and the lord of the ascendant is in the 2nd house, the newborn will be prodigal and uselessly consuming his goods. ¶ When Saturn is retrograde or <term>combust</term><note>so close to the sun that its influence is "burned up" or hidden</note> and existing in any of his dignities in the midheaven: the newborn will not benefit others from his goods so that he will come to poverty, especially if Venus looks upon him with a bad aspect. ¶ When the lord of the 3rd house looks upon the lord of the 2nd house with a bad aspect: the newborn will be prodigal and will acquire enmities and hatred by occasion of his riches.

# ¶ On the avarice of the newborn. Chapter 41.

When the <term>Head of the Dragon</term><note>the North Node of the Moon</note> is with the Sun, Venus, and Mercury in the ascendant: the newborn will be greedy. ¶ When Mercury in a daytime birth is oriental and in the 2nd house: the one who is born will be stingy and loving his money beyond measure.

# ¶ On the envy of the newborn. Chapter 42.

When Mercury, swift in course in a nighttime birth, appears from under the rays of the western sun and is in the 2nd house and in the <term>term</term><note>a specific degree-based subdivision of a zodiac sign</note> of Mars: the newborn will be envious. ¶ When the Moon, separated from Saturn, applies to Mercury, the newborn will be envious. ¶ When Mercury, bound to Mars, is in the ascendant and Jupiter does not look upon them: the newborn will be envious.

# ¶ On the pride or magnanimity of the newborn. Chapter 43.

When Venus and Saturn are with the Moon or look upon it: the newborn will be proud and boastful, especially if they are elevated. ¶ When Mercury, Jupiter, and Mars are looking at the Moon together: the newborn will be of great spirit and a speaker of great things. ¶ When the Moon, separated from Saturn, binds to Mercury: the newborn will be magnanimous and without shame. ¶ When [the planet] is in its own house and in the term of Mars and binds to the Sun in opposition from the 7th house, the newborn will be of a proud spirit and will seek to appear great: and because of this, he will dress himself well.

# ¶ On the cheerfulness of the newborn's face. Chapter 44.

When Venus is in her house or exaltation and in the 7th house and Mars looks upon her with a good aspect: the newborn will be of a cheerful face and of good response: yet he will be a flatterer and as if a fool. ¶ When Venus is in the midheaven or the 7th house and Mercury looks upon her with a good aspect: the newborn will be of a cheerful face, yet a flatterer and of a good tongue. ¶ When Venus is in the 7th house and in her own <term>face</term><note>a 10-degree subdivision of a sign, also called a decan</note>: and Mars looks upon her from his own house with a bad aspect: the newborn will be without shame and a flatterer. ¶ When the Moon is in the ascendant and Venus looks upon her with a 3rd aspect: the newborn will be of a cheerful face and of good meeting.

# ¶ On the joy of the newborn. Chapter 45.

When the Moon, diminished in light, is in her own house and in a good place with Venus, the newborn will be joyful and will travel through joyful places for the sake of joy without laziness. ¶ When the Moon and the lord of the ascendant are oriental and fortunate and are free from the aspect of the misfortunes: the newborn will be joyful. ¶ When Mercury is in the house or exaltation of the Moon, direct and outside the sun's rays: and Venus looks upon him with a good aspect, and he looks upon the Moon: the newborn will be joyful, yet mobile and of much change. ¶ When Mercury is in the ascendant, 10th, 11th, or 5th house: and the Moon from the exaltation of Mars or the lord of the house of the Moon or the lord of the 10th looks upon him: the newborn will be joyful and mobile in all his affairs.

# ¶ On the sadness of the newborn. Chapter 46.

When Saturn is the <term>Almuten</term><note>the planet with the most dignity or power over a specific point</note> of the nativity and at the end of the lord of the ascendant without the aspect of a fortune: the newborn will be sad, evil-speaking, and unhealthy. ¶ When Mars is the lord of the face of the ascendant and Saturn looks upon him with a good aspect and Venus from the opposite: the newborn will be sad. ¶ When Venus is unfortunate and does not look upon the 7th house. ¶ If Mars and Saturn look upon the Moon: the newborn will be sad and of many thoughts, yet proud and of a hard heart. ¶ When Mercury is in the midheaven with Saturn: the newborn will be weak, lazy, and without joy, and similarly if the Moon is in the house of Saturn. ¶ When the Moon is <term>void of course</term><note>moving through a sign without making an aspect to another planet</note>: and the misfortunes look upon her, the newborn will be without joy, lazy, and weak. ¶ When the Moon and Saturn are oriental and joined together: the newborn will be without joy. ¶ When the Moon, separated from Venus, applies by conjunction to Mars in the house of Saturn and in the places of <term>abscission</term><note>a cutting off of light or influence</note>: the newborn will be without joy and diminished in things and work. ¶ When the Moon, separated from Mars, is joined to Mercury in the house of Saturn and both are oriental: the newborn will be without joy, lazy, and a wretched man.

# ¶ On the greediness of the newborn. Chapter 47.

When Mars is fortunate in a daytime birth in the 4th house: the newborn will eat greedily and thereby incur illnesses. ¶ When [a sign] is ascending and Mars is there and the Sun looks upon him with a good aspect: the newborn will be a greedy eater. ¶ When Mars is the lord of the ascendant and in the 2nd house and in a fiery sign: the newborn will eat greedily. ¶ When Venus is in the first face of Leo and in a bad place and the Sun looks upon her with a good aspect, the newborn will be a great eater.

# ¶ On newborns sowing discord among men. Chapter 48.

When Mars is in the 9th house, falling from Jupiter and Venus, the newborn will be a sower of discord and war. ¶ When the Moon, separated from the part existing in the ascendant, binds to itself placed in the 7th house: and Mars is in the 4th house: the newborn will sow discord among men. ¶ When the ascendant is Scorpio and Mars looks upon it with a bad aspect: the newborn will be a sower of discord.

# ¶ On the evil thoughts of the newborn. Chapter 49.

When Mars and the Sun are in one place: the newborn will be of evil thought. ¶ When Mercury is in the 6th or 12th house and the 1st house is one of the houses of Mars: the newborn will always think evil and speak ill of others.

# ¶ On the beauty of the newborn. Chapter 50.

When Venus looks upon the Moon with a 3rd aspect, the newborn will be cheerful and adorned like a woman. ¶ When Venus looks upon Jupiter with a 4th aspect, the newborn will be of beautiful stature and of good being. ¶ When the Moon looks upon Venus with a 4th aspect: the newborn will desire garments of silk: and will be of great vice and quietude.

# ¶ On the weightiness or gravity and quietude of newborns. Chapter 51.

When the <term>Part of Fortune</term> falls in Capricorn: the newborn will be quiet and will act with deliberation. ¶ When the Moon with Saturn is in the ascendant or midheaven: and Jupiter looks upon her: the newborn will be quiet. ¶ When Jupiter is in the ascendant and Saturn looks upon him, and Mars: and its lord is falling in the ascendant: the newborn will be weighty and quiet. ¶ When the Moon, without the aspect of Mars, is with Jupiter in a fixed sign: the newborn will be weighty and quiet: and this more strongly if she is with Jupiter in any of the <term>angles</term><note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>.

# ¶ On the speed of the newborn. Chapter 52.

When Mars, without the aspect of Jupiter, is in the ascendant, the newborn will do his things quickly and without deliberation and then will be reputed a fool. ¶ When the Moon is in the ascendant: and the ascending sign is a fiery sign and Jupiter looks upon it from the 7th house: the newborn will be too quick in acting and as if a fool because of too much speed. ¶ When Mars, without the aspect of the Moon, is in the 4th house: the newborn will look little to the end of his affairs. ¶ When the Moon and the lord of the ascendant are in the midheaven: and Mars looks upon them without the aspect of Jupiter. The newborn will be treacherous and without any prudence: and one who will acquire for himself lawsuits and discords.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of astrological influences on the human body, specifically focusing on physical ailments and eye conditions determined by the positions of the planets at the moment of birth.</meta>

### On the illnesses of the native. Chapter 53.
**W**hen the Sun in a daytime birth is hindered by any misfortune <note>original: "infortuna," referring to a malefic planet like Saturn or Mars</note> of the eyes, the native will have an illness in the right eye, or perhaps will be one-eyed or cross-eyed. And if the Sun is so disposed in a nocturnal birth, the native will have an illness in the stomach or brain. When the Moon is above the earth and unfortunate, the native will suffer an illness in the left eye. But if she is unfortunate beneath the earth, it signifies pain of the neck, throat, or lungs arising from coldness and humidity. When Mercury is unfortunate above the earth, it portends illness of the tongue. And if he is unfortunate beneath the earth, the illness will be in "fistulous" places <note>likely referring to ulcers or abscesses</note>. If Venus and Mars are unfortunate, it signifies an illness of the nostrils, such that the native will not perceive or desire scents, and perhaps will suffer a "distillation" <note>a discharge or catarrh</note> in them. And if they are unfortunate beneath the earth, there will be illness in the liver, veins, kidneys, or urinary tracts, along with a common appetite. When Saturn and Jupiter are unfortunate above the earth—if they are burnt <note>term: "combust" (perbusti); a planet too close to the Sun, weakening its power</note> or in opposition to their "heights" <note>apogees</note>, or in bad places, or in dark degrees—it signifies illness in the ears or their passages. And if they are unfortunate beneath the earth, there will be pain in the spleen or the spirit, like those who believe they will die suddenly. Certain wise men have said that when the Moon is above the earth in a nocturnal birth, her significance is over the right eye, and the Sun in a nocturnal birth is over the left eye. When the misfortunes <note>malefic planets</note> are in the "angles" <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note> and no fortune <note>benefic planet like Jupiter or Venus</note> regards them, nor has any dignity there, it signifies chronic or inseparable illnesses. When the Moon is separated from any planet and a fortune does not regard her, it portends illnesses. When the Moon is in the 6th house without the aspect of a fortune and the misfortunes regard her, it portends chronic illnesses. When the lord of the 6th house is in the Ascendant and in a "movable" sign <note>Aries, Cancer, Libra, or Capricorn</note>, and the fortunes are falling away from it, the native's illnesses will be chronic and inseparable. When Mars is in Libra without the power of Jupiter, it signifies chronic illnesses. When the Moon is in the Ascendant and the misfortunes regard her, it signifies illnesses and pains. When the misfortunes are in the angles and regard the "luminaries" <note>the Sun and Moon</note>, the native will have an inseparable illness in his body. When the misfortunes are "oriental" <note>rising before the Sun</note> under the rays, it signifies illnesses; if they are "occidental" <note>setting after the Sun</note>, he will have misery and weakness. When the Moon is bound with a misfortune, it signifies chronic illnesses and weakness of the body. When the sign of the 6th house is hindered by the aspect or rays of a misfortune, it signifies illnesses. When the sign of the Ascendant is movable, the said illnesses will be in the eyes. And if the sign of the 6th house is movable and its lord is in a movable sign, the native's illnesses will arise from phlegm. And if the lord of the lord of the 6th house regards it, they will be from heat or fire. And if Saturn and Jupiter regard the said lord, they will be of the nature of heat and humidity. And if Venus is the lady of the 6th house, the native's illnesses will be around the kidneys and private parts, or from excessive intercourse. And if Mercury is the lord of the 6th house, the native's illnesses will be in the tongue or hearing. And if the Moon is the lady of the 6th house, the native's illnesses will be in the genitals and lower parts. And these things we have said concerning illnesses in general. But because the wise men in their books have placed particular significations, we have proposed to declare them in the following sections.

### On blindness and illnesses of the eyes. Chapter 54.
**W**hen the Sun and Moon are besieged by misfortunes, and the Ascendant is unfortunate and its lord is in a bad place, and also the fortunes are falling away from the Ascendant, the native will be blind or will have an inseparable illness in the eyes. When the Moon is diminished in light and number in the 6th house, and Mars regards her from the 4th house or in opposition, it signifies blindness. When the misfortunes are in the same degree with the luminaries, or the luminaries regard them without the aspect of a fortune from the 4th house or in opposition, the native will lose his sight. When the Sun is the lord of the 6th house and is made unfortunate by the bad aspect of some misfortune without the aspect of a fortune, it signifies illness in the eyes. When the Moon is the lady of the 6th house, being in the degree of the Ascendant, and is made unfortunate by a bad aspect of a misfortune, and the fortunes do not regard her, the native will have an illness in the eyes. When the "Tail of the Dragon" <note>term: "Cauda Draconis"; the South Node of the Moon, considered malefic</note> is in the degree of the Ascendant and the luminaries are unfortunate, the native will lose something from the eyes, unless the fortunes regard them there with a good aspect. When Mars is the lord of the 6th house, and the Ascendant is a movable sign, and the Moon is diminished in light and number with the Tail of the Dragon and in the 4th or opposite aspect of Mars, the native will lose his sight. When Mars, being above the Moon in the 7th house, is burnt, it signifies illness of the eyes. When a misfortune is in the 3rd house and Mars is unfortunate in the Ascendant, the native's eyes will be diseased. When the misfortunes are with the luminaries in "falling" places <note>cadent houses</note>, they signify illnesses of the eyes. When the luminaries are in signs signifying chronic illnesses, and the misfortunes regard them with a bad aspect, and the fortunes are falling away from them, the native will have illnesses in the eyes. When the Sun and Moon are in the 7th house and Mars rises immediately after the Moon, they signify illnesses of the eyes. When the sign of the 2nd house is one of the houses of Saturn, and the Sun—the lord of the Part of Fortune or the Ascendant—is in the 2nd house, it portends illnesses of the eyes. And it must be known that in certain signs there are certain degrees that destroy the sight: In Taurus, the place of *Achozade* <note>likely a corruption of an Arabic star name or degree</note> at the 6th, 9th, 7th, 8th, and 10th degrees. In Cancer, from the 9th degree to the 15th. In Leo, the place of *Alepheref* at the 18th, 27th, and 28th degrees. In Scorpio, the 19th and 28th. And according to Dionysius, in Scorpio the 8th, 5th, 9th, 10th, and 22nd. In Sagittarius, the 1st, 7th, 8th, and 9th degrees. In Capricorn, from the 26th degree to the 29th. In Aquarius, the 6th, 10th, and 19th.

### On the loss of one eye. Chapter 55.
**W**hen the Moon, increasing in light, is made unfortunate by Mars, or when diminished in light by Saturn, and the Sun is in a good place, it signifies the loss of the left eye or an inseparable infirmity. When the Sun in a daytime birth is made unfortunate and the Moon is free from misfortunes, it portends blindness in the right eye. When the Moon in a nocturnal birth is unfortunate above the earth and, being the lord of the 6th house, is with the Tail of the Dragon and the Sun is unfortunate, the native will be blind or will lose one eye. When the Sun in a daytime birth is eclipsed, it signifies the loss of the right eye. When the Sun is the lord of the 2nd house in a birth, the native will have an illness in the right eye, unless the fortunes regard him.

### On a cloudiness of the eyes. Chapter 56.
**W**hen any of the parts <note>Arabic Parts/Lots</note> is under the rays of the Sun, and especially Mars or Mercury, the native will have a "nebula" <note>cataract or corneal opacity</note> or a spot in the eye, such that the sight is hindered by it. When the Moon is with Mars in the same sign under the rays of the Sun, it signifies a nebula in the eye. When Venus is the lady of the 6th house in the Ascendant and a misfortune regards her with a bad aspect, the native will suffer from tearing of the eyes and humidity, or will have a scar or nebula in them.

### On one-eyed or cross-eyed people. Chapter 57.
**W**hen the Moon is joined to Saturn, he being oriental and under the rays of the Sun, the native will be cross-eyed or one-eyed. When the Sun and Moon are joined in a movable sign, and falling from the Ascendant, and also bound by misfortunes with the aspect of a fortune, the native will be one-eyed. When the Sun in a daytime birth and in a "tortuous" sign <note>signs of short ascension: Capricorn to Gemini</note> is hindered, and Mars regards the Sun, the native will be one-eyed, especially if the Sun is in a dark degree.

### On dark eyes. Chapter 58.
**W**hen the Moon is in the middle of Taurus or in the 6th, 9th, or 10th degree, or in the 19th degree of Cancer, or in the first, 7th, 9th, or 18th of Sagittarius, the native will have a nebula in the eye or his eyes will be dark. When the Moon is in the Ascendant and in the "Burnt Path" <note>term: "Via Combusta"; a specific section of the zodiac, usually late Libra to early Scorpio</note> or in a dark degree, or in a degree signifying the destruction of sight, it signifies darkness of the eyes. When the Sun is beneath the earth in the 4th aspect of Saturn, the native will have dark eyes. When the Moon is in any of the degrees signifying the destruction of sight, it signifies darkness of the eyes, especially if the Moon is diminished in light. And it must be known that in the signs there are certain dark degrees, namely: In Taurus, the 9th and 10th degrees. In Leo, the 5th, 12th, 14th, and 16th. In Virgo, the 13th, 19th, and 21st. In Libra, the 19th, 20th, and 21st. In Scorpio, the 21st, 22nd, and 23rd. In Capricorn, from the 11th degree to the 15th. In Aquarius, the 2nd, 3rd, and 4th.

### On weak sight. Chapter 59.
**W**hen the Moon, diminished in light, is in a bad aspect of Saturn and Mars, it signifies weakness of sight. If Saturn and Mars are in the 2nd sign from the Sun, and the Moon—unfortunate and diminished in number and light—regards him from the West or the Midheaven, the native will be of weak sight. When the Moon is besieged by misfortunes and a fortune regards her from the 4th or opposite aspect, it portends weakness of sight. When the Moon, diminished in light and number, [regards] Saturn existing in Sagittarius in the 4th or opposite aspect...

even if they behold it: the one born will have weak vision. When the Moon, at the hour of separation from its conjunction or prevention <note>original: "pruētione" (praeventione), referring to the full moon preceding birth</note> with the Sun, is bound to Saturn or Mars: the one born will have weak vision, or will suffer an inseparable sickness in the eyes. When the Moon appears from under the Sun's rays and an ill-fortune <note>a malefic planet like Mars or Saturn</note> beholds it with a bad aspect, it will weaken the vision of the one born. When the Moon is joined to Saturn under the Sun's rays, with Jupiter not beholding it there, it signifies weakness of vision and a chronic sickness of the eyes. When Mars and the Sun are in the western angle, and Mars is strong and oriental, there will be weakness in the vision of the one born or a chronic sickness in the eyes. When the Sun and Moon are in dark degrees and descending in their circle under the rays of Saturn, it signifies a watering of the eyes, especially if Saturn, being above them, is in humid signs. And the worst that can be in sicknesses of the eyes is when the Sun, being made unfortunate by Mars, suffers an eclipse: for this signifies the destruction of the eyes.

### Concerning sicknesses of the ears. Chapter 60.

**W**hen Mercury, being the lord of the 6th house, is in a bad place from the ascendant, and Saturn beholds it with a 4th aspect <note>a square aspect</note> or beholds the 6th house from the opposite side: the one born will be deaf. When the Moon, full of light, meets Mars in the 6th house: the one born will sometimes hear nothing. When Mercury, in a nocturnal nativity, appears from under the rays, and is also retrograde and in a bad aspect of a fortune <note>a benefic planet like Venus or Jupiter</note> in the 6th house: the one born will be heavy of hearing. When Mercury is in the house of Saturn, being unfortunate and in the 10th house, and an ill-fortune beholds it: the one born will lack all hearing, especially if the fortunes are peregrine <note>planets in a sign where they have no essential dignity</note> to him.

### Concerning the speech of the one born. Chapter 61.

**W**hen Mercury, being under the Sun's rays and in the aspect of the Moon, is in any of the signs lacking a voice—namely in Taurus, Scorpio, or Pisces: the one born will be mute. When Mercury is in Scorpio and in the terms and face of Mars, and the Moon beholds them in opposite aspect: the one born will be a stammerer or somewhat impeded in his speech. When Mercury, being the lord of the sixth house, is unfortunate in the ascendant: the one born will speak with difficulty. When Mercury is the lord of the 6th house and in a sign lacking a voice, and ill-fortunes behold it in opposite aspect: the one born will be mute. When Mercury is in the conjunction and term of Mars, and Mars beholds it with a bad aspect: the one born will be of an impeded tongue and sometimes mute. When the Moon, full of light, is bound to Mars from the ascendant: the one born will have a chronic sickness in the tongue. When Mercury is in the house and term of Saturn: the one born will speak with labor. When Venus is retrograde in the house of Saturn and, being in the aspect of Mars, possesses the tenth house, and Mercury is falling from her: the one born will be a stammerer or much impeded in the tongue, so that he can scarcely speak.

### Concerning those born hunchbacked. Chapter 62.

**W**hen the Moon is at the beginning or end of a sign, and Saturn beholds it: the one born will be hunchbacked. When the Moon is in the 3rd house and Saturn, descending and being in a movable sign, is elevated above her: the one born will be hunchbacked. When Saturn, descending toward the earth, and Mars, the lord of the ascendant and being in a sign of crooked ascent and little latitude, are joined: the one born will be hunchbacked, especially if Mars is in the 3rd part of that sign.

### Concerning those born leprous. Chapter 63.

**W**hen the Moon, separated from the conjunction of the Sun, has bound itself to Saturn and the fortunes do not behold it: the one born will be leprous. When the Moon is with Saturn and Mars in Aries or Taurus: the one born will be of a foul-smelling body and sometimes leprous. When Saturn, Mars, the Moon, and Venus are in Cancer, Scorpio, or Pisces: they signify <term>morphea</term> <note>a skin disease causing discolored patches</note> or cancer or an abscess eating the flesh and causing a stench. When Jupiter is retrograde, and the lord of the 6th house is peregrine and unfortunate in the aspect, house, or term: the one born will have redness in the face or a scar or an abscess. When Mars or Saturn are in the 7th house, peregrine and wild <note>feral</note>, and the fortunes of the ascendant behold Mars and Saturn: the one born will have a redness in the face declining toward leprosy. When Venus is disposed as was said of Jupiter, it portends an abscess and redness in the face. When Jupiter is unfortunate in the 6th house, it signifies a bad liver, as well as flatulence and a sickness in the face which is called <term>rosa</term> or <term>rosura</term> <note>likely rosacea</note>.

### Concerning those born having white morphea. Chapter 64.

**W**hen Venus and the Moon are in a watery sign with ill-fortunes: the one born will suffer white morphea. When the Moon is with an ill-fortune in the same sign under the Sun's rays: the one born will incur white morphea with <unclear>fante</unclear> and the falling out of hair. When the Moon is in Aries, Cancer, Scorpio, Capricorn, or Pisces and is made unfortunate by Saturn, it signifies morphea. When any of the aforesaid signs is the ascendant and the Part of Fortune is absent or fortunes are in it: morphea will invade the one born.

### Concerning those born lunatic, foolish, or one-eyed. Chapter 65.

**W**hen Mars is in the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house</note>, or the Sun beholds it in the 4th or opposite aspect: the one born will be a lunatic or of bad sense or sometimes a fool or fatuous. When Mercury is with Saturn in the ascendant and Mars beholds it in opposite aspect: the one born will be a lunatic, unless a fortune beholds them with a good aspect. When the Moon is joined to Saturn in the ascendant and unfortunate Mercury beholds them: the one born will incur foolishness, especially if the fortunes do not behold them there. When Mars is between the Sun and Moon in the same sign: the one born will be made a lunatic or one-eyed <note>original: "luscus"</note>. When Mercury or Mars is in the ascendant and Saturn is above it in the angle of the midheaven, and Mercury is in opposition to the Moon from the 7th house: the one born will be a lunatic or one-eyed. When the Moon, separated from Saturn, is joined to Mars without the aspect of a fortune: the one born will be a lunatic or of little sense. When ill-fortunes behold the ascendant and its lord with a bad aspect, and the fortunes do not radiate there: the one born will be of little sense. When the Moon, separated from the conjunction of Mars, is under the Sun's rays, and the fortunes do not behold it there with a good aspect: the one born will be a lunatic. When the lords of the triplicity of the Sun in a diurnal nativity are in opposition to the Sun, and the lords of the triplicity of the Moon in a nocturnal nativity are in opposition to the Moon: the one born will be a lunatic. When Saturn beholds the lord of the conjunction or prevention, and the Moon is diminished in light: the one born will be a lunatic, especially if the fortunes do not behold it there. When the Moon, increasing in light, is in Sagittarius or Pisces and beholds Mars with a bad aspect without the aspect of a fortune: the one born will be made a lunatic. When the Moon and Mars are in the ascendant and Saturn in the 7th house beholds them: the one born will be a lunatic, especially if a fortune does not behold the Moon. When Mercury and Saturn are wild and behold each other in opposite aspect, and the Moon in any of the angles beholds Mars, and the lord of the ascendant is in the 7th house: the one born will be a lunatic. When Mercury is separated from the lord of the ascendant, the one born will sometimes utter strange words. When the Sun, which signifies the senses, is made unfortunate by both ill-fortunes, and also in opposition to the ascendant and in the term of an ill-fortune, and its lords behold it: the one born will be melancholic or a fool. When the Moon and the lord of the ascendant are made unfortunate by Mars, and Mercury is joined with them: the one born will be a lunatic and as if a fool. When the Sun and Moon are peregrine or in the sixth house and joined in the same degree, and the ill-fortunes behold them without the aspect of the fortunes: the one born will be made a lunatic or a fool.

### Concerning those born epileptic. Chapter 66.

**W**hen the Sun and Venus are in the ascendant and Saturn beholds them from the western angle of the earth: the one born will be epileptic or diminished in sense, or of those who are called "star-struck" <note>original: "stellatici"</note>. When the Sun is in the ascendant and the ascendant is the 9th sign, and the Moon is in Pisces, and Saturn in Sagittarius, and Mars in Gemini or Virgo: it signifies falling epileptics. When the Moon in a diurnal nativity does not behold Mercury nor join the ascendant: the one born will be epileptic.

### Concerning those born paralytic. Chapter 67.

**W**hen the Moon is peregrine not far from Saturn, it signifies sickness in the nerves and members from coldness, or paralytic pain. When the Moon, diminished in light, is with Saturn in a phlegmatic sign under the Sun's rays without the aspect of a fortune: the one born will incur a paralytic sickness. When the Moon, being peregrine, is with Saturn in any angle and under the Sun's rays without the aspect of a fortune: the one born will be paralytic. When the sign of the 6th house and its lord are unfortunate from the ascendant, and Saturn beholds it without the aspect of Jupiter: the one born will incur a cold and humid sickness.

### Concerning those born with heart ailments. Chapter 68.

**W**hen the Sun, being in the 6th house, is unfortunate and the fortunes are falling from it: it signifies weakness of the heart and its sickness. When Jupiter is unfortunate in the angle of the earth, and especially by Saturn: the one born will have tightness and pain of the heart. When in a diurnal nativity Saturn, Jupiter, and Mars are in the 4th [house]...

### Concerning those born with liver ailments. Chapter 69.
**W**hen the 6th house from the ascendant is the house of Jupiter, and Jupiter is in it, or if it is afflicted by an unfavorable aspect from the malefics <note>Malefics: traditionally Saturn and Mars, planets believed to bring challenge or harm.</note> in the opposite position: the native will have pain in the liver and will easily fall into a fever. And if the Moon is the mistress of the 6th house and the malefics behold her without any aspect from the benefics <note>Benefics: traditionally Jupiter and Venus, planets believed to bring luck or healing.</note>, and if Mars is the stronger malefic aspect: it signifies pain of the liver and a chronic illness proceeding from the liver; especially if Mars is in a nocturnal term and in the 4th or 7th house. And if the Moon and the Lord of the Ascendant are afflicted by Mars: the native will have liver pain and hot illnesses, and perhaps blood will exit his body through an incision or a blow. And if the Sun, in a daytime birth, is in Scorpio: the native will suffer pain in the joints.

### Concerning those born with spleen ailments. Chapter 70.
**W**hen the Moon is the mistress of the 6th house and the malefics—especially Saturn—behold her without the aspect of any benefic, the native will have pain in the spleen. And if the Lord of the Ascendant is in its own rising and the malefics behold it: the native will incur pain in the spleen. And if the Lord of the Ascendant in a daytime birth is combust <note>Combust: a planet so close to the Sun that its power is "burned up" or hidden.</note> or hindered or in a bad place, and Mars, being in the angle of the earth <note>The 4th House, representing the lowest point of the sky.</note>, hinders it, it signifies pain of the spleen. And if Saturn, being the Lord of the Ascendant, is afflicted by its own misfortune: the native will have incurable illness and abscesses. And if the Moon, diminished in light and number, is in the house of Saturn, especially in a nocturnal birth: the native will be feverish and will have a tumor or hardness in the spleen.

### Concerning those born with lung ailments. Chapter 71.
**W**hen the Moon is afflicted beneath the earth, the native will have an abscess in the lung. And it will be worse if the Moon is in the angle of the earth: for the native will have a heavy cough from the lung and will spit up phlegm with pus. And if Venus, being the mistress of the 6th house, is favored by Saturn while in a fire sign: the native will suffer from heat with dryness and pain of the lung. And if the Moon beneath the earth is afflicted by Saturn in the 4th or opposite aspect: the native will have a suffocation of the breath.

### Concerning those born with stomach ailments. Chapter 72.
**W**hen Mars and Saturn are in the 6th or 12th house: the native will suffer in the stomach and will have a "cancer" <note>In this period, 'cancer' often referred to any spreading, eating sore or chronic ulcer.</note> leading to strange pains. And if the Sun in a daytime birth is hindered and afflicted: the native's illnesses will mostly be in the stomach. And if the Moon is weak and afflicted in the 6th house: the native will be sick with many illnesses existing in the heart or stomach.

### Concerning those born with pain in the belly and intestines. Chapter 73.
**W**hen Mars is in the 7th house and in Scorpio without the presence of Jupiter or Venus: it signifies excoriation <note>Skinning or severe irritation of the lining.</note> and pain of the intestines. And if Mars, in a daytime birth, is in the 3rd or 4th house with the Tail of the Dragon <note>The South Node of the Moon, considered a point of depletion or misfortune.</note> without a benefic aspect: it signifies ulceration of the intestines. And if Saturn and Mars are in the 6th or 12th house without a benefic aspect: the native will have pain in the intestines and ulceration that consumes, and the blood will have putrefaction. And if Saturn with the Tail of the Dragon is in the 7th house, which signifies the belly: it portends pain of the intestines with windiness, coldness, and dryness, as well as pain of the ilium and the colic passion. And if the Lord of the 6th house is in the ascendant and in a moveable sign, and the Lord of the Ascendant is likewise in a moveable sign and Saturn beholds it: it signifies a chronic illness from moisture and blood and pain of the belly. And if Saturn is peregrine <note>Peregrine: a planet in a position where it has no essential dignity or "standing," making it weak or wandering.</note> and combust in the term of Venus: the native will have illnesses in the belly. And if the sign of the 6th house is afflicted by its lord in the 6th or 7th house under the rays of the Sun: the native will incur pain and inflammation of the belly. And if the Part of Fortune or the Moon or the Lord of the Ascendant are afflicted in the 7th house: the native will suffer pain of the belly, and the pain will be according to the nature and quality of the sign of the 7th house.

### Concerning ailments of the genitals. Chapter 74.
**W**hen the Moon is separated from Saturn (who is in any of the angles) while under the rays of the Sun and diminished in light: the native will have pain of the belly and coldness of the genitals. And if the Moon, increasing in light but diminished in number, beholds Mars in a daytime birth: it portends pains of the genitals and chronic illness in them. And if Venus is peregrine in the ascendant and Saturn beholds her: it signifies pain of the genitals. And if the Moon, diminished in light, is in the 2nd house and in a moist sign, and joins Saturn who is in the opposite of his apogee: the native will have swelling or relaxation or a rupture <note>Likely referring to a hernia.</note> in the genitals. And if Venus is retrograde with Mars in the 8th house, especially in Scorpio: the native's genitals will be large. And if the Moon, Venus, and Mars are in the same place and Saturn beholds them without the aspect of Jupiter: the native will have illness in the genitals.

### Concerning the stone and gravel. Chapter 75.
**W**hen Saturn is the significator of illnesses, he being afflicted, and the Moon is bound to him by conjunction or aspect: it signifies the stone and gravel <note>Kidney or bladder stones.</note> in the kidneys or bladder, as well as pain and dryness in the genitals and the penis. And if the Lord of the part of infirmity beholds the 6th house, and the Lord of the 6th and 7th houses is afflicted: the native has pain in the kidneys and bladder, for the 6th house will suffer.

### Concerning those born with much intercourse. Chapter 76.
**W**hen Venus is in a lustful sign: the native will desire intercourse. And if Venus is in the 3rd [house], or a fortune, or peregrine in the face of Gemini: the native will be incontinent and sterile. And if Venus is in the first face of Leo and in a bad place: the native will be openly incontinent. And if Venus is in Aries and in a good aspect of Mars: the native will be incontinent. And if Venus is in Pisces and Mars beholds her from his exaltation: the native will have much intercourse and be a fornicator, and thereby will procure death for himself. And if Venus, being the mistress of the 7th house, is afflicted by Mars in a bad place: the native will be incontinent. And if Venus is in the house of the Sun and in a bad aspect of Mars: the native will be incontinent and a great fornicator. And if Venus is with the Tail of the Dragon without the aspect of Jupiter in Libra: the native will be incontinent and a sodomite.

### Concerning those born with little intercourse. Chapter 77.
**W**hen Venus and Saturn are afflicted and peregrine in the 10th house: the native will be impotent toward intercourse. And if the Moon is in the 2nd house with Saturn, and Venus being retrograde beholds it with a [7th] aspect: it portends impotence of intercourse. And if Saturn is retrograde in the 6th or 12th house and in a moist sign: it signifies an impediment of the seed <note>Sperm or reproductive fluid.</note> and coldness. And if Saturn is peregrine and retrograde in the term of Venus: it signifies an impediment of the seed and pain of the genitals. And if the Moon, separated from Venus, binds to Mercury existing in the 7th house: it signifies an impediment of intercourse, but it will not destroy it entirely.

### Concerning those born as eunuchs and hermaphrodites. Chapter 78.
**W**hen Saturn is in the angle of the earth and he and Venus, being afflicted, behold an eclipsed Sun: the native will be castrated. And if Venus and the Moon are afflicted in the 6th or 12th house and Saturn is elevated above them: the native will be castrated. And if Saturn is elevated above the Moon and Venus, and Mars beholds him with a bad aspect: the member of the native will be cut off with iron, or the native will be castrated. And if the Sun is peregrine in any of the angles and Saturn, Venus, and Mercury are with it, and Mars beholds it with a bad aspect: the native will be castrated. And if Leo is the ascendant in a daytime birth and Venus, Mars, and the Sun are in it: the native will be castrated. And if Saturn, the Sun, Venus, and Mercury behold the 7th house from the angles, and Mars is in it afflicting them: the native will be castrated. And if the masculine part is in the sign and part of a female, and with it a bright star of the nature of Mars is occidental from the Sun by 198 degrees: the native will be a hermaphrodite and will have both sexes, namely of a man and a woman.

### Concerning sterile sons and daughters. Chapter 79.
**W**hen Saturn is peregrine in the ascendant and Venus is retrograde beholding him from the western angle: the native will lack offspring. And if the Moon and Venus are in sterile signs—namely in Gemini, Leo, and Virgo—and Mars and Saturn behold them with a bad aspect: the native will not have offspring, nor will a female native. And if Saturn is peregrine and retrograde in the western angle or the midheaven, and Jupiter is afflicted and the Moon, diminished in light and number, beholds him from under the rays of the Sun: the native will lack offspring, and a female native will be sterile. And if Venus is retrograde and in the 2nd house (by which the womb of a woman is signified) and also in a sterile sign, and Saturn beholds her with a bad aspect: the native will be sterile. And if Saturn is elevated above Venus and the Moon is afflicted in the 6th or 12th house: the native [will lack] offspring...

...he will not have; the woman will not become pregnant. ¶ When Mars and Saturn are in the 6th house and in a movable sign <note>Signs like Aries, Cancer, Libra, or Capricorn, which signify change</note> without the aspect of Fortune, being retrograde, and both behold Venus with a bad aspect: the male born will be sterile and impeded in the female's place <note>likely referring to impotence or genital dysfunction</note>; and judge the same for a female born. ¶ When Venus, without any aspect of Fortune, is burned <term>combust: within 8.5 degrees of the Sun, weakening the planet's power</term> in the house of Saturn, and Mars beholds her with a bad aspect: the male born will have no offspring, and the female born will be made sterile.

### Concerning those born who are not loved by women. Chapter 80.

**W**hen Venus is unfortunate in the house of the Sun, women will abhor the male born; because he will be of bad intercourse. ¶ When Venus is in a movable sign other than her own house, being retrograde and in a bad aspect of misfortune: the male born will be abhorred by women. ¶ When the Moon is burned in the house of Venus and in a bad aspect of fortune, and Venus is a pilgrim <term>peregrine: a planet in a position where it has no essential dignity or strength</term> and retrograde, being besieged by two misfortunes: women will abhor the male born in intercourse. ¶ When Venus is unfortunate in the 7th house and the Lord of the Ascendant beholds her from the Ascendant with an opposite aspect, and the Moon is joined with Saturn in the 3rd house and in the aspect of Venus: the male born will be abhorred by women and will be reviled on account of the stench of his groin.

### Concerning the sickness of the anus. Chapter 81.

**W**hen the Dragon's Tail <note>The South Node of the Moon, often associated with loss or physical affliction</note> is in Libra: the one born will have hemorrhoids or another vice in the anus. ¶ When Saturn is a pilgrim in the 2nd house, and the Lord Mars is retrograde and beholds him with an opposite aspect, it signifies hemorrhoids. ¶ When Mars is in the place of the conjunction or prevention <note>the preceding New or Full Moon</note> of the hour of the preceding birth, the one born will suffer dry hemorrhoids, or will have a fistula in the anus and will be weak. ¶ When the Lord of the Ascendant is in the 7th or 4th house with Mars, it signifies pain of the anus and hemorrhoids with a flow of blood. ¶ When Mars is in the Ascendant, it places pain of the intestines and bloody matter flowing from the anus. ¶ When Saturn and Mars are in the Ascendant without the aspect of Fortune, and the Lord beholds them from the West: pain of the anus with windiness and hemorrhoids will invade the one born, and he will cast out blood with bloody matter from the anus. ¶ When the Lord of the Ascendant is a misfortune and in the 7th house without the aspect of Fortune: the one born will suffer pain in the lower part of the belly and the anus. ¶ When Saturn, in a daytime birth, is retrograde in the 7th house without the aspect of Fortune, and Mars beholds him with a bad aspect: the one born will suffer pain in the lower part of the anus and will cast out blood with bloody matter from the anus.

### Concerning those born of short stature. Chapter 82.

**W**hen the Moon is in the last part of the sign of the 4th house and Saturn beholds her: the one born will be of short stature. ¶ When the Moon is in a sign of crooked ascension <note>Signs that take less time to rise, such as Capricorn through Gemini</note> and is in the aspect of Saturn existing in the opposite of his height: the one born will be of short stature, having small and diminished limbs, and perhaps will lack some limb. ¶ When the Moon is under the rays of the Sun and Saturn beholds her: the one born will be of short stature.

### Concerning those born of tall and equal stature. Chapter 83.

**W**hen the Moon is fortunate in the Midheaven and the Lord of the Ascendant is in the same sign with her, it signifies beauty of stature with equality. ¶ When the Lord of the Ascendant is fortunate in the Midheaven and free from the bad aspect or conjunction of Saturn, and Mars beholds the Lord of the Ascendant: the one born will have a beautiful stature. ¶ When a sign of direct ascension is the Ascendant and its Lord is in a sign of direct ascension: it likewise signifies length of stature. And this is stronger if the Moon is not burned nor descending in her circle.

### Concerning those born of weak bodies. Chapter 84.

**W**hen the Moon, diminished in light, is separated from a misfortune and burned in an angle: the one born will be of a weak body and lazy in his affairs. ¶ When the Sun is a pilgrim in the Ascendant in some term of misfortune, and Saturn beholds him with a 3rd or opposite aspect: the one born will be weak and lazy. ¶ When the Moon is in the Ascendant and in the sign of Aries, and is bound to Saturn in a movable sign: the one born will be weak and of short life. ¶ When the Sun is falling from the Ascendant, and Mars, descending in his circle, beholds him from a succeeding angle, the Moon being in the 3rd or opposite aspect of Saturn: the one born will be weak and of short movement.

### Concerning those born bald. Chapter 85.

**W**hen Leo, Virgo, or Scorpio, or Sagittarius is the Ascendant and Mars is in them: the one born will be bald. ¶ When Cancer is the Ascendant and Mars or the Moon is in it, or if Mars beholds it with a 4th or opposite aspect: the one born will be bald. ¶ When the Part of Fortune and the Part of Faith and their Lords are in Aries: the one born will be bald.

### Concerning those born with a thin beard. Chapter 86.

**W**hen Aries, Cancer, Scorpio, or Pisces is the Ascendant and the Part of Fortune is there, and with Mars beholding the said part: the jaws of the one born will be hairless, and his beard few and thin.

### Concerning those born having foul sweat and breath. Chapter 87.

**W**hen Venus is retrograde in the house of Saturn and in his bad aspect: the one born will have foul sweat and breath, and this is stronger if Venus possesses the sign of Capricorn. ¶ When Mercury, being the Lord of the 6th house, is in a bad aspect of Saturn: the sweat of those born will be foul. ¶ When Venus, being the Lady of the 6th house, is retrograde in the house of the Moon and beholds in a bad aspect of Saturn: the one born and his armpits will stink. ¶ When Venus is burned in the house of Jupiter and Saturn beholds her with a bad aspect without the aspect of Jupiter: the one born will be entirely stinking. ¶ When Venus is in a movable sign in the term of Saturn and in his aspect: the one born will have a stench in the mouth and armpits. ¶ When the Moon is with Saturn in Aries and in the Ascendant: the one born will be of foul breath and a stranger.

### Concerning the sickness of joints and the breaking of limbs. Chapter 88.

**W**hen the Moon and the Part of Infirmity are in the 7th house and Mars beholds them with an opposite aspect: the body of the one born will break and his joints will be impeded. ¶ When the Moon is in a sign of crooked ascension burned under the rays of the Sun, and Mars is retrograde and beholds it with a bad aspect without the Moon beholding him: the one born will suffer a chronic sickness so that even some limb will be cut off. ¶ When the Moon is in the Ascendant and joined to Mars, or she being in the Ascendant as a pilgrim without a good aspect beholds Mars from an opposite aspect: some of the limbs of the one born will be truncated.

### Concerning the sicknesses of the hands and feet. Chapter 89.

**W**hen Saturn and Mars are with Jupiter in the house of Venus, she being unfortunate in the 4th house: the one born will have an impediment in the hands and feet. ¶ When misfortunes from the 6th or 12th house behold Saturn without the good aspect of Jupiter: the one born will be hunched. ¶ When Saturn and Mars, or their Zenzaar <note>original: "Zenzaar", likely a corruption of the Arabic 'Zanjir' referring to the nodes or a specific planetary chain</note> and the Sun are in the 6th house: it signifies chronic sickness in the feet. ¶ When the Part of Fortune and the Part of Assistance man or a staff. ¶ When Saturn, the Moon, and Mars are pilgrims in the 12th house and Jupiter does not behold them with a good aspect: it portends chronic sickness in the feet. ¶ When the Part of Infirmity is in a movable sign or in the aspect of misfortune and the fortunes do not behold it: the one born will have crooked legs and feet. ¶ When the Lord of the Ascendant is in the 12th house with a misfortune, or a misfortune beholds him with a 7th or opposite aspect: it signifies chronic sickness in the feet. ¶ When the Sun and Moon are in the 6th house and misfortunes behold them: the one born will suffer gout in the feet or chiragra <term>chiragra: gout of the hand</term> in the hands. ¶ When Mars, retrograde and a pilgrim, possesses the 6th house: the one born will have some impediment in the feet. ¶ When the sign of the 6th house is movable and its Lord is in a movable sign: the one born will have crooked feet. ¶ When Saturn is a pilgrim and impeded in the 6th house: the And if it is from the Ascendant up to the 4th house: it will be in youth. ¶ And if from the 4th house up to the 7th: it will be in old age and around the time of death. ¶ Know that when the significator of infirmities is in the term of the earth, you ought to divide that term according to...

## Concerning the Mastery of the Native. Chapter 90.

**K**now that there are three lords of the masteries <note>original: *magisteriorum*; refers to one's professional skill, craft, or trade</note>, namely: Mars, Venus, and Mercury. Mars, on account of his lightness, mobility, heat, joy, and strength, was made one of the lords of the masteries. And Mercury indeed, because he possesses intellect, subtlety, judgment, speech, and writing, was made a lord of the masteries along with Mars. Venus, however, because she governs the work of the hands and wondrous forms, as well as perfected things with durability and slowness, assumed the lordship of the masteries with the others. Nor, to me, can any mastery be rightly performed without the aid of the aforesaid planets. For a mastery does not exist without ingenuity and artifice; and ingenuity cannot exist without intellect; intellect cannot exist without order and thought; and order and thought cannot exist without knowledge and study. Knowledge indeed and study cannot exist without durability. The ancients, therefore, not without cause, attributed the lordships of the masteries to the three aforesaid planets.

But Saturn, Jupiter, the Sun, and the Moon have nothing in the masteries, nor do they send anything, without the participation of the three aforesaid planets. For the Sun rules over kingdoms and kings, as well as high and sublime men, and they rule with authority <note>original: *galijs*, likely referring to ceremonial helmets or symbols of office</note> and do not perform crafts; rather, they have servants and artisans under them. Saturn brings about laziness, greed, heaviness, misery, foulness, and sadness. And because of this, when a native has Saturn as a "stranger" <note>term: *peregrinus*; a planet in a position where it has no essential dignity or strength</note> in their nativity, and especially without any good aspect from the three aforementioned planets signifying mastery, or if he looks upon the said lords of mastery with a bad aspect: the native will suffer injuries and reproaches on account of their mastery, and their state will be one not of goodness, but of misery. And if any planet aids Saturn with an aspect and approval: the native will dig the earth, make streams and ditches, bury the dead, and move them from place to place. Or he will work in fields and vineyards like a servant and a wretch, receiving wages therefrom.

Jupiter is the lord of religion, opinion, and simplicity. And so, if he himself in a nativity should be a stranger or unfortunate without the aspect of any of the said lords: the native will be freed from these worldly things and will think on the things of God, leave the world, and lead a life of poverty. The Moon is the lady of weakness, wandering, speed, little intellect, and swift response, and her greatest work is to go and return. When the Moon is ill-disposed in any nativity: the native's movement will be fruitless. And if her place is significant, it signifies the carrying of wood and sacks, manual labor, and heralds; also those who sow discord among others by saying that such a person spoke ill of them. And it signifies every work in which one labors with hands and feet; and this according to the aspect and reception with the three aforesaid lords and other planets.

Furthermore, it should be known that some have masteries in speech, voice, and tongue; and some have works not of the hand, such as writers; others have works in reputations, selling, and buying; and others are lazy without a mastery. When you wish to discern the mastery of a native, if you find one of the lords of the masteries in his own house, give the native a higher mastery; and if he is strong and fortunate and in his exaltation, give him the highest mastery he can have, for the native will be famous and highly named in said mastery. And if the said lord is not retrograde: diminish the mastery by half. And if he is retrograde and a stranger: diminish the mastery by three parts. And if he is retrograde, a stranger, and "combust" <note>term: *combustus*; a planet so close to the Sun that its influence is said to be burned up or hidden</note>: diminish the entire mastery, for the native will have nothing of it but the fame and the name.

And know that the significator of the mastery signifies the work in the masteries according to the nature of the sign in which it is found. And if the said lord is not in his own house, but in a sign in which he has no dignity: join the nature of the sign of some lord with his own nature. And if the significator of the mastery is fortunate in the house of Jupiter, it signifies a good and high mastery. And if it is in the house of Saturn without the aspect of a fortune <note>term: *fortuna*; a beneficial planet like Jupiter or Venus</note>, it will signify a bad, miserable, and vile mastery. And if it is fortunate in the house of Mars, it signifies a mastery with ingenuity and artifice in which joy and gladness adhere. And if it is fortunate in the house of the Sun, it portends a mastery in the affairs of lords and princes with honor. And if it is in the house of the Moon: it will cause a strange mastery to be shown, which possesses cunning and ingenuity. And if the significator of the mastery is fortunate in the Ascendant, or by its degree before the Ascendant: the native will be fortunate in his mastery and increasing in his fortune. And if it is beyond these degrees within the 12th house: the native will be fortunate by God and decreasing in his fortune. And if it is "wild" <note>original: *feralis*; a planet acting without the influence of others</note> in the 2nd house, there is bad thought with reproach brought into the mastery of the native. And if it is in the 10th house: the native's mastery will be adorned and elevated.

And if the significator of the mastery is in its own sign free from impediments: the native will have joy and fortune in his mastery. And if it is fortunate in a strong place: the native will be hated in his mastery. And if the said significator is in its "descent" <note>term: *descensio/fall*; the sign where a planet's influence is weakest</note>, it will give the native a mastery of great labor and little profit. And if it has some dignity in that place, it will give the native a middling mastery. And if the said significator exists in the house of its descent: the native will not care for his mastery. And if by chance he looks upon it, he will perform the mastery he loves and will not remove his mind from it. And if the significators of misfortune look upon it, especially Saturn: he will give the native a mastery with pain and heavy labor. And because of this, the native will desire to leave it. And if Mars looks upon the said significator in place of Saturn: the native will impose infamy, heat, and greatness of soul upon that mastery. And if Mars is then unfortunate: the native will easily leave his mastery. And perhaps that mastery will be useless to him.

And know also that the **Fire Signs** signify fiery masteries, such as the masteries of blacksmiths, craftsmen, bakers, namely glassmakers and those who work with threads. And the **Earth Signs** signify masteries that are occupied with the earth, such as: the masteries of potters, masons, diggers, and joiners. And the **Air Signs** signify singers, jesters, bird-catchers, and the like. And the **Water Signs** signify fishermen, washerwomen, fullers, and the like.

And if the significator of the mastery is in the **Signs of Wild Beasts**: the native's mastery will be concerning wild beasts and streams, such as Lions, Bears, Monkeys, and the like. And if it is in a **Human Sign**: the native will be a physician, a barber-surgeon, and so with other masteries that concern men. And if it is in **Aries**: it signifies shepherds and butchers, bell-makers, and the like. And if Venus, being the significatrix of the mastery, is in a human sign and one of the superior planets looks upon her with a more good aspect from a human sign: the native will be a joyful singer and a player, and so with other similar things. And if an unfortunate planet looks upon her, the native's mastery will be concerning fragrant things and sweet-smelling fruits, and so forth. And if it looks upon her from an earth sign: the native will be a seller of gems or glass and the like. And if it looks upon her from a water sign with a good aspect, he will be a seller of pearls and white, shining stones, and fish found in the sea. And if it looks upon her from a fire sign in the same way: the native will be a goldsmith and so with other masteries of craftsmanship that involve fire. And if they look upon her from a **Quadruped Sign**: the native will be a seller of horses, [unclear: small rodents?], and wild asses, or mountain oxen, and so with other similar things.

And if Mars is the significator of the mastery: and any of the planets signifying masteries looks upon him from a fire sign: the native will be a smith or a caster of metals, and so forth. And if it looks upon him from an earth sign: the native will be a potter or a glassmaker: and so with other masteries that come from earth and fire. And if it looks upon him from a water sign, the native will be a bath-keeper or a tavern-keeper or a cheese-maker or a distiller of waters, and so with other masteries that come from water and fire. And if it looks upon him from an air sign and he is a stranger in it: the native will hurl stones with engines or machines, or will be an archer or a bird-catcher, and so with other masteries that are done through the air or in the air. And if he has some dignity in the said sign: the native will be a physician. And if he is descending, a stranger, and unfortunate in the said sign, it signifies barbers and [unclear: signalers?]. And if it looks upon him from the sign of a beast and he has dignity and fortune in it: the native will be a doctor of beasts <note>a veterinarian</note>. And if he is a stranger and in his "fall" in the said sign: the native will be a carrier of dead beasts outside the towns and their flayer or preparer, and so with other foul masteries.

And if Mercury, being the significator of ingenuity, is in a human sign and any of the planets signifying masteries looks upon him with a good aspect, it signifies the teaching of words and the remembrance of holy things, of beautiful men and histories, and also judges philosophy and the science of formal writing. And if in a sign...
