<meta>This page continues the astrological discourse on the "magisterium" (profession or craft) of a person based on planetary alignments at birth. It follows the previous discussion on how the elements and signs dictate one's career path.</meta>

If [Mars] is in a **fiery** sign and fortunate, he makes money-changers and minters. And if he is in an **earthy** sign, he signifies polishers of the earth, their measurement, numbering, and calculation, as well as geometry. And if he is in an **airy** sign, he portends bird-catchers. And if he is in a **watery** sign, he signifies a leader of waters and rivers. And you must look in the craft of the newborn to the part of the craft and its lordship, to the binding of the Moon with misfortune and her separation in the sign in which she is; likewise the terms of the Sun and Moon and the lords of their houses, and which planets behold the said significators. For if a fortunate planet beholds them with a good aspect, it will give the newborn strength, joy, and fortune in his craft, especially if that beholding planet be a Fortune <note>In astrology, "Fortunes" are beneficial planets like Jupiter or Venus.</note>. And if it beholds them with a bad aspect, the newborn will despise his craft, especially if that beholding planet be a Fortune; for then the craft of the newborn will be of little profit, much labor, and great sadness. When the Moon is in her house or exaltation or in any of her dignities, and binds to the Sun from the 10th house, and one of the greater [planets] beholds her with a good aspect, it signifies a craft concerning kings and the exaltation of the newborn. And if the Moon is so disposed and Mars beholds her with a good aspect, the newborn will be a leader of soldiers and an ordainer of wars, as well as a judge of fornication and robbery. And if Mercury beholds them in the place of Mars, it signifies the ordering of money and the collections of kings. And if Jupiter beholds her in the place of Mercury, the newborn will be a judge and a preserver of the king's justice. And if Saturn, being in the place of the craft-significator, beholds her, and Jupiter is joined with Saturn and has power, the newborn will be the lord of the houses of the king or of some great man. And if the binding of Saturn is through conjunction with Venus, and both are in the Ascendant or the 11th house and in a **human sign**, it signifies songs, liberality, and joy. And if Mercury beholds them, it portends the composition of musical instruments. And if Mars beholds them, it signifies oculists <note>Eye doctors or those who work with vision.</note>, carvers of images, and violent games. And if Jupiter beholds them in the place of Mars, he indicates books of law and their reading, as well as sweet songs in matters of faith and voices that sometimes indicate weeping and the lord of prayers. And if Saturn beholds them in the place of Jupiter, it signifies the lamentation of the dead and sorrow. And if the Moon binds Mercury in a **four-footed sign**, it shows teaching; and if the Moon beholds the Sun with a good aspect, it portends the charters of kings and their writings. And if she beholds Jupiter in the place of the Sun, it signifies the writing of books, simplicity, and history. And perhaps the newborn will be a writer of judgments, or an advocate, or a notary. And if she beholds Venus in the place of Jupiter, it indicates the writing of verses for women and their teaching and wisdom. And if she beholds Mars in the place of Venus, the newborn will be a horseshoer, or a driver of beasts, or a physician. And if she beholds Saturn in the place of Venus, the newborn will be a carver of necromantic images. And if the binding of the Moon is with Saturn, and Saturn is in an **earthy sign**, it portends building and the mastery of buildings. And if the Sun beholds Saturn, it signifies building from great stones. And if Jupiter beholds her in the place of the Sun, it indicates the building of churches and places in which oratories stand and the [temples] of idols. And if Venus beholds her in the place of Jupiter, it portends every building of beautiful appearance. And if Mars beholds him in the place of Venus, it shows the building of ovens, or steam baths, or furnaces. And if Mercury beholds him in the place of Mars, it signifies the building of the foundations of houses. And if Saturn is in a **watery sign**, it indicates the composition of unbaked bricks and vessels or walls made of mud. And if his binding is with Mars, and Mars is in a **fiery sign**, it signifies a mastery of fire. And if the Sun beholds him, the craft of the newborn will be around kings, or the newborn will be a maker of arms and swords. And if Jupiter beholds him in the place of the Sun, the newborn will be a master of bronze or copper or metal. And if Saturn beholds him in the place of Jupiter, the newborn will be a smith. And if Venus beholds him in the place of Saturn, the newborn will be a goldsmith. And if Mercury beholds him in the place of Venus, the newborn will be a money-changer or minter or a carver of seals. And if there is an aspect of the Sun and Moon to him, the newborn will be a worker of lead. And if the aspect is to Mercury, the newborn will be a teacher of children or a judge. And if Mars beholds him from a **human sign**, the newborn will be a physician, especially if Mars is fortunate. And if he is retrograde or fortunate, the newborn will be a barber. And if Mars beholds him from the sign of a **beast**, the newborn will be a butcher. And if he beholds him from the last part of Sagittarius, the newborn will be a doctor of beasts <note>A veterinarian.</note>. And if the aspect is of Venus, the newborn will be a <term>barifaldus</term> <note>Likely a servant, official, or perhaps a specific type of entertainer/juggler.</note>. And if the aspect is of Saturn and Saturn is in a **human sign**, the newborn will be a digger. And if he is in the sign of a **beast**, he will be a preparer of hides. And it should be known that when any planet, the lord of the significators of the craft, beholds in a receptive and agreeable aspect, it will give the newborn subtlety in the craft. And if many behold the part of the significators of the craft, the newborn will have many crafts. And if any of the said [planets] is received, the newborn will love the craft of that preceding part and cast other things aside. And if the significator of the craft is under the rays of the Sun, the newborn will hold his craft in hatred and will be lazy and idle in it, unless Mercury is the significator of the craft, because then he will make the newborn a writer of books or a master of high artistry. And these things are said generally of the crafts of the newborn; let us descend to other particulars.

### Concerning those born as weavers. Chapter 91.

When Mercury, being the significator of the craft, is descending or retrograde, the newborn will be a weaver. And if the Sun beholds him, he will be a weaver of carpets and silks. And if Venus beholds him, he will be a weaver of buckram and other things made of silk. And if Saturn beholds him, he will be a weaver of coarse cloths and fustians and felt. And if Jupiter beholds him, [a weaver] of <unclear>allissons</unclear> and fine cloths of wool. And if the Moon beholds him, he will be a weaver of mats. And if Saturn is in Gemini, he will be a weaver of linen cloths.

### Concerning those born as shoemakers. Chapter 92.

When Mercury is unfortunate in the 3rd house, as has been said, the newborn will be a shoemaker. And if Jupiter beholds him from the sign of a **beast**, the newborn will be a skinner <note>A furrier or pelt-worker.</note>. And if the Sun beholds him, the newborn will be a maker of banners and tents and silks. And if Venus beholds him, the newborn will be a maker of <unclear>cotinariū</unclear> and <unclear>lante</unclear>. And if the Moon beholds him, the newborn will be a maker of cloths of linen or silk. And if none of the planets behold him, the newborn will be a worker of silk and a shoemaker.

### Concerning those born as tanners or leather-workers. Chapter 93.

When Mars is joined to Jupiter in any of the angles of the firmament, and Venus is from them in a **fiery sign** and the other in the sign of a **beast**, the newborn will be a maker of slippers. And if Saturn beholds them, the newborn will cut and sew hides. And if Mercury mixes himself with Mars, [he will make] the trappings or ornament with which men live in the land of the Moors and carry it on their heads with the fruit of the <unclear>Sazaph</unclear> <note>Possibly a reference to a specific North African or Moorish headgear or trade good.</note>. And if Saturn mixes with Mars, the newborn will be a preparer of hides or a maker of leather instruments for fighting. And if Venus beholds Mars from the house of Saturn, the newborn will be a tanner and a worker of hides. And if the Sun joins Venus in the firmament, the newborn will be a maker of reins and thongs with which beasts are struck, and of scabbards.

### Concerning those born as carpenters. Chapter 94.

When Mars beholds Mercury from the house of the craft, and the sign of that house is from the signs of seeds and things growing from the earth, the newborn will be a carpenter or of that craft which is of wood and iron. And if Venus beholds them, the newborn will be a maker of trumpets, or flutes, or lutes and similar instruments. And if Saturn beholds them, the newborn will be a carpenter of houses and of all things which builders need regarding wood. And if the Sun beholds them, the newborn will be a carpenter of shields and other instruments which are set forth for kings and battles. And if Jupiter beholds them, the newborn will be a worker of instruments which bishops and abbots use in churches, or instruments by which stones are raised on high. And if the Moon beholds them, the newborn will be a worker of ships and bridges. When Saturn, Mars, and Venus are together in a masculine sign of Mars, and Mars is in Aries, Leo, or Sagittarius, the newborn will be a carpenter or a merchant of wood.

### Concerning those born as painters and sculptors. Chapter 95.

When the Moon in the term of Mercury beholds him from **moist signs**, the newborn will use various colors in his paintings that are pleasing to see. And if Mars beholds her, the newborn will be a carver of wood. And if Saturn beholds her, the newborn will be a carver of dark stones or gypsum. And if the Sun beholds her, the newborn will be a painter or carver in gold or gems. And if Jupiter beholds her, he will be a painter or illuminator of books of faith, or a carver of images before which all people pray and genuflect. When the Moon is in the place of Jupiter, the newborn will be a carver of ships or of vessels in which water [is kept].

And you should know that Mercury signifies painting or sculpture in houses and in every hollowed wooden thing, as well as the minting of coinage.

## Concerning those born to be dyers. Chapter 96.

**W**hen Venus and Mars are in the place of mastery: or have a confirmation of bonds or aspects in it: the native will be a dyer of greatness if they are in fiery and watery signs. And if Saturn regards them: the native will be a dyer of black cloths. And if the Sun regards them, he will be a dyer of bright cloths and of all things in which there is gold, as well as beauty and light: such as the banners of kings and their tents. And if Mercury regards them: the native will be an "Abbasienus" dyer <note>likely referring to a specific style or origin of cloth, possibly Saracen or 'Abbasid'</note> and of all green things or things colored with a medium color; these are the fustians. And if Jupiter regards them: the native will be a dyer of cloths or things of a saffron color. And if the Moon regards them: the native will be a dyer of the moon <note>likely silver or pale colors</note> with which fans are made.

## Concerning those born to be diggers. Chapter 97.

**W**hen Mars, being the significator of mastery, is unfortunate or peregrine <note>term: peregrine - a planet in a position where it has no essential dignity or strength</note> and is in an earthy sign: the native will be a digger of the earth. And if the Sun regards him: the native will dig gold and silver and precious stones from the earth. And if Jupiter regards him with a bad aspect: the native will be a digger for things made because of wars and the protection of some place. And if Saturn regards him: the native will be a digger of latrines and of foul and dark places. And if Venus is with Saturn: the native will be a digger of wells or fountains. And if Mercury is in the place of Venus: the native will be a digger of gardens or places in which water is carried. And if the Moon is in the place of Mercury, the native will be a digger of lakes or marshes.

## Concerning those born to be sailors. Chapter 98.

**W**hen Saturn or Mercury is in the middle of the sky: the native will be a sailor or a master of sailors. And if the Sun regards them, the native will be a sailor for sublime men and of ships which are made for kings and noblemen. And if Jupiter is in the place of the Sun: the native will be a sailor of ships made for the occasion of war, especially if Mars is confirmed with them. When the aspect of Mars is in the place of Jupiter: the native [makes] instruments with which sailors strike and set fire. When Venus is in the place of Mars: the native will be a master of a shouting ship <note>perhaps a coxswain or signalman</note> and one who exhorts the servants to sail. And if the Moon is in the place of Venus: the native will be a sailor of small boats or a master of sailors. When the Moon is in the west and Saturn is immobile, and [Mars] is bound on the left side: a famous sailor and sometimes a lord of the sea.

## Concerning those born to be smiths. Chapter 99.

**W**hen Mars and Saturn are in any angle, and Mars is in the middle of the sky and in a fiery sign: the native will be a smith. And if the Sun regards them, he will forge swords and breastplates and iron spears. And if Jupiter regards them: the native will be a maker of mirrors or nails or the clasps of books and of all work made of tin. And if the Moon regards them, the native will be a maker of instruments made for drawing water from wells. And if Venus is in the place of the Moon: the native will be a maker of chests and of instruments pertaining to women. And if Mercury is in the place of Venus, with him being in the last house of Mars: the native will be a maker of knives or other iron instruments which craftsmen require.

## Concerning those born to be entertainers. Chapter 100.

**W**hen the Moon and Mercury are with Mars and Venus and one lends its power to the other: the native will be a cithara player or a hurdy-gurdy player. And if the said planets are receding from the angles: the native will dance for entertainment. And if Mercury and Venus are in their own terms: the native will be a dancer, especially if one of them is in Capricorn. When Mars and Mercury are in an angle of the earth: the native will be of those who walk upon ropes. When Venus with Mercury is in her own house and in an angle: the native will be a mime or such an entertainer who, being weak in the hands, touches with the tongue. When Mercury and Venus are confirmed to each other and Venus is in the angles or in the 4th house from the lord, and Venus is in the term of another or being oriental in her house of exaltation or triplicity: the native is an entertainer of the voice, a dancer, and a clapper of hands.

## Concerning those born to be physicians and surgeons. Chapter 101.

**W**hen Mars and Venus are joined: and regard each other by some aspect: or are receding: the native will be a physician and a master of the natures of men. When Mars and Mercury are in the house of Venus or one of them: the native will be a physician and a necromancer and will deceive men with his experiments. When Mars is the significator of mastery and the Moon is bound to him from a bestial sign <note>signs representing animals, like Aries or Leo</note>: the native will be a physician. And if no one but Mars regards him: a surgeon and one who performs his cures with fire. And if Venus regards him: the native will be a cutter of veins <note>a phlebotomist</note> or a giver of medicines. And if Saturn regards him, he will give enemas and will visit hemorrhoids and other passions of the anus. And if [Jupiter] regards Mars in this, he will heal the sick through charms and the speaking of words. And if Mercury regards him: the native will be an enchanter and a physician of words or medicines made by art. And if the Sun regards him, he will be a surgeon of the eyes. And if the Moon regards him: the native will be a puller of teeth and a general surgeon. And if the master of the lord is in the middle of the sky and in a bestial sign so that one binds to the other: the native will be a surgeon of broken limbs.

## Concerning those born to be fishermen, fowlers, or hunters. Chapter 102.

**W**hen [a planet] regards the angle of the west: the native will be a hunter or a fisherman. When Mercury and the Moon in her fullness are in Taurus and Mercury is with her: the native will be a nurturer of falcons or goshawks or hawks, or will catch birds by ingenuity and cunning. When the Moon with Mercury is in a watery sign: the native will be a fisherman.

## Concerning those born to be rope-makers. Chapter 103.

**W**hen Saturn is in the house of mastery and in a common sign and any of the lords of mastery regard him: the native will be a maker of ropes. And if Mars regards him, he will make them for torturers. And if Venus regards him: the native will make ropes of hemp. And if the Sun regards him: the native will be a maker of muzzles and halters for horses. And if Jupiter regards him: the native will compose ropes for the hands. And if Mercury regards him, or if Mars alone is the significator or in a bestial sign: the native will compose ropes or muzzles or halters from the hair of animals. And if the Moon regards him: the native will make cords or ropes for whipping men or striking horses. And if the Sun regards him in the house or term of Mars: the native will be a composer of the cables or ropes which powerful men use in wars.

## Concerning those born to be sellers of fragrant spices. Chapter 104.

**W**hen [Venus] is in any dignity, the native will be a seller of fragrant spices. When Venus is the significatrix of mastery and is placed in a sign signifying mastery such as Libra, and Saturn regards her from Capricorn: the native will be a seller of musk or amber. And because Venus being in her own house or in the exaltation of Saturn signifies good and beautiful trade and of good odor. And if Venus regards Saturn from Taurus: the native will be a seller of fragrant ointments and a composer of oils such as jasmine or lily and the like. And if Venus, being the significatrix of the work, is in a human sign: the native will be a seller of spices which men use, such as ginger, cinnamon, and the like.

## Concerning those born to be apothecaries. Chapter 105.

**W**hen the ascendant is a watery sign and Jupiter and Saturn are in the 4th house: the native will be an apothecary. And if Mars and Venus are the significators of mastery and one regards the other in a quartile aspect: the native will be an apothecary. And if Mars is in the place of mastery and regards Venus being in a watery sign from a watery sign: the native will be a seller of ointments or eye-salves and the like. And if Mars, being the lord of mastery, regards Saturn from Taurus or Gemini: the native will be a seller of colors and dyes. And if the Sun is with a combust Saturn in Capricorn: the native will be a merchant of gall-nuts and barks or roots from which dye is made.

## Concerning merchants of fruits. Chapter 106.

**W**hen Saturn is in Capricorn and Jupiter regards him from Cancer: the native will be a merchant of many grains or other noble fruits. And if Jupiter regards him from Pisces: the native will be a merchant of fruits and sweet fruits. And if he regards him from Taurus: the native will be a merchant of fruits from the fields. And if Mars regards Saturn, existing in Capricorn, from Cancer: the native will be a merchant of small fruits having hard shells.

### On Merchants of Herbs, Roots, and Seeds. Chapter 107.
When Saturn is in Capricorn and the Moon regards <note>original: "aspexerit" - an astrological term meaning the planets are in a specific geometric relationship or "aspect"</note> him from a sacred sign: the person born will be a merchant of herbs. And if Mercury is in a watery sign and the Moon regards him, or binds him in a fertile sign: he will be a merchant of melons and other similar seeds. And if the Moon regards Mercury while he is in the Ascendant from the angle of the Earth <note>The Fourth House or Imum Coeli</note>: he will be a merchant of turnips and other similar roots.

### On Farmers and Husbandmen. Chapter 108.
When Mercury and Venus are in the Midheaven and in an earthy sign, and Saturn regards them from an earthy or watery sign: the person born will be a farmer. And when the significator of the profession <note>original: "signator magisterij" - the planet indicating one's career</note> binds to Saturn from a movable sign, it indicates the person will be a farmer. When Venus is the significator of the profession and Mars, falling from the Sun, strengthens her: he will be a farmer. When Saturn, as significator of the profession, brings ill fortune by his aspect and the Moon binds from a movable sign: he will be a farmer and a gardener. When Saturn is in Taurus: he will be a sower and a cultivator of lands.

### On Merchants of <term>Rethau</term> and Sweet-Smelling Things. Chapter 109.
<note>Rethau: Likely a corruption of 'reubarbarum' (rhubarb) or a specific aromatic resin/root used in medieval pharmacy.</note>
When the spouse <note>likely referring to a planetary conjunction</note> is the significator of the profession and Saturn regards her from a sacred sign or Scorpio: he will be a merchant of *Rethau* and of all sweet-smelling things. When Saturn is in Taurus and Venus regards him from Virgo: he will be a merchant of *Rethau* and sweet-smelling things. When Venus is in Virgo and Saturn regards her from a watery sign mixed with the former: he will be a merchant of *Rethau* and other similar things.

### On Merchants of Linen Cloth or Silk. Chapter 110.
When Venus is in the Ascendant or the Midheaven and in a <unclear>fertile</unclear> sign: he will be a merchant of linen cloths. When Venus is the significator of the profession and is in a fertile sign, and the other significator is in a bestial sign <note>Signs representing animals, like Aries or Leo</note>: he will be a merchant of linen and cotton <note>original: "bombicis" - silk or cotton</note>. And if Saturn is in Capricorn and Mercury regards him from Virgo: he will be a merchant of cloths mixed of linen and silk. For when Mercury is in his own house or exaltation, he signifies good and famous trade. And if Mercury regards him from Taurus: he will be a merchant of very fine new cloths. And if Saturn is in Taurus and Mercury regards him from Virgo, you shall judge the same. And if Mercury is with Saturn in Capricorn: he will be a merchant of coarse linen cloths. And if Saturn regards from Libra: he will be a merchant of coarse silk cloths. And if Saturn regards the Moon from Libra: he will be a merchant of coarse <term>mandalia</term> <note>A type of head-covering or handkerchief</note> because every linen cloth of low price is profitable. And if Saturn is in Aquarius and Mercury regards him from Libra: he will be a merchant of precious silk cloths, such as <term>samite</term> <note>A rich, heavy silk fabric, often interwoven with gold</note> and similar cloths. And if Jupiter is with Saturn: he will be a merchant of old cloths. And if Saturn is in Taurus and Jupiter regards him from Virgo: he will be a merchant of coarse and old cloths.

### On Merchants of Wool. Chapter 111.
When the significator of the profession is in a bestial sign and is the lord of the house of death: the person born will be a merchant of wool. And if Mars regards it there: he will be a merchant of skins with the wool. And if Jupiter regards it there: he will be a maker and merchant of cloths made of hair. And if Mercury, the significator of the profession, regards it and that significator is Venus: he will be a merchant of precious carpets. And if Saturn is in Aquarius and the Moon regards him from the house of Mars: he will be a merchant of wool or <term>alluscorum</term> <note>Possibly dyed or tawny-colored</note> cloths.

### On Merchants of Grain and Other Seeds. Chapter 112.
When Mercury, the significator of the profession, is in a fertile sign and Saturn regards him from a fertile sign: he will be a merchant of wheat. And if the Moon regards him from Virgo: he will be a merchant of seeds. And if Saturn is in Taurus and the significator of the profession regards him: he will be a merchant of seeds. And if Jupiter is there and the significator of the profession regards him: he will be a merchant of diverse seeds. And if he regards him from Virgo: he will be a merchant of grain. And if Mars regards Saturn from Virgo: he will be a merchant of small and moist seeds. And if Mars is with Saturn in Taurus: he will be a merchant of <term>subulium</term> <note>pointed or awl-shaped</note> and sharp seeds, such as mustard and the like. And if Saturn regards the Sun and Virgo: he will be a merchant of grain and fruits; and if the Moon is with Saturn: he will be a merchant of soft seeds. And if the Moon regards Saturn from Virgo: he will be a merchant of grain and other seeds. And if the Moon regards Saturn, Venus, and Capricorn: he will be a merchant of grain. And if she regards Venus with a good aspect: he will be a merchant of wheat.

### On Merchants of Leathers. Chapter 113.
When Saturn is in Aquarius and Mars regards him: he will be a merchant of the hides of mules and oxen. And if Jupiter receives Saturn from the house of the profession and regards him from an airy sign: he will be a merchant of <term>solearum</term>, that is, of new shoes. And if Mars is retrograde and regards Saturn from an airy sign: he will be a merchant of old shoes. And if Mars regards Saturn from a bestial sign: he will be a merchant of the skins of dogs, or badgers, or foxes. And if the Sun regards the place of Mars: he will be a merchant of <term>allumace</term> <note>alum-tanned</note> leathers. And if the aspect of the Sun to Mars is from the sign of Gemini: he will be a merchant of new shoes and badger skins. And if the Sun regards Mercury from Gemini or is with him: he will be a merchant of carpets made of leather. And if Mercury regards Saturn from Gemini: he will be a merchant of books covered in leather. And if Jupiter is in the house of the significator of the profession and in a bestial sign, and the Moon regards him from a bestial sign: he will be a merchant of the hides of rams, goats, and cows, especially if Mars is with the Sun.

### On Merchants of Servants. Chapter 114.
When Jupiter is the lord of the house of the profession and the lord of the 6th house regards him: he will be a merchant of servants. When Venus is the lady of the 6th house: he will be a merchant of famous and well-singing servants. And if Mars is in the place of Venus: he will be a merchant of Christian <insert>slaves</insert> and will sell them to Moors or other foreigners who are not of the law of Christ. And if Saturn is in the place of Mars: he will be a merchant of black servants. And if Mercury is in the place of Saturn: he will be a merchant of boys and girls. And if the Moon is in the place of Mercury: he will be a merchant of those who are not his own. And if the Sun is in the place of the Moon: he will be a merchant of captives, for the Sun does not signify servants but those who can be liberated from servitude.

### On Merchants of Four-Footed Animals. Chapter 115.
When Jupiter is in the house of the significator of the profession and the lord of the 6th house regards him from a quadruped sign: he will be a merchant of beasts that help men. And if Venus is the lady of the 6th house: he will be a merchant of camels and dromedaries. And if Mercury is the lord of the 6th house: he will be a merchant of donkeys. And if the Moon is the lady of the 6th house: he will be a merchant of cows. And if the Sun is the lord of the 6th house: he will be a merchant of horses and palfreys. And if Saturn is the lord of the 6th house: he will be a merchant of goats and <unclear>horses</unclear>. And if no planet regards Jupiter: he will be a merchant of sheep. And if the lord of the 6th is in the place of the lord of the 6th: he will be a merchant of sheep and rams. And if the lord of the 3rd or 9th house is in the place of the lord of the 6th and in a quadruped sign: he will be a merchant of rams, sheep, and cows, or a merchant of milk or cheeses. And if the lord of the 7th house binds to Jupiter or is with him or with the lord of the 4th house: he will be a merchant of dogs, cats, or wild beasts.

### On Merchants of Birds. Chapter 116.
When Jupiter is the lord of the house of the profession and Jupiter regards him from bestial signs: he will be a merchant of birds. And if the Moon is in a watery sign: he will be a merchant of birds living in or near the water. And if the Sun regards them: he will be a merchant of cranes and other high-flying birds. And if Saturn regards him: he will be a merchant of crows and all birds that eat living things and carrion. And if Mercury is the significator...

If the master of the profession <note>Latin: *magisterii*</note> is alone, or under the rays of the sun: Mars will make the person a merchant of small gold items, or of things that are like sparrows with trembling tails and threads.

## Concerning fish merchants. Chapter 117.

$\P$ When Mars is the significator of the profession and Saturn looks upon him from Cancer: the newborn will be a fish merchant.
$\P$ And if the significator of the profession is in a watery sign and Mars looks upon him from a watery sign: he will be a fish merchant. $\P$ And if Mercury is the significator of the profession and the Moon looks upon him from a watery sign: he will be a merchant of small fish. $\P$ And if Mercury is with Saturn in Pisces and the Moon looks upon him: he will be a merchant of tasteless <note>likely meaning freshwater or common</note> fish. $\P$ And if Jupiter looks upon them: he will be a merchant of large fish. $\P$ And if the Moon is in the west, diminished in light, and in the house of the significator of the profession: and Jupiter looks upon her from the sign of a beast <note>zodiac signs representing animals, like Aries or Leo</note> with a good aspect: he will be a merchant of salted fish.

## Concerning those born to be potters. Chapter 118.

$\P$ When Saturn is in Taurus: and Jupiter or the Sun looks upon him from Cancer and Mars from a fiery sign: he will be a potter. $\P$ And if Mars looks upon him from Cancer: he will be a merchant of vessels made of earth. $\P$ And if the Moon looks upon him from the opposite side: he will be a potter. $\P$ And if Jupiter looks upon him from Capricorn: he will be a maker of plaster or items baked or made from marsh-clay. $\P$ And if Mars looks upon him from Aries: he will be a lime-burner. $\P$ And if Saturn is in Aries and Venus looks upon him from Capricorn: he will be a potter of beautiful vessels. $\P$ And if Mercury looks at Saturn from Aries: he will be a digger of quicksilver <note>mercury</note>. $\P$ And if Saturn is in Aries and the parts do not recede from him, or if the lord of the profession is in a fiery sign and Mars looks upon him from that sign: he will be a digger of metals. $\P$ And if Jupiter looks upon him: he will be a potter.

## Concerning turners and those who fashion cups. Chapter 119.

$\P$ When Venus is with Saturn in Aries, the person will fashion cups and other wine vessels. $\P$ And if Venus looks upon Saturn from Leo: he will repair wine vessels. $\P$ And if Saturn is in Taurus: and Venus looks upon him from Cancer: and Mars from a fiery sign: he will make glass vessels. $\P$ And if Mercury looks upon him from Cancer: he will make engravings or writings on said vessels. $\P$ And if Venus looks upon him from a fiery sign: he will fashion many beautiful works of glass.

## Concerning merchants or preparers of pearls. Chapter 120.

$\P$ When Venus, the significatrix of the profession, is with the Sun: he will be a preparer or merchant of pearls. $\P$ And if Mars looks upon them: he will be an alchemist. $\P$ And if the lord of the profession is in a watery sign: and Mars looks upon him from a fiery sign: he will be a merchant of pearls. $\P$ And if Saturn is in a watery sign: and Venus looks upon him: he will be a merchant of small pearls. $\P$ And if Venus, the mistress of the profession, is in Gemini with Saturn: he will be a merchant or preparer of medium-sized pearls. $\P$ And if Saturn is outside the sign of Gemini: he will be a merchant of small pearls. $\P$ And if Saturn is without the aspect of any planet in a watery sign and in the house of the profession: he will be a merchant of sea-stones of low price.

## Concerning those born to be money-changers. Chapter 121.

$\P$ When the Sun is the significator of the profession: and the lord of the 2nd house <note>the house of wealth</note> is in a fiery sign and strikes from the Ascendant: he will be a money-changer. $\P$ And if the Moon is in her own house or exaltation and looks with a good aspect upon the 10th house: he will be a changer of goods. $\P$ And if Saturn is in Aries: and the Sun looks upon him from Sagittarius: he will be a preparer of gold and silver, or a goldsmith, or a minter of coins. $\P$ And if the lord of the 2nd house is with Saturn in Aries: he will be a digger of gold and silver. $\P$ And if he looks from Leo: he will extract gold and silver from other things. $\P$ And if he looks from Sagittarius: he will be a minter.

## Concerning those born to be butchers and bakers. Chapter 122.

$\P$ When Mars, the significator of the profession, is in the sign of a beast: he will be a butcher: or the master of a place where blood is shed. $\P$ And if Jupiter looks upon him from the sign of a beast: he will be a "butcher" of churchmen <note>likely a metaphor for a harsh disciplinarian or legal adversary</note>. $\P$ And if Venus looks upon him from Taurus: he will be a slaughterer of oxen or a butcher. $\P$ And if she looks upon him from Sagittarius: he will be a doctor of beasts <note>a veterinarian</note>. $\P$ And if she looks upon him from a human sign: he will be a shearer of beasts or a barber of men. $\P$ And if Mercury looks upon him: he will be an incisor of veins <note>a bloodletter</note>. $\P$ And if the Sun looks upon him with a bad aspect and is with the Head of Algol <note>a star associated with decapitation and misfortune</note>: he will be a cutter of bodies and heads. $\P$ And if the luminaries <note>Sun and Moon</note> are with the Head of Algol: and Mars looks upon them with a bad aspect: the head of the newborn shall be cut off: or he will be hanged with hands or feet cut off. $\P$ And if the significator of the profession is in the sign of a beast $\P$ And if Mars looks at Saturn from a fiery sign: he will be a bread-maker or baker. $\P$ And if he looks from a watery sign: he will make curdled things <note>cheese or jellies</note>.

## Concerning merchants of dates, little apples, and olives. Chapter 123.

$\P$ When the Sun is in the house of the significator of the profession and looks at Saturn from Taurus: he will be a merchant of dates. $\P$ When Jupiter is in the house of the master of the profession in a sterile sign and Venus looks upon him from a sterile sign: he will be a merchant of dates. $\P$ And if the Sun looks at Saturn from Virgo: he will be a merchant of little apples and olives. $\P$ And if Saturn is with Mars in Capricorn: he will be a merchant of red and small fruits such as *hoafox* <note>unclear; possibly a local name for a berry</note>. $\P$ And if he looks at Saturn from Virgo: he will be a merchant of coriander and dry seeds. $\P$ And if the Moon is with Saturn in Capricorn: he will be a merchant of eye-salves and seeds. $\P$ When Jupiter is in his exaltation: he will be a merchant of sugar: especially if he looks at the Sun and Saturn.

## Concerning merchants of bread and wine. Chapter 124.

$\P$ When Saturn is in Capricorn and Mars and Venus look upon him from a watery sign: he will be a tavern-keeper or wine merchant. $\P$ When the Sun looks at Saturn from Taurus and Venus with Mars is in the Ascendant: he will be a merchant of wine made from the juices of fruits. $\P$ And if Jupiter looks upon the Moon and Saturn being in Capricorn from the sign of a beast: or the Moon looks at Saturn being in Capricorn: he will be a merchant of bread. $\P$ And if Mars and Venus are in the western angle and the Moon looks at them from the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house</note> or from the house of the profession: he will be an innkeeper, tavern-keeper, or a pimp.

## Concerning merchants of sugar canes, arrows, spears, or wood. Chapter 125.

$\P$ When the Moon is in the house of the significator of the profession: or in a watery sign and Mercury looks upon them from a watery sign: he will be a merchant of sugar canes. $\P$ And if Mars looks upon him: he will be a merchant of spears. $\P$ And if Mars is under the rays of the Sun and Mercury is joined to him: he will be a merchant of arrows. $\P$ And if Jupiter is fixed with them: he will be a merchant of sugar canes. $\P$ And if Mars is in a watery sign and Mercury, being under the rays of the Sun, looks upon him from a sterile sign: he will be a merchant of wood. $\P$ And if Mercury looks at Saturn from Cancer or the Moon from Scorpio and Saturn is in Capricorn: he will be a merchant of wood or arrows. $\P$ And if Mercury looks upon him from Pisces: he will be a merchant of straw, or stalks, or herbs.

## Concerning those born to be messengers. Chapter 126.

$\P$ When the Sun is in the house of the profession and the Moon looks upon him: he will be a messenger or a runner. $\P$ And if the Sun looks at Mars being in his fall: he will be a <unclear>m̃edaz</unclear> on his journey or embassy. $\P$ And if he looks at Saturn: he will be a runner.

## Concerning various professions of those born. Chapter 127.

$\P$ When Mercury is in a four-footed sign, especially in Sagittarius: he will be a betrayer of some profession. $\P$ And if he looks at Jupiter in a four-footed sign: he will be a porter of burdens. $\P$ And if the Moon is with him: he will be a water-carrier. $\P$ And if the Sun looks at the Moon being in her exaltation: he will be a runner from morning and evening. $\P$ When Saturn is in Cancer: and Mars looks upon him with a good aspect: he will be a washer of clothes. $\P$ And if he is thus in Virgo: he will be a maker of plaster. $\P$ And if Saturn is in Aquarius: he will be a baker. $\P$ And if in Gemini: he will be a weaver. $\P$ And if in Taurus: he will be a sower. $\P$ When the Moon is with Mars or looks at him from opposition: he will likewise be a merchant of oil. $\P$ And if Venus is the significatrix of the profession and Jupiter looks upon her from a fertile sign: he will likewise be a merchant of oil. $\P$ And if Saturn is with Jupiter and Jupiter is unfortunate, led, or retrograde: he will be a tavern-keeper, innkeeper, or pimp. $\P$ And if the Moon is in the house of the profession and in the angle of the earth: he will be a wine-drawer. $\P$ And if Mercury is in Capricorn and Mars is above him, he will be [unspecified]. $\P$ And if Sa...

If Saturn was born in Aries, he will be a builder of houses. When Saturn regards the Moon or its twelfth-part <note>original: "duodenaria," a subdivision of a zodiac sign</note> and the Moon is in the term of Saturn or in one of the angles or succeeding houses: the native will be a destroyer of houses. And if the Tail of the Dragon <note>The South Node of the Moon, often considered a point of misfortune or decrease</note> is in the ascendant: the native will be a manager and a reciter of lands. And if the presiding planet at birth is Mercury: the master of the native will be from our wisdom <note>likely referring to the occult or astrological sciences</note> if Mercury was retrograde. But if he was retrograde: the native will be a cobbler or a repairer of old clothes.

### To find the lord of the profession. Chapter 128.

You shall recognize the presiding planet in the profession thus: look to the places of Mars, Venus, and Mercury. That planet which has lordship in the said places is called the presiding lord in the profession. And if Saturn mixes with the presiding planet, the native's profession will be base, such as preparing hides or paving streets. And if both are in earthy signs: the native will be a burier of the dead. And if Jupiter regards the presiding planet of the profession, the native will receive great profit and riches from his profession. And for the most part, the native's profession will revolve around matters of law and judgments. And if the Sun regards him with a good aspect: the native's profession will be concerning kings or their affairs. Hermes said: if the presiding planet in the profession is combust <note>too close to the Sun</note> or retrograde, or in the 6th or 12th house, and no planet regards the Moon: the native will beg for his food or seek it from door to door. Ptolemy says that one must look to the planet that is oriental of the Sun, and according to its nature and the nature of the sign in which it is, and the nature of the aspects of the planets, such will be the native's profession. Hermes says: that whenever the presiding planet in the profession possesses the sign of Scorpio: the native will be a thief; because if the Moon is in opposition to Mercury, and Mars regards them from any of the angles, the native's profession will be thievery.

### Chapter on the primary fortune of the birth. Chapter 129.

Because of this, it must be known that the fortunes of men are diverse. For the fortune of some will be complete so that throughout their whole life: they will have riches, honors, and dignities. Others truly will be mediocre in their fortune until the end of their life. Some also will be miserable through their whole life: having nothing or very little. There are moreover others who in the beginning, middle, or end will be rich or miserable. And others who from many riches come to misery and beggary. And on the contrary, there are some who from miseries and beggary are elevated to dignities, riches, and honors. And there are some men who have their fortune from faith and religion. Others from a king. Others from their knowledge. Others from trade. Others from wit, cunning, or mastery. Others from patience. Others from violence and audacity. Others from chance. There are also others who were always miserable and become fortunate in the hour of death. The ancient sages gather the fortune of the native from many places: namely from the lords of the triplicity of the Sun by day and the Moon by night. And from the Part of Fortune and its lord: as well as from the lords of its triplicity. From the Midheaven and its lord; from the ascendant and its lord; from the 11th house from the ascendant and its lord; and from the 11th house from the Part of Fortune and its lord. When therefore the aforesaid places are fortunate and the lords of the triplicity are strong and firm, and moreover the fortunes <note>benefic planets like Jupiter and Venus</note> regard the luminaries, and the Part of Fortune is in the ascendant or its firmament, the native's fortune will be good and durable until the end of his life. And if any of the Beibene stars <note>fixed stars of primary importance in medieval astrology</note> are in the ascendant or Midheaven, they signify the native's fortune and his sublimity. And it must be known that those in their dignities and existing in the angles give greater fortune than elsewhere.

### Concerning those born as kings: and their fortune. Chapter 129. <note>The chapter numbering repeats in the original text</note>

When the Sun is in the degree of the ascendant and in the house of its exaltation or its face: and Jupiter regards it from a fortunate place: the native will be a famous king, a fortunate conqueror in all things and protected by God: moreover a man of right faith and fearing God, and perhaps he will be the head of others. When Venus, the lady of the ascendant and of the Part of Fortune, is in the 5th house and in her exaltation, and is direct under the rays of the Sun and occidental: the native will be a great king, liberal: simple, delightful: humble: mild, peaceful: and he will desire to direct his joy and to do good to his subjects. When Saturn is placed in the 5th house as we have said of Venus: the native will be sad, not caring for the evil of various injuries done to him: he will also be of a coarse soul, of much malice: of little piety and unfortunate in his kingdom. When Jupiter is in the Midheaven, direct and increasing in number, received by the Sun and in fortunes falling from him: the native will be a great king and lordship <meta>catchword: su (his)</meta>

he shall have over the cities of his kingdom; and his exaltation shall be beautiful. When the Sun and Moon are in the Midheaven and fortunate in their dignities, and the fortunes regard them with a good aspect while the rays of the unfortunates <note>malefic planets like Mars and Saturn</note> do not: the native will be a king or lord of cities: in which he will be as a leader and whatever he commands shall be fulfilled, and he will be a man of good sense and good memory and well-formed in business. When the Sun and Venus are joined with Jupiter and the Moon: or regard them from a house without the aspect of the unfortunates: the native will be a great king in cities and commanding cities: and he shall live honorably and shall inherit his kingdom and leave it to his sons: and they truly shall inherit it after him. When the Moon regards the Sun with a good aspect: and Saturn is in the Midheaven with a good aspect and regards Mars: the native will be a wealthy king, strong in his kingdom and fortunate in his acts and powerful: a conqueror of his enemies. When the Part of Fortune is in a good place and appearing with its light and with the fortunes appearing and strong, and the unfortunates do not regard them: the native will be a great king or a strong leader. When the Sun is in the ascendant and in its house or exaltation and the Moon is in the descendant and both are free from the unfortunates: the native will be a great king, adorned and strong and powerful in his affairs and kingdom. When the Moon is in the Midheaven or its exaltation and Jupiter, Mars, Venus, and Mercury being direct regard it with a good aspect, and Saturn does not regard it: the native will be a great king and lord of peoples and cities and one who will fulfill his words and deeds in all things. When Venus is with Jupiter and Saturn and Venus regards the Moon from the Midheaven: the native will be a great and fortunate king and beloved by his subjects and protected by God, moreover a true and upright man and obedient to God. When the Sun is in the Midheaven and in its house or exaltation: the native will be a king and born of the lineage of kings and one who will order his business with rectitude and goodness: and he will be protected by God and secure from enemies. And if any of the Beibene stars of the nature of Jupiter and Mercury are in the ascendant or Midheaven, or the Sun and Moon are with it: the native will be an elevated and adorned king and praised for his wisdom and his son: under whose command his subjects shall prosper: and he will be of good conversation and of good response when necessary, and while he is present he will be agreeable in his words and swift in his business. And his strength being apparent: he will fear God, do good to his subjects, his liberality will be ample, of great piety, and in all his acts fortunate and aided.

### Concerning the native and their lordships and fortune in them. Chapter 130.

When Jupiter without the evil aspect of an unfortunate regards the Sun from opposition: the native will be a lord or governor of some kingdom of great fame, name, and lordship: and one who will have the power of commanding and forbidding. When the Sun, being in its house or exaltation, is in the Midheaven: the native will be great in his place and high in rank: and because of this he will pursue government and lordship in the city. When the Moon, increasing in number and light, is in the 12th house with the Head of Genzahar <note>The North Node of the Moon</note> without the aspect of Saturn or Mars, and a fortune is in the 11th house: the native will be great and noted in his province and cities, faithful in the money and affairs of the king. When the first lord of the triplicity of the Sun by day or the first lord of the triplicity of the Moon by night is from the beginning of the sign in which it is up to the middle of the same sign: and in any of the angles, the native will be of great fame and beloved and elevated by great lords, and he will have under him the power of commanding and forbidding, as well as helpers, scribes, and followers: so that banners or swords shall be carried before him and his commands shall be fulfilled by the people. When the Sun is in the western angle and in its house or exaltation: and the fortunes regard it without the rays of the unfortunates: the native will have great lordship and be praised in it and will be of good ordination. When Venus is with Jupiter, Saturn, and Mars and they regard the Moon from the Midheaven with a good aspect: the native will have command and lordship over others of his city and his command will be fulfilled by the citizens. When the Moon is in Cancer with Jupiter, being in the ascendant or Midheaven: the native will be of good fortune and good memory and will have lordship from the king of his climate and the king will trust in him. When the Sun and Moon are in the ascendant and in the house of Mars and the fortunes regard them, the native will be a lord of many places and a conqueror of his enemies: of good adornment and powerful to bring evil upon his enemies and swift to kill. When Jupiter regards the Sun with a third aspect <note>a trine</note>: the native will be an elevated lord and

### Concerning Masters of the Soldiers and their fortune. Chapter 131.
When the Moon is with Venus, and Jupiter and Mars look upon them with a trine aspect <note>an angle of 120 degrees, considered very harmonious</note>: the native will be a Master of the Soldiers <term>Magister Militum: a high-ranking military commander</term> who receives the property of others by force; he will be renowned in killing and strife, and because of this, he will hold honor and dignity among kings. When Mars is in the middle of the sky and looks upon Jupiter and the Moon: the native will be a Master of the Soldiers, fortunate in killing and a famous victor; for this reason, he will have honor from kings. When Saturn is with Jupiter and Mercury: the native will be a Master of the Soldiers, a victor over enemies, and protected by God. When Mars is existing in the Ascendant <note>the eastern horizon at birth</note> in his own house or exaltation: the native will be a head of soldiers and a master of wars, accomplishing great things. When Mars is in the angle of the earth <note>the 4th House or Imum Coeli, the lowest point of the chart</note> and Jupiter is in the middle of the sky: the native will be a great Master of the Soldiers, subtle, and a victor of many cities; he will be fortunate in strife and in killing. When Jupiter looks upon the Moon and Mars with a trine aspect: the native will be a master of horses and a head of soldiers, and honored by all, especially if Jupiter is in a masculine sign. When the Sun is in a masculine sign and looks upon the Moon and Mars with a trine aspect: the native will be famous in killing and called bold and astute; he will be an organizer of wars, always conquering, and will have what he wants from his enemies. When Jupiter is in the 5th house existing in his own face <note>a specific 10-degree subdivision of a sign</note> and is the lord of the house of life and the part of fortune, and Mars looks upon him with a good aspect in a daytime birth: the native will be great among men and of long-lasting fame. They will also carry banners or swords in battle; he will be a master of wars, accomplishing great things. But if [Mars is dominant], he will rule over men with iron. When Saturn and Mars are in the 9th house and appear from under the rays of the Sun <note>becoming visible as they move away from the Sun's glare</note>, and they look upon the Part of Fortune existing in the Ascendant: the native will be great and powerful, a head of many soldiers and a victor in many things; but he will not fear God, rather he will do those things which are forbidden by God. When Mars is in a nocturnal birth in his own house or exaltation and in the middle of the sky: the native, though he may be of lowly parentage, will be elevated by kings or powerful men; he will be wealthy and the king's head of soldiers. When any of the Beibene stars <note>original: "stellis beibeniis" - fixed stars of primary importance in medieval astrology</note> of the 2nd magnitude and of the nature of Mars and Jupiter is in the Ascendant or middle of the sky, or with the Sun or Moon: the native will be of great heart and position and of elevated rank; he will be a lord of soldiers, and he will gain villas and strong castles by command, and will be proficient in the acts of war; he will be a victor and of good memory. And if any of the Beibene stars of the 1st magnitude and of the nature of Mars and Mercury is in the aforementioned places: the native will be a lord of soldiers, placing himself in great acts of war and command; he will be proficient and fortunate in his affairs and wars; he will be the victor of his enemies. He will organize wars with deliberation and knowledge; he will have good sense and will look toward the state of his life and his final end long before death, especially if it is a nocturnal birth. But if it is a daytime birth: the native will be puffed up, furious, of little piety, and keeping evil in his heart. When the Part of Fortune is between Mars and Jupiter, and its lord is in a good and fortunate place: the native will be a Master of the Soldiers; he will have fame and a name and a share in slaughter and strength by the will of God.

### Concerning those born as Soldiers and their fortune. Chapter 132.
When Mars is in his own house or exaltation and existing in the middle of the sky, being the lord of the Ascendant, and looks upon the Moon there: the native will be a brave and famous soldier, and will have a reputation for willingly exposing himself to dangerous places. When the Moon is in the middle of the sky or the Sun is in the Ascendant: the native will be strong and swift in his business. And enough has already been said where we treated above concerning the mastery or commands of soldiers; judge therefore according to what is written there.

### Concerning those born as Wise Men and their fortune. Chapter 133.
When Mercury is with Venus in the Ascendant or middle of the sky: the native will be wise and a good writer <note>original: "dictator" - here meaning a composer of documents or a secretary</note>, by which occasion he will be elevated to status and honor; and the longer he lives, the more his honor and dignity will increase. When Jupiter is with Mercury in the middle of the sky, and the Sun looks upon them with a good aspect: the native will be a famous writer and will have dominion in the houses of kings. When the Moon is in a square aspect <note>90 degrees</note> to the Sun, and Mercury looks upon her while being free from misfortunes and the aspect of Jupiter, and Mercury is with fortune: the native will be a very great and named writer, and for this reason will be fortunate and wealthy. When Jupiter and Mercury look from the middle of the sky: the native will be a writer. When Mercury is in his own house and is joined to Jupiter existing in the middle of the sky: the native will be the king's writer and will be famous for it. When Mercury, in a nocturnal birth, is in the Ascendant and in his exaltation: the native will be a secretary of kings or princes and a wise writer, and from this will have good fame and fortune; he will gather money and so persevere to the end of his life. When Mercury is the lord of the house of substance <note>the 2nd House, relating to wealth</note> and Jupiter is with him or looks upon him with a good aspect in a strong and fortunate place: the native will be fortunate in writing and will have good from it and gain much money. When Mercury is in the 8th house and existing in his own face, and also appearing from under the rays of the Sun and falling from misfortunes: and the lord of his house looks upon him with reception <note>a supportive relationship between planets</note> in a good aspect: the native will be fortunate in writing and advocacy and a gatherer of much money. When Mercury appears rising from under the rays of the Sun, and also existing in his own house or exaltation, possesses the 9th house; and Jupiter, free from misfortunes, looks upon him with a good aspect, especially from the middle of the sky: the native will be the King's writer and wise in great secrets; fortunate, of beautiful organization, faithful, famous, of good memory, and sufficient in all things. When the first lord of the triplicity <note>a set of three signs of the same element</note> in a nocturnal birth is at the end of his sign (namely from the 25th degree to the end) and in any of the angles: the native will be a faithful writer and will have dominion and the power of commanding and forbidding.

### Concerning the Stewards of Kings and their fortune. Chapter 134.
When Jupiter is with Saturn in his own house: the native will be a steward <term>Dispensator: a manager of finances or a household</term> of kings or royal houses or of sublime wives, and will consequently obtain profit and money. When Saturn is in the 12th house and has dignity and strength in it: the native will be a steward of kings, and other stewards will render accounts to him. When Saturn, in a daytime birth, is in his own house and in the Ascendant, and Jupiter looks upon him from the middle of the sky: the native will be a steward of kings or of sublime men, and will gather many riches from it. When Venus is in the house of Jupiter and Jupiter looks upon her from the middle of the sky with a good aspect: the native will be a steward of queens or sublime ladies. When Mercury, rising in the east in his own house or exaltation, possesses the 2nd house, and Venus looks upon him with a good aspect: the native will be a steward of some powerful and noble lord or lady. When Venus, in a nocturnal birth, is in her exaltation and with Jupiter possesses the 3rd house: the native will be a steward of sublime lords. If Venus and the Moon look from the 4th house: the native will be a master of the house.

### Concerning those born who will be greater than their parents. Chapter 135.
When Saturn looks upon Jupiter from his own house with a trine aspect: the native will be greater than his parents and all those of his house. When Jupiter is in his own house and they look upon the Moon with a trine aspect from the middle of the sky: the native will be greater than his kindred and nobler than all his ancestors. When Saturn and the Sun are with Jupiter in his own house or exaltation, and they look upon the Moon and Venus from the Ascendant or middle of the sky with a good aspect: the native will have height and dominion and great fortune, and will be made more notable than his kindred. When the Sun, existing in any of his dignities, is in the Ascendant without the aspect of a misfortune, and fortune looks upon him from the middle of the sky: the native will have dominion from the king and be loved by him; he will be wealthy and notable to all his kindred. When the Sun is fortunate in the middle of the sky and the misfortunes are falling away from him: the native will be more notable than all his kindred. When Mercury is fortunate in the Ascendant without the aspect of a misfortune, and the Moon looks upon him with a good aspect: the native will have good sense and the depth of knowledge, and because of this will acquire dominion and be made nobler than his parents. When the Sun with the Moon are in the angles and are fortunate in a daytime birth: the native will be nobler than his parents. When Jupiter in any of his dignities is for—

### Chapter 12. On the House or Fortune of the Native.

¶ When Saturn, being in his own house, looks upon it with a 3rd aspect <note>A trine aspect, 120 degrees</note> with <gloss>reception</gloss> <note>A condition where one planet is in the dignity of another</note>: and Jupiter looks upon Saturn with a good aspect and with reception from an angle in like manner: the native shall be wealthy with much money and of good fame. ¶ When Venus is in Taurus: and Saturn is in Libra in a daytime birth: the native shall be wealthy, praised, with much money, and having prominent women and children. ¶ When the lord of the present fortune is fortunate and in a good place: such as in the 9th or 11th house, and looks upon the part of fortune with a good aspect: the native shall be wealthy, of a refined substance, and luxurious. ¶ When the part of fortune or its lord is in the Ascendant, oriental <note>rising before the sun</note>, and outside the sun's rays <note>visible, not combust</note> and without the aspect of misfortunes: and Jupiter, being fortunate, looks upon them from the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house or Imum Coeli</note>: the native shall be wealthy and have much money, but he shall hide it: and judge the same, namely that he will be wealthy, even granted that Jupiter does not look upon it nor its lord. ¶ When the lord of the house of substance is fortunate in the Ascendant: the native shall be wealthy and of a good life. ¶ When the part of fortune looks upon the Sun or is appearing with it from under the rays without the aspect of a fortune: the native shall be wealthy and praised in all his works. ¶ When Venus is in the triplicity of Jupiter, direct and outside the Sun's rays, and possesses any of the angles: the native shall sometimes be wealthy. ¶ When the Moon is in the triplicity of Jupiter and fortune is in the Ascendant: and Jupiter, being fortunate, possesses a good place in the figure: the native shall have many riches and shall be fortunate on all sides. ¶ When Jupiter is direct and from under the Sun's rays and free from misfortunes in the house of the Moon: the native shall gain money on a journey and shall always be wealthy. ¶ When Jupiter is direct and in any angle: and is with the Moon and free from misfortunes and combustion: the native shall be wealthy and shall live in abundance and in a good state. ¶ When Jupiter, being in any of his dignities, is fortunate in any of the angles: the native shall be wealthy, high, and famous, and his mastery shall be beautiful: and he shall live a laudable life. ¶ When the Sun is with Venus and Mercury, and Saturn and the Moon in the middle of the sky <note>the 10th house or Midheaven</note> look upon them: the native shall be wealthy and profit shall not be removed from him, and his deeds shall be beautiful. ¶ When Jupiter and Venus are both free from misfortunes: and the Moon looks upon them with a good aspect: the native shall be wealthy and shall mingle with kings and sublime men. ¶ When Jupiter is in his exaltation and in the Ascendant: and Saturn, being in Capricorn, looks upon him from the west: the native shall have money and fame. ¶ If the Sun, being in his exaltation, looks upon the Moon with a 3rd aspect and both are free from misfortunes: the native shall be wealthy and always abounding in good things, as well as leading an adorned life: especially if it be a daytime birth. ¶ When the Sun is in a masculine sign and free from misfortunes, and the fortunes look upon him from their own houses with a good aspect: the native shall be wealthy. ¶ When the Sun and Venus with the Moon are in the middle of the sky: and Venus is direct and in a good aspect of Jupiter: the native shall be wealthy, of a healthy body, and honored by sublime men. ¶ When the Sun is with Jupiter and they look upon the Moon from the middle of the sky with a good aspect: the native shall be wealthy with ample money: as well as of great lordship and fame. ¶ When the Sun and Mercury are with Jupiter in his house, or look upon Jupiter in his house or exaltation, and the misfortunes fall away from them: the native shall have much money and shall have honor and utility in the city where he dwells. ¶ When the Sun, being in any of his dignities, looks upon the Moon from the Ascendant or 4th house with a good aspect: and the misfortunes fall away from him: the native shall be famous and fortunate. ¶ When the part of money and also its lord are free from misfortunes in a good and strong place and the fortunes look upon them: the native throughout his whole life shall be rejoicing in ample money and honored. ¶ When the lord of the house of the part of money is joined to the lord of the Ascendant: the native shall gain money without labor: and it shall come to him from a place where he does not hope. ¶ And if the lord of the Ascendant is joined to the lord of the house of money: the native shall acquire money with labor. ¶ And if a planet carrying light <note>the Moon or a faster planet</note> brings light between them: the native shall have money according to the nature of that carrier. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant is separated from the lord of the house of substance: the native shall not acquire fame and shall leave his mastery. ¶ And if the lord of the house of money is separated from the lord of the Ascendant: he shall labor much to acquire money: but shall gain nothing or little.

### ¶ On the Fortune of the Native regarding Hidden Money Found in the Earth or elsewhere. Chapter 136.

¶ When Saturn, being in any of his dignities in the 4th house and fortunate in a good aspect of Jupiter: the native shall find great money hidden under the earth: especially if Saturn is slow in his motion. ¶ When Jupiter, being in the angle of the earth, and Venus looks upon him with a good aspect: and both are free from misfortunes: the native shall find great profit or buried money. ¶ When the lord of the 2nd house is in the 4th house and impels light and order to the part in the Ascendant or the 4th house: the secrets of money shall be opened to the native and he shall find buried money. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant is in the Ascendant: and Jupiter from the angle of the earth impels to the same: the native shall find buried money. ¶ When Venus, the lady of the 2nd house, is in the angle of the earth: and the Sun and Moon in elevated signs look upon them: the native shall extract silver from the earth and find buried money. ¶ When Mercury, being the lord of the 2nd house, is fortunate in the angle of the earth: the native by his own sense shall extract grave things and understand the secrets of sciences, and nothing shall be hidden from him that he wishes to think out or ordain.

### ¶ On the Fortune of the Native proceeding from the Heirs of the Dead. Chapter 137.

¶ When the Moon, full of light and increased in number <note>waxing</note>, is in the 8th house in a nighttime birth, and Jupiter, being fortunate and in any of his dignities, looks upon them from the 11th house: the native shall be wealthy and shall inherit the money of the dead. ¶ When the lord of the 8th house, being a fortune, is in the 2nd house, not combust nor retrograde: the native shall have the fame of his heirs. ¶ When the lord of the 2nd house gives his power to the lord of the eighth with reception from the term <note>an Egyptian dignity</note> and is in the house of a fortune: the native shall inherit the fame of the heirs: and judge similarly if the part of fortune is joined with any fortune in the 8th house. ¶ When the lord of the 8th house with the Sun is not combust but lord of the 8th house is a fortune, and the lord of the house of substance is joined to it: the native shall have something from the dead. ¶ When Saturn is in the 8th house in a daytime birth and the native shall have good fortune and obtain money from the dead. ¶ And if any of the misfortunes look upon it with a good aspect, it may be by his warning, and he shall have paternal inheritances and dignities: and he shall be a good heir in the place of his father. ¶ When the part of possession is in the 8th house and its strength is greater than the lord of the 2nd house: the native shall have money and inheritances. ¶ When the lord of the part of fortune is in the 8th house and the lord of the ascendant looks upon it with an aspect: the native shall live from the things of the dead.

### ¶ On the Fortune of the Native from Fields & Gardens. Chapter 138.

¶ When Venus, being in any of her dignities, is in the angle of the earth and free from misfortunes: the native shall have many fields. ¶ When Saturn is in a good place and in his own house or exaltation: and looks upon the Ascendant with a trine aspect: the native shall be wealthy from the lands of an enemy or fields. ¶ When Saturn is as has been said: and is joined to them in the Ascendant or looking upon it from a strong place: the native shall have fortune from fields and vineyards. ¶ When Saturn is direct and being in his exaltation is in the middle of the sky: the native shall be lord of fields, lands, and vineyards, and shall love populations and buildings and shall be fortunate in them. ¶ When any of the benign stars of the 2nd magnitude, and of the nature of Mars and Venus, is in the Ascendant or middle of the sky with the Sun or Moon: the native shall be wealthy and fortunate in lands or fields and shall build Houses and Palaces. ¶ And if any of the benign stars of the 1st magnitude and of the nature of Jupiter and Saturn are so situated: the native shall be wealthy and moneyed, as well as abounding in inheritances, lands, and vineyards, and the same shall obtain fame and lordship. ¶ When the Moon, separated from the Sun, is in the house of the Sun and is joined to a fortunate Saturn: the native shall have profit from lands: and shall build houses. ¶ When Saturn is in the angle of the west and in the house of Jupiter, and Jupiter is in the angle of the earth and being in Pisces is joined...
