But if Saturn should be unfortunate in the western angle <note>The 7th House, associated with partnerships and the setting sun</note>: the place where he was born will be destroyed and go to ruin. And if Mars should be unfortunate in the west and should behold the Moon with a fourth aspect <note>A square aspect, usually considered tense or difficult</note>, there will be destruction of said place by fire. And if the Sun, Mars, and the Head of Zenzahar <note>The North Node of the Moon, also known as Caput Draconis</note> should be present and misfortune beholds them, the house of the one born shall be destroyed.

# On the Fortune of the One Born from Slaves and Servants. Chapter 139.

When Mercury is in any of his dignities and in a good sign and place, as well as free from misfortunes, and the fortunes <note>Benefic planets, usually Jupiter and Venus</note> behold him with a good aspect: the one born will pursue fortune and profit from servants. When, for the sake of the servants being free from misfortunes, [a planet] is elevated in the middle of the sky above the ascendant and the fortunes behold it: the one born will have many servants and receive profit and fortune from them. When the lord of the 6th house <note>The house traditionally governing servants and small animals</note> is in any angle and fortunate, and Venus is among the fortunes: the one born will have fortune and profit from servants. When Saturn is in any of his dignities and in a good place of the figure and the fortunes behold him with a good aspect, and the lord of the ascendant raises him up: the one born will have his [fortune] through maidservants. When Jupiter is in the 6th house and in any of his dignities, and the lord of the 3rd house with Mercury is in any angle, and likewise free from misfortunes: the one born will have his [servants] and profit with them. When Mercury is in the 12th house and the fortunes behold him: the one born sees his own, and from that purchase men shall pursue profit. When Venus, being in any of her dignities, is in the middle of the sky and the Moon from the house of his own beholds her: the one born will have his [servants] and profit with them. When the lord of the 6th house is in the 7th house or under the earth and unfortunate: the servants of the one born shall die. Similarly, when the Tail of the Dragon <note>The South Node of the Moon, associated with loss</note> is in the 6th house: the one born will lose servants. When Saturn is in the 2nd, 6th, or 12th house and unfortunate: the one born will have sadness from his own. When Saturn is in the 6th house, the birth being nocturnal, and is unfortunate: the one born will incur loss by occasion of his own. When Mercury, being in any of his dignities in the ascendant or middle of the sky and in a common sign, is unfortunate: the one born will notice similar things. When the lord of the house of children is in the 6th house: the one born sees his own children become as servants or exposes them to the service of others. When the lord of the 6th house is in the 5th house: the one born will permit his own [servants] to beget other servants. When the lord of the house of children is in the 6th house and the lord of the ascendant has bound them: the one born sees or will hire out his children. When the lord of the house of his own is in the 10th house or in the 10th house from the lord of the ascendant: the one born will elevate his servants.

# On the Fortune of the One Born from Beasts. Chapter 140.

[When the lord of his] city is fortunate, especially in a sign of quadrupeds <note>Signs like Aries, Taurus, Leo, Sagittarius, or Capricorn</note>: the one born will buy beasts and have profit from them. When Saturn is in the western angle and directed in a quadruped sign and exists safe from the rays of the sun in any of his dignities, and Jupiter beholds him with a good aspect: the one born will pursue fortune from beasts. And judge the same if Saturn is in the sign of a beast and Jupiter beholds him from the sign of a beast. When Mars, the birth being nocturnal, is in the 4th house and in a quadruped sign and especially in a fiery sign, and the Sun and Jupiter, being fortunate in the 10th or 11th house, behold Mars: the one born will be a lord of four-footed beasts and fortunate from them. When Mars is fortunate in any of his dignities and in a quadruped sign, and the lord of that sign is received by Jupiter and has knocked: the one born will buy many beasts. When the lord of the 12th house, being in a quadruped sign, is joined to Jupiter or Venus: the one born will be fortunate from beasts and have profit therefrom. If Sagittarius should be the ascendant, and Jupiter is joined to Mars or is himself from a quadruped sign, and they behold Jupiter existing in Cancer: the one born will profit from beasts. When the twelfth-part <note>original: "duodeāria" - a 2.5 degree division of a sign</note> of the 7th [house], especially of the Sun, Moon, and lord of the ascendant, falls in a quadruped sign: the one born will be a rider of beasts. When the Sun and Moon [and] the ascendant for fortune—the lord of the conjunction or prevention of the birth—is in a quadruped sign: the one born will buy beasts. When Mars is the lord of the ascendant of the conjunction or prevention of the birth, and also in a good place of the figure and in any of his dignities, and the fortunes from a good place behold him with a good aspect: the one born will buy beasts for riding. When Mars is in a doubtful place from the ascendant and in a fiery sign and Jupiter beholds him with a good aspect: the one born will buy great beasts. And if it be [in Capricorn?]: the one born will have diverse beasts, such as horses, camels, sheep, and oxen. When the sign of the 12th house is watery and Saturn is a pilgrim <note>A planet with no essential dignity in its current position</note> in it: the one born will lose beasts, because they will die for him.

# On the Fortune of the One Born from Trade. Chapter 141.

When Jupiter, being in any of his dignities, possesses the 10th house and Mars beholds him with a good aspect: the one born will be a great merchant of merchandise. When Mercury is direct and fortunate in the house of Venus, and Venus beholds him from the 10th house with a good aspect: the one born will be a great merchant. When Mercury is fortunate in a good place: the one born will gain his substance from trade, writing, or accounting. When Venus is in the ascendant or in a common sign, especially in her exaltation, and free from misfortunes, retrogradation, and combustion <note>Being too close to the Sun</note>: the one born will be a merchant of good memory, acquiring much money in many ways and fortunate in trades. When Mercury, the birth being nocturnal, is in the 2nd house and in any of his dignities, oriental and free from the rays of the sun: the one born will trade with another's money and gain much and have fortune in this. When Mercury, the birth being diurnal, is in the 6th house, fortunate and in a good aspect of the whole, and the fortunes fall away from him: the one born will be fortunate in selling and buying. When Jupiter is fortunate in the middle of the sky and Mercury is oriental and free from misfortunes and possesses a house with any of the fortunes: the one born will be a fortunate merchant and of much money. When Mercury is direct in the house of the Moon and free from the rays of the sun, and the lord of the 2nd house and the Moon in Virgo behold him with a good aspect: the one born will be fortunate in trades. When Mercury is the lord of the 10th house and, being direct, is in the house of Saturn, and Saturn is direct and safe from "profusion" <note>likely a corruption of 'combustion' or 'perfusion'</note> and beholds him from a third [aspect]: the one born will be wise in trades. When the Moon is increased in number and light and free from misfortunes, and Mercury, being in a good aspect of the whole, beholds her from the 10th house with a good aspect: the one born will rejoice in trades and have fortune from them. When Mercury is direct in the 10th house and Jupiter is also direct and beholds him from Pisces: the one born will be a strong and fortunate merchant. When Jupiter, being the lord of the 10th house, is in any of his dignities and fortunate Mars beholds him with a trine aspect: the one born will be fortunate in trades from the middle of life until the end.

# On the Mediocre Fortune of the One Born. Chapter 142.

When the Part of Fortune is unfortunate and a fortune, being oriental and strong, beholds it with a good aspect and from a strong [place]: the one born will be moderately fortunate and will have profited from the fortunate [aspect]. When the lord of the triplicity of the luminaries is in the last degree of a sign which is the term of the misfortunes, being in any angle or succedent house: the one born will be of good status and life and content with a little, so that he asks nothing of anyone. But if the fortunes behold the Part of Fortune, and the lord of the Part of Fortune, being strong and fortunate, is in a good place: the life of the one born will be mediocre, so that the one born will not be greatly elevated nor yet cast down. When the three significators—namely the lord of the ascendant, the lord of the middle of the sky, and the lord of the 11th house—are fortunate, or one of them fortunate and another unfortunate: the one born will be moderately fortunate, so that he will be neither rich nor yet poor. When the lord of the Part of Fortune is unfortunate and in a bad place, and the oriental and strong fortunes existing in some of their dignities behold the Moon and the Part of Fortune: the life of the one born will be mediocre and will suffice for him. When the misfortunes behold the Part of Fortune and the fortunes fall away from it, and the lord of the house of substance is fortunate and the lord of the ascendant is fortunate in the 2nd house: he will be neither rich nor poor. When the Part of the Day in a diurnal birth and of the Night in a nocturnal birth behold each other with a good aspect, and one is fortunate and the other unfortunate: the one born will be sometimes rich and sometimes poor. When the Part of Fortune is in the term of a fortune and in a good place, and a fortune and similarly the Part [of Fortune] in the 6th house, being a pilgrim and retrograde, beholds it: the one born will be mediocre between poverty and riches. When the lord of the ascendant is fortunate and safe from retrogradation and combustion, and also in the 2nd house and in any of his dignities: the one born will not profit [greatly], but yet will acquire it with labor.

# On the Misery and Labor of the One Born. Chapter 143.

When the lord of the 2nd house is retrograde or combust or a pilgrim, and the misfortunes [behold] him with a bad aspect...

### Concerning those born to be reduced to servitude. Chapter 144.

WHEN Saturn is in its own house or exaltation, and also in any angle but under the rays of the Sun: the person born will do something because of which he will be reduced to servitude. ¶ When the Moon is in any angle or succeeding house, especially in the 7th house or its exaltation, and is joined with any malefic: the person born will come to a crime or a stain, or perhaps will be captured and made a servant. ¶ But if Jupiter and Venus look upon the Moon: the person born will escape from that danger of crime or servitude. ¶ When Saturn is with an eclipsed Sun in the angle of the earth <note>The 4th house or Imum Coeli</note>: the person born will be captured in his childhood and made a servant. ¶ When the Part of Servants in the angle of the earth is unfortunate and looks upon the Ascendant, and its lord does not: the person born will do something because of which he will become a servant. ¶ When the Moon is in the 3rd house of a person born during the day: the person born will be a servant and will perform his duties. ¶ Especially if a malefic is joined to it or looks upon it: for then the person born, in order to have his food, will serve others. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant is in the 12th house and in the aspect of a benefic, especially Saturn <note>Note: Saturn is usually a malefic, but here the text specifies its aspect</note>: the person born will be transported from his land and made a servant. ¶ When the Moon is wandering in the 6th or 12th house and the lord of the triplicity of the sign in which she is is unfortunate and in a bad location: the person born will be a servant. ¶ But if she is in any angle or succeeding house with a malefic and a malefic looks upon them: the person born will also be made a servant. ¶ When Mars is unfortunate in any angle and is in a sign following the Sun, and the Moon looks upon it from a sign in which she has no dignity: the person born will become a servant. ¶ When Saturn, in a nocturnal birth, behaves in the same way as we said of Mars: it will expose the person born on the roads and he will be made a servant. ¶ When Jupiter and Venus are unfortunate in the 12th house and Saturn and Mars are retrograde and look upon them with a bad aspect: the life of the person born will consist of servitude, misery, and wretchedness.

### Concerning those born to be imprisoned. Chapter 145.

WHEN Mars is unfortunate in the Ascendant and looks upon the Moon with a bad aspect: the person born will be imprisoned. ¶ When the Moon and Jupiter are unfortunate and joined together, and the Moon binds herself to Mars from a bad location, and Mars is the lord of the 12th house, wandering and retrograde in the 6th house: the person born will suffer heavy evils and prison. ¶ When Saturn and Mars are "wild" with the Moon in the 4th house, or being wild, one looks upon the other with a bad aspect: fear or sadness from blows and prison will happen to the person born. ¶ When Jupiter, Saturn, Mars, and the Moon are in one sign and are unfortunate in the 12th house: the person born will incur heavy evils, as well as imprisonments and losses. ¶ When the Moon, in a daytime birth, is in the house of Mars without the aspect of a benefic and is unfortunate, and Saturn is the lord of the 12th house and is "combust" <note>term: "p̃bustus/combustus" - so close to the Sun that its power is burned up or hidden</note> and looks upon the Moon with a bad aspect: the person born will have heavy evils and remain in prison for a long time. ¶ When Mars and Mercury are unfortunate in the West <note>The 7th house</note> or the angle of the earth and Mars is the lord of the 12th house, and Jupiter is with them in the same place and in his own term: the person born will endure poverty, misery, and prisons; but afterward he will escape and have good fortune. ¶ When the ruler of the person born is under the rays of the Sun, the Sun not being in Aries or Leo, but in the 10th house: kings will harm the person born and will bind his feet in shackles; and if any of the malefics look upon this: kings will kill the person born. ¶ When the Ascendant or Mercury is unfortunate and gives its strength to Saturn being in the 4th house: the person born will dwell in prison and captivity for many years. ¶ When the lord of the 9th house is combust in any angle: the person born will be captured on a journey and enter captivity in prison. ¶ When the ruler of the person born is in the 6th or 12th house: the person born will be captured by his enemies. ¶ When Venus is the lady of the 6th house and is in the 12th house, and is joined with a retrograde Mars: evils will happen to the person born, and he will be imprisoned on account of women. ¶ When Saturn, Mars, and Mercury are combust in the West, and the lord of the 12th house is unfortunate and looks upon them with a bad aspect: the person born will incur evils and sadness on account of his wife and children, and will be imprisoned because of them. ¶ When the Moon is diminished in light and number in the 8th house, and the lord of the 12th house is wandering, wild, and unfortunate, looking upon them with a bad aspect: the person born will incur deceitful blows and wounds.

### Concerning those born as beggars. Chapter 146.

WHEN the malefics are unfortunate in the angles and the benefics are receding from them and are in the West: the person born will be a beggar. ¶ And judge it so if the Part of Fortune and the Moon are unfortunate. ¶ When Saturn and Mars are retrograde with the Moon in the Midheaven <note>The 10th house</note> and no benefic looks upon them: the person born will be destitute and a beggar. ¶ When Mars is unfortunate and looks upon the Moon without any good aspect from a square <note>original: ".4." referring to a 90-degree aspect</note> or an aspect of opposition: the person born will be poor and his fortune will be with labor and misery. ¶ When the Ascendant, Sun, Moon, and Part of Fortune and the lords of their houses are not looked upon by a good aspect: the person born will live in poverty and hardship. ¶ When the lords of the Western angle and the lord of the 2nd house and the lord of the angle of the earth do not look upon the Sun in a daytime birth or the Moon in a nocturnal birth, but are falling from the angles and are unfortunate: the person born will be poor and miserable. ¶ When the Part of Fortune is in an unfortunate location and looks upon a malefic from a square or opposition, and neither the lords of said part nor the benefics look upon it, nor does said lord look upon the Sun: the person born will be a beggar. ¶ When the Sun in a daytime birth is unfortunate and falling from the angles, and the lord of the 2nd house is combust: the person born will be poor and destitute. ¶ When Mars is unfortunate in the 2nd house, as well as retrograde and without the aspect of the benefics, and the benefics are falling and "wild" with the lord of the 2nd house: the person born will always be in need. ¶ When Saturn is wandering with the Moon in any of the angles and Mars looks upon them with a bad aspect: the person born will be in need and live a shameful life. ¶ When the lord of the conjunction or prevention <note>referring to the New or Full Moon preceding the birth</note> of the birth is a malefic and unfortunate, as well as falling from an angle, especially in the 6th house: the person born will be poor. ¶ When the Part of Fortune is in the 12th house and the Moon is with it, and no malefics look upon them, and the benefics in the angles or succeeding houses are unfortunate: the person born will be poor and in need. ¶ When the Moon is with a wandering malefic and in a bad aspect of a malefic, and the benefics are falling: the person born will be poor. ¶ When the Hyleg <note>term: "hyleg" - the planet or point that acts as the "giver of life" in a chart</note> is falling and the malefics strengthen it: they signify a fall and indigence. ¶ When the lords of the angles and succeeding houses are falling: the person born will be in need. ¶ When the Moon, separated from a benefic, binds herself to Saturn being in a bad location, and the lord of the 2nd house is unfortunate and in their aspect, and the benefics are falling: the person born will be poor and hardworking. ¶ When the Moon is diminished in light and number, and being wandering, is separated from Mars and applies to Saturn through a conjunction in a bad location: the person born will be in need. ¶ When any of the malefics are in the house of substance <note>the 2nd house</note> as a wanderer, and the lord of the house of substance is hindered in a bad location and being unfortunate is strengthened by malefics, and the benefics have fallen away from it: the person born will be a beggar. ¶ When the Part of Money and its lord are falling and in the West or unfortunate, and the malefics are wild and falling: the person born will be hardworking, miserable, and destitute. ¶ When the lords of the terms of the Sun and Moon are falling and combust and without the aspect of a benefic, and the benefics and the lord of the house of substance are in bad, unfortunate locations: the person born will suffer poverty, indigence, and hardship.

### Concerning those born who are raised from poverty to riches. Chapter 147.

WHEN two malefics are in the angles and the benefics are fortunate in the succeeding houses: the person born in the beginning of his life

he will be wretched; but in the end, wealthy. When one of the lords of the **triplicity** <note>A group of three zodiac signs belonging to the same element (Fire, Earth, Air, Water), each ruled by specific planets.</note> in the 4th house is direct <note>Moving forward from the perspective of Earth, considered a positive strength.</note> and safe from conjunction <note>Likely meaning safe from being "combust" or burned up by the Sun's proximity.</note>, the native <note>The person whose birth chart is being read.</note> will be raised from evil to good at the end of life. When the **Part of Fortune** <note>A specific mathematical point in a chart representing prosperity.</note> and its lord are in good places but are unfortunate <note>Afflicted by "malefic" planets like Saturn or Mars.</note>, and others look upon them from evil places, the native will be needy at the beginning of his life, but fortunate in the end. And know that the **Part of Fortune** signifies the beginning of life, and its lord the end. When the sign of the conjunction or prevention <note>The New or Full Moon preceding the birth.</note> of the nativity is unfortunate, but its lord is in a good place with a fortunate aspect, the native will have labor in the beginning of life, but riches in the end. Judge the same when the sign in which the Moon was three days after the birth is unfortunate, but its lord is fortunate and strong. When the place of the conjunction or prevention of the nativity is unfortunate, and its lord is in a good place and the Fortunes <note>Jupiter and Venus.</note> look upon it, the native will be raised to fortune and riches after poverty and labor. When **Jupiter** is in the ascendant, the 10th, or the 11th house, and is neither retrograde nor within the rays of the Sun <note>original: "in radijs solis" — meaning too close to the Sun to be seen.</note>, the native will have fortune before the middle of his days. And if he is in the angles <note>The 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses.</note> or cadent houses <note>The 3rd, 6th, 9th, or 12th houses, usually considered weaker.</note>, he will be fortunate after the middle of his days. And if he is horizontal from the Sun—that is, in his first rising—or after the opposition of the Sun in his second station, he will have honor and riches before the middle of his years. You shall know the quantity of riches according to the strength of **Jupiter**, and this will be fortunate if **Jupiter** is the principal of the birth, or the lord of the hour, or the lord of the place of the Sun in a day birth, or the place of the Moon in a night birth. And if **Jupiter** is not the principal over the aforesaid places, the native will have few riches. When the Moon is unfortunate and between her and the Sun in the east there are 15 degrees at the beginning of her occultation <note>When the Moon is hidden by the Sun's light.</note>, and her lord is fortunate and in a fortunate place, the native will be raised from misfortune to fortune. When **Jupiter** is in the angle of the earth <note>The 4th house, the lowest point of the chart.</note>, being unfortunate but in one of his dignities <note>Signs where the planet has specific power, like its Home or Exaltation.</note>, and an misfortune rules in the ascendant but with an aspect of a Fortune, the native will be raised from misfortune to fortune. When **Saturn** is in his own term <note>A specific subdivision of a zodiac sign.</note> and **Venus** in her own house looks upon the Moon with a good aspect, the native will be raised from misfortune to fortune by occasion of women. When the first lord of the triplicity of the Sun in a day birth, and of the Moon in a night birth, is unfortunate in good places, and the Fortunes look upon him with a good aspect and with reception <note>A favorable astrological relationship where planets "host" one another.</note>, the native will be raised from indigence to fortune.

#### On those born who come from riches to poverty.

#### Chapter 148.

When the lords of the triplicity of the Sun in a day birth and the Moon in a night birth are in good places, and the Misfortunes <note>Saturn and Mars.</note> afflict them without reception, the native will come from riches to poverty. When the lord of the ascendant is an misfortune and in the 2nd house <note>The house of personal wealth.</note> and in any of his dignities, and the Fortunes do not look upon him with a good aspect and reception, the native will dissipate his wealth by his own will. And if the said Fortune is not in any of his dignities, the native's substance will be stolen secretly, or robbers meeting him will openly snatch it away. When both Misfortunes are in the 2nd house without the aspect of the Fortunes, the native will descend from riches to indigence. When the Moon is joined to **Saturn** in any of the angles, the native will come to indigence. And if the native is a king or prince, his mandate will not be fulfilled and his people will ruin him. When the **Part of Fortune** and its lord are falling from the Sun, the native will become unfortunate after fortune. When the **Part of Fortune** and its lord are strong in good places but are unfortunate, and the Misfortunes afflict them, the native will descend from riches to misfortune. When the lord of the prevention of the birth and the lord of his house are in evil places and in their dignities, the beginning of life will be better than the end. When an misfortune is in the 2nd house and looks upon the lord of the 2nd house with an evil aspect, or is joined with him in an evil place, it portends loss, fall, and misfortunes to the native according to the quality of that sign: for if that sign is of the nature of **Saturn**, his loss will come by occasion of his own people and the wretched. And if of the nature of **Jupiter**, by occasion of great men or ecclesiastics. And if of the nature of **Mars**, by occasion of soldiers, thieves, or wars. And if of the nature of the **Sun**, by occasion of parents, grandfathers, or inheritance. And if of the nature of **Venus**, by occasion of noble women. And if of the nature of **Mercury**, by occasion of wise men, scribes, or merchandise. And if of the nature of the **Moon**, by occasion of infirm women, messengers, or couriers. When you find one of the misfortunes in the middle of the sky <note>The 10th house, representing career and public standing.</note> without dignity, and another at the end of a sign in which it has no dignity or strength, and with this it is in an evil place, and the lord of the house of substance is adverse; and if **Jupiter** and **Venus** are in an evil place, you will not doubt to judge misfortune for the native, as well as the loss of his things and the removal of fortune, unless God preserves him.

#### Chapter on the 3rd House: or on the Matter of Brothers.

The ancient wise men look at the matter of brothers from many places: namely, from the 3rd house and its lord; from the lords of its triplicity; from the **Part of Brothers** and its lord; from the **Part of Fortune** and its lord; and also from **Saturn, Jupiter, Mars, the Sun, Venus, Mercury, and the Moon**; and any planet being in the 3rd house. For by the 3rd house and its lord we shall know whether the native will have many brothers, or whether he will be alone; the **Part of Fortune** and its lord indicate the fortune of the brothers and their elevation or depression from misfortune. The **Sun** and **Saturn** signify elder brothers; **Jupiter** and **Mars** middle ones; **Mercury** small ones; the **Moon** elder sisters; and **Venus** younger ones. When you wish to know whether the native is the firstborn or not: when the lord of the triplicity of the ascendant is in the ascendant, the native was the firstborn. And if he is in the middle of the sky, he will be first or second. And if he is in the 6th, he will be first or seventh. And if you find any of the planets between the ascendant and the middle of the sky, the native will be the first. When the lord of the triplicity of the ascendant in a day birth is in the ascendant, the native will be the first of the brothers who will be healthy, because those who were before him will die. When the **Sun** is in the ascendant, the native will be the first and will see a diminution and fewness of brothers. When the lord of the house of brothers is in the ascendant, the native will be alone without brothers. And if he is in a sign of many children and a Fortune looks upon him, the native will have many brothers and sisters. When the **Head of Zezaher** <note>Likely a corruption of "Capa Draconis" or the North Node of the Moon.</note> is in the 4th house, and **Jupiter, the Sun, and Venus** are with the **Moon** or look upon it, the native will have many brothers and will rejoice with them. When the **Part of Brothers** is strong and fortunate in any of the angles, the native will have brothers of great power. When the lord of the **Part of Brothers** is in a sign of many children, the native will have many brothers. And if he is strong in the earth <note>In the lower hemisphere of the chart.</note>, the native will have brothers older than himself. When **Jupiter, Venus, and Mercury** are in a good and strong place and in feminine signs, he will have sisters. When the lord of the house of brothers is in a good place and oriental <note>Rising before the Sun.</note>, and a Fortune looks upon him, the native will have brothers. And if the lord of the triplicity of **Mars** is in a sign of many children, the native will have many brothers. When the **Moon** is the lady of the 3rd house, the native will have many sisters. When the lord of the 3rd house and the lord of the **Part of Brothers** are in the ascendant or middle of the sky, the native will have brothers. And if they are in sterile signs, the native will lack brothers. And if one part of them is in signs of water and the other in sterile signs, a mediocre number of brothers is signified; this must be judged according to the proportion of the planets. When the **Part of Brothers** is in a masculine sign and **Mars** is in the ascendant, the native will have brothers without sisters; and if it is the opposite, you shall judge the opposite. And if one part is in a masculine sign and the other in a feminine, the native will have brothers and sisters. When **Venus** is in the 3rd house and in a feminine sign, the native will have sisters older than himself. And judge the same when the **Moon** is separated from **Mars**. And if **Venus** is as I have said of the **Moon**, the native will have sisters older than himself. When the planets that give testimony to the brothers and sisters are in a good and strong place and ascending in their circle, it indicates an addition and multitude of brothers and sisters.

#### On the fewness of brothers.

#### Chapter 149.

When the first lord of the triplicity of the ascendant is in the 3rd house, the native will lack brothers, and if he has any, they will die before him. When **Saturn, Mars, and the Sun** are in dry signs and in cadent places, they signify the death of brothers. When **Mars** and the lord of the 3rd house and the lords of the **Part of Brothers** are under the rays of the Sun, all brothers born before him will die. And if all three are not in this manner, one part of the brothers will live and another will die; especially if he is unfortunate in that place. When the **Part of Brothers** is joined with **Saturn**, or in the 4th house or in aspect opposite to it, and the Fortunes do not look upon it, it signifies the death of the native's brothers. When the **Part of Misfortune** afflicts the **Part of Brothers**, there will be death among the sisters. When **Venus**, the lady of the 3rd house, is unfortunate, the sisters born will die. And if the **Moon** is in the place of **Venus**, the older sisters will die. When **Saturn** or the **Sun** is unfortunate, the older brothers of the native will die. And if **Jupiter** is unfortunate, the middle brothers will die. And if some are unfortunate and others not, one part of the brothers will die and another will be saved. When the misfortunes between the ascendant and the 4th house are unfortunate, the native will lack brothers; nor will a son be born to the mother unless dead. When the lord of the triplicity of the ascendant is under the earth and the misfortunes afflict him, the brothers of the native will die. And judge the same if an misfortune is in the ascendant and the middle of the sky. When **Venus** with **Saturn** is under the earth, the sisters of the native will die. When the **Part of Brothers** is in the house of **Mars** and the misfortunes look upon it with an evil aspect, the brothers born will die. And if the Sun or...

**Saturn** <note>original: "Saturnus"</note>, if he is the lord of that part, will signify death, etc. And if **Jupiter** or **Mars** is the lord of that part, or if that part is in their houses: the middle-born brothers will die. **When** the Moon, having separated from Saturn, joins with Jupiter: the elder-born brothers will die. And if **Venus** is in the place of the Moon: the younger ones will die. **When** the lord of the house of brothers is oriental <note>appearing in the east before sunrise</note> in the 1st or 8th house in aspect to a fortune <note>a "fortune" refers to a lucky planet like Jupiter or Venus</note>: the native will inherit the brothers' property. **When** the lord of the part of brothers is in his own dignity and in the 2nd house, and its lord is strong while the lord of the ascendant is unfortunate: the native's brothers will inherit from him.

---

### On the Fortune of the Native with Brothers. Chapter 150.

**When** the lord of the 3rd house <note>the 3rd house traditionally governs siblings</note>, appearing from under the sunbeams, is in a good place and in any of the dignities of the Moon, and the Moon is in the same sign, and the misfortunes <note>unlucky planets like Saturn or Mars</note> are separated from the lord of the 3rd house and also from the Moon: the native will have profit from his brothers, and they from him, as if they were one; and one will have good honor from the other. **When** the part of many testimonies is in the sign of the brothers and beholds the native's <term>prolegomena</term> <note>preliminary astrological factors</note> with a good aspect, it signifies the arrival of brothers and affection between them. **When** the lord of the 3rd house is in a good place and in any of his dignities, the native will have joy from his brothers and they will love him. **When** the part of fortune is in the 3rd house and the lord of the said part of fortune is in a good place, they portend affection of the brothers for the native. **When** the part of brothers is in the 3rd, 9th, or 11th house and the fortunes behold them, it indicates kindness among the brothers and the profit of one with the other. **When** the lord of the part of his brothers strikes the power of the lord of the ascendant, the native's brothers will honor him, prefer him, and be in obedience and under his protection. **When** the part of brothers is fortunate in the house of children, the native will have brothers younger than himself and they will be under his protection. **When** the part of brothers is fortunate in the 2nd house, the life of the brothers will come from the native and he will be generous to them from his goods. **When** the lord of the house of substance strikes the power of the lord of the house of brothers, the native's brothers will have money by his occasion; especially if the said lord of substance is a fortunate fortune and receives the lord of the house of brothers. **When** Mars is fortunate in his own house or exaltation and beholds the part of fortune with a good aspect: the native will have profit from the side of his brothers.

---

### On the Enmity of Brothers. Chapter 151.

**When** the lord of the house of brothers is the lord of the 9th, a pilgrim <note>a planet in a sign where it has no essential dignity</note> and unfortunate: the native's brothers will be enemies to him and he to them, and they will do evil to him and he to them. **When** Saturn is a pilgrim in the 3rd house and Mars beholds it with a bad aspect, the brothers and sisters will be in enmity and the native will be an enemy to them. **When** Jupiter is retrograde in the 3rd house with the Dragon's Tail <note>the South Node of the Moon, considered unlucky</note> and is with Saturn: the native will have evil by occasion of the brothers. **When** the part of brothers is in the 7th house with a misfortune, the native's brothers will plot against him and strive to destroy him. **When** Saturn, Mars, the Sun, and the Moon behold each other with a bad aspect, the native's brothers will kill one another. **When** Taurus or the Moon is in the 3rd house and Mars beholds it with a bad aspect, and the lord of the 3rd house is unfortunate and in a bad place, damages will happen to the native through his brothers who will seek to kill him, unless he is protected by a fortune. **When** the lord of the 3rd house is unfortunate and a pilgrim in the 6th house, and there is a misfortune in the 3rd house: the native will receive evils and damages from his brothers. **When** the lord of the 3rd house is in the Ascendant or beholds the degree of the ascendant with a good aspect, there will be perfect friendship between the native and his brothers; and if he beholds it with a bad aspect, there will be hatred and anger between them. **When** Mars, being a pilgrim, beholds the degree of the ascendant or its lord with a bad aspect: the native will fight with his brothers. **When** Cancer or Capricorn is ascending, the native will litigate with his brothers; and if Aries or Libra is ascending: the native will be the occasion of his own death and they will renew lawsuits for themselves in a foreign land or with men who are guests. **And if** Taurus or Scorpio is ascending: the native will have a dispute with women and will pursue his own from the lands; but many of his friends will die. **And if** Gemini or Sagittarius is ascending, the native's brothers will destroy his property and the children of his sons will be his enemies.

---

### On the Misfortune and Fall of Brothers. Chapter 152.

**When** the lord of the house of brothers is a misfortune and is retrograde and in a bad place, and the part of brothers is in the 2nd house unfortunate under the sunbeams: the native's brothers will be unfortunate and some of them will be imprisoned or suffer a chronic illness. **And** if the lord of the part is in the 6th house, made unfortunate by Saturn: the native's brothers will be servants. **When** the part of brothers is unfortunate in the house of travel and its lord is also unfortunate, it signifies the foreignness of the native's brothers and separation from him. **When** the lord of the ascendant and the lord of the part of brothers do not behold each other, and the lord of the part of brothers does not radiate upon the part itself: the native's brothers will be separated and not joined. **When** the part of fortune is joined with a misfortune in the 3rd house, or beholds a misfortune with a bad aspect, it portends the labor of the native's brothers and their fall, labor, and misery. **When** in the matter of brothers there are many testimonies of misfortune and in a bad place: the native's brothers will be hardworking/laborious. **And** if you wish to know the number of brothers, look to the lord of the triplicity of the ascendant; and if you find him above the earth, calculate from him to the ascendant, and however many [degrees] are between them, such will be the number of brothers; and for each sign, count two brothers. **And** if the said lord of the triplicity is below the earth, calculate from the ascendant to the sign in which he is. **And** what comes out will be the number of brothers. **When** if the sign of brothers is common <note>mutable signs: Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, Pisces</note> and its lord is in a common sign: **The native** will have brothers who will not be from one father and one mother, but will be from his father only or from his mother only. **Look** also to the lords of the triplicity of the house of brothers if they are with the part of fortune and the lord of the said part is in a good place. **For** then the native's brothers will have good fame, elevation, and high status. **Know** however that the first lord of the triplicity signifies the elder brothers, the 2nd the middle, and the 3rd the younger. **Speak** therefore of the state of the brothers according to the victory of the lords, their strength or weakness, and the fortunes and misfortunes. **Abucate** <note>likely a reference to Al-Baqillani or another Arabic astrologer</note> **said**: **When** the lord of the house of brothers falls in the 5th house of fathers, and the lord of that place is a pleasing male and in a masculine sign, it signifies brothers on the father's side. **And** if that planet is Venus or in a feminine sign, it indicates sisters on the father's side.

---

### Chapter 11. On the 4th House.

**The state** of the father is known from 8 significators: namely from the 4th house and its lord on the father's side, and its lord, and Saturn, and the Sun, and the planet existing in the 4th house; also from the place of the conjunction or prevention preceding the birth. **But** the state of the mother from the 10th house and from the Ascendant and its lord on the mother's side, and its lord from the Moon and Venus, and the planet existing in the 10th house; also from the place of the conjunction or prevention preceding the birth. **When** therefore the planet's position and signs concerning the parents' affairs are in a strong place, fortunate and received, indicate good and fortune to the parents from that birth. **And** if it is different from this, you shall judge the contrary.

---

### On the Progression of the Native from the Mother's Womb, whether it was Quick or Slow. Chapter 153.

**When** the Moon is in an angle and in a feminine sign: the progression <note>birth/delivery</note> of that native will be swift and perhaps on the road or in a bath or in a high place outside the house. **When** they find the Moon with the aspect or conjunction of fortunes, or when the Sun or Moon is in a masculine sign in direct descent in the birth of a male, or in a feminine sign in the birth of a female, they signify swiftness of progression with health and directness. **When** the Moon is with Mars or in his good aspect and Jupiter beholds them with a good aspect, it portends swiftness of progression with the native's escape and safety. **When** Mercury is in the Ascendant and in the house of Mars, and Mars is fortunate in a good place, judge the same. **When** the Moon, having escaped from the sunbeams, is in the ascendant or the Midheaven, you shall also judge the same. **When** Venus and the Moon in the birth of females are in a feminine sign, they indicate the beauty of the mother and the daughter with swiftness of progression. **When** Jupiter and the Moon are in a good place in the birth of a male: they signify swiftness of progression with health, escape, and the joy of the mother with the child. **When** misfortunes are in the 12th house, they signify a slow progression with the mother's heaviness; for the mother will be infirm and suffer pain in the womb; also from that progression she will have evil, sadness, and labor. **When** the Moon is separated from Saturn: it portends to the mother heaviness of progression and difficulty; also from that pregnancy, sadness and labor. **When** the Sun, Moon, and the lord of the ascendant are in a feminine sign and all the ascensions in a masculine birth, or in a feminine sign in a female birth, it signifies heaviness, sadness, prolongation, or labor in the progression of the son or daughter. **When** if Saturn is stationary in any of the angles, the woman perhaps will not give birth until she reaches death. **When** a planet is stationary or retrograde in the ascendant, it signifies slowness of progression and heaviness in the birth.

It transitions from general birth conditions to specific chapters on the parents' social standing, downfall, and relationship with the child.</meta>

When the Moon has gone toward the point <note>original: "punctionem," likely referring to a syzygy or specific degree</note> and the distance between her and the point is less than 12 degrees, or if the Moon is "void of course" <note>term: void of course - when the Moon makes no further major aspects before leaving its current sign</note>, the mother will be delayed in childbirth and will have heaviness and labor in it. When the Sun, Moon, and the Ascendant <note>original: "Alẽus," likely a corruption of Almuten or Ascensio</note> are in Scorpio, Capricorn, or Aquarius, the mother will be burdened in childbirth and will have pain and labor in it. When the Moon is in a sign of slow ascent and the malefics <note>term: infortunae - the "unlucky" planets, usually Saturn and Mars</note> behold it with a bad aspect, the delivery will be heavy, slow, and laborious, and perhaps even fatal.

### On the Exaltation of the Parents. Chapter 154.

When the Lord of the Father <note>note: the planet ruling the Lot of the Father</note> among the parents is in an angle <note>term: angle - the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses, the strongest positions</note> or a succedent house, and also in its own house or exaltation, it signifies the exaltation of the father and his honor and the elevation of his status. When the Lot of the Mother <note>original: "pars matris"</note> is situated in the same way I described for the Lot of the Father, it signifies the elevation and honor of the mother. When Jupiter is in the 4th house, being in his own house or exaltation, the parents of the native <note>term: native - the person whose birth chart is being read</note> will be famous, wealthy, and will hold some dignity or honor. When the Lord of the Father is found fortunate in any angle and strong and free from the aspect of a malefic, the parents of the native will be famous and high-ranking among their kindred. When the Lord of the 4th house is in an angle, the parents of the native will be of great status. When the Lot of the Father is in the 3rd, 9th, or 11th house, the parents of the native will be of great status; the parents of the native will be famous and praised in their city. When the Sun is in its own house or exaltation and in a good place in the birth chart, if it is a daytime birth, the father of the native will be of high name and great voice in his city. And if Saturn is so situated in a nighttime birth, it will indicate the same. When the Father <note>note: the Sun or the Lot of the Father</note> and the significator of the father are in the Midheaven <note>original: "medio celi"</note> and in their own houses or exaltations, and the benefics <note>term: fortunae - the "lucky" planets, Jupiter and Venus</note> behold them free from malefics, the parents of the native will be a great lord. When the aforementioned Lot, the degree of the point, or the point preceding the birth are similarly beheld by a good and strong aspect, the parents of the native will be of high name and good. When the Sun beholds the "angle of the earth" <note>note: the 4th house cusp</note> as well as Saturn and Jupiter with reception and a good aspect, the parents of the native are of noble birth and have lordship. When the Moon and Venus behold the 4th house, there will be fame and praise for the mother. When Venus is in the Ascendant and in a fortunate sign, the mother of the native will be more noble than the father.

### On the Fall or Depression of the Parents. Chapter 155.

When the Sun is a "peregrine" <note>term: peregrine - a planet in a sign where it has no essential dignity, like a wandering stranger</note> in the 12th house, and the Lord of that house and the 4th house are in bad places, and similarly the malefics are in bad places, the parents of the native will be brought low. When the planet having the greatest testimony in the signification of the parents is peregrine in the 12th house, the parents of the native will be of small status and their gain will be from wages. When the Moon is diminished in light and number in Scorpio and in the 4th house, and the Lord of the 4th house is in a bad place, "feral" <note>term: feral - a planet that makes no aspects to others, like a wild beast</note> and retrograde, and also lacking the aspect of a benefic, the mother of the native will be of small status and poverty. When the Lord of the house of the Sun, or the Lord of the Lot of the Father, or the "twelfth-part" <note>term: duodenaria - a 2.5 degree division of a sign</note> of the Sun, or the Lord of the Triplicity of the Sun is in the 6th house and in aspect to a malefic, the parents of the native will be greedy, of bad roots, and falling. When the twelfth-part of the Moon is in the 6th house with a malefic or in a bad aspect to a benefic, the parents of the native will be lowly and unworthy. When the Lord of the Father is in a shameful place without the aspect of a benefic and is peregrine, the parents of the native will be laborious and of little or no achievement. When the Lord of the house of the Sun with the Lord of its triplicity are in cadent places <note>term: cadent - the 3rd, 6th, 9th, and 12th houses, considered weak</note> and in bad aspect to a benefic, the parents of the native will be lowly. When a malefic afflicts the degree of the point or the point preceding the birth, the father of the native will fall from his status. When the sign of the point or conjunction is afflicted, and both malefics are in the angle of the earth or behold that angle with a bad aspect without the radiation of benefics, the parents of the native will be from bad people and procreated in servitude. When the Lord of the house of the Sun, or the Lord of the point or conjunction of the birth, or the Lord of the Lot of the Father is afflicted, it portends the fall or depression of the native's father. When the Lord of the house of the Moon and the Lord of the house of the point or conjunction, or the Lord of the house of the Lot of the Mother is afflicted, it signifies the death and fall of the mother. When the Sun is "feral" with the Tail of the Dragon <note>term: Tail of the Dragon - the South Node of the Moon, considered a point of decrease or loss</note> in a bad sign, the parents of the native will be falling or slaves. When the Sun and Moon in the point or conjunction of the birth are joined to malefics, and the benefics are cadent from the places of their joys, the parents of the native will not be of the same status, nor of the same village or city.

### On the friendship and enmity of the native with the parents. Chap. 156.

If it is shown that the Moon is in the term of Saturn in a nighttime birth, and both malefics behold it with a bad aspect without the aspect of benefics, there will be enmity and hatred between the parents of the native and the native himself. When the Lot of Fortune and the Lot of the Mother are with a malefic, and also with the Lord of the 4th house without reception <note>term: reception - when a planet is in a sign ruled by another planet that aspects it, indicating cooperation</note>, the parents of the native will be hostile to the native, and he will have heaviness and labor from them. When the Lord of the Ascendant is peregrine and beholds the Lord of the 4th house and the Sun in a daytime birth (or Saturn in a nighttime birth), as well as the Lot of the Father in opposite aspect without reception, the native will be contrary to the parents. When the Sun is peregrine in the 4th house and Mars beholds it with an opposite aspect without reception, and similarly Saturn radiates there with an unfortunate aspect to the 4th house, the parents of the native will be hostile to the native. When the Sun and the Lords of its triplicity are in a bad place in a daytime birth, and the malefics afflict them without reception by conjunction or bad aspect, there will be enmity between the native and the parents. When the Sun, being feral in the 4th house, is bodily joined to the planet having many testimonies of the father in the native's birth, and the Lord of the 4th house is peregrine and afflicted by a bad aspect of a malefic in the 8th house, the parents will bury the native alive or will deny that he is their child. When the planet that is the Almuten <note>term: Almuten - the planet with the most dignity over a certain point</note> of the parents is separated from the sign of the native by point or aspect, the heart of the parents will be separated from the native and they will hold him in hatred.

### On the Fortune of the Native with the Parents. Chapter 157.

When the Lord of the Lordship of the Ascendant is in Leo and in the 2nd house, direct <note>term: direct - moving forward in the zodiac, as opposed to retrograde</note> and safe from combustion <note>term: combustion - being too close to the Sun, which "burns up" a planet's power</note>, and also in good aspect to the Lord of the 4th house, the native will be fortunate from the father's money. And if the Ascendant-ruler in the house of the Moon is so situated, the native will be fortunate from the mother's money. When the Sun is in the house of Jupiter and in the angle of the earth, joined with Jupiter by a minute degree, and the Lord of the 4th house beholds them with reception and a good aspect, the native will have money from the parents and will be fortunate from it. When Saturn is in his own house or exaltation and in the Midheaven, and the Lord of the 4th house beholds him from the angle of the earth with reception, the native will be fortunate from the father and will have money from him. When the Lord of the 4th house knocks from the 8th house <note>note: the 8th house rules inheritance</note>, the native will inherit the parents' money. When the Lord of the house of money gives its power to the Lord of the 4th house, the parents will inherit the native's money.

### On the Evils dissipating the parents' money. Chapter 158.

When the twelfth-part of the Sun falls in the 12th house and the malefics behold it with a bad aspect, the native will consume the parents' money and make them sad. When Saturn is in the twelfth-part of the house with the Lord of the 4th house and is peregrine, the native will dissipate the parents' money. When the malefics are in the 4th house and its Lord is afflicted with the Lord of the 5th house, the native will lose the parents' money. When the Sun and Mars are joined together and feral, and the Moon is diminished in light and number, and similarly Saturn joins in a bad place and Mars beholds with a bad radiation without the aspect of a benefic, and the Lord of the house of substance is afflicted in a bad place, the money will be diminished from the native.

### On the Fortune of the Native's Parents. Chapter 159.

When the Lord of the 4th house is fortunate in its own house or exaltation in a good place, and the Lord of the house of substance is in its own dignity and in the 4th house, and the Lot of the Father and its Lord are fortunate, the parents of the native will be fortunate; they will also see good and increase in their affairs. When the Sun is in the 4th house, in its term and in the face of a benefic in a daytime birth, and in good aspect to Jupiter, it indicates the goodness of the life of the native's parents. When the Sun is bodily joined with Jupiter in the house of Jupiter, and the Lord of the 4th house beholds them with a good aspect, it portends a good status and fortune for the native's parents. When Saturn is in his own house or exaltation in the Midheaven, and Jupiter is the Lord of the 4th house and beholds it with a good aspect, the parents of the native will be fortunate and rich. When the Lot of the Father is in good aspect to the Lord of the house of substance, and both it and the said Lot are fortunate and free from malefics, it indicates direction, safety, and fortune for the native's parents. When the Lord of the house of the Sun is in its own house or exaltation with the Lord of the Lot of the Father, being in some angle and fortunate, the parents of the native will have dignity or lordship. When Saturn, being in a daytime birth, is received by the Lord of the house of substance, and the Sun, Lord of the 4th house, beholds him from Aries or Leo with a trine aspect, the parents of the native will find good, profit, and fortune. When the Moon is joined to Venus in Taurus and possesses the Midheaven, and Mars in Scorpio beholds her from the angle of the earth, the parents of the native will consequently obtain fortune and joy from the native.

### On the labor and hindrance of the native's parents. Chapter 160.

<summary>This page details astrological conditions determining the social status, wealth, and health of a native's parents, as well as the quality of the relationship between the child and their mother and father. It covers both "exaltation" (success) and "fall" (poverty or disgrace) based on planetary aspects and house placements.</summary>
<keywords>astrology, parents, inheritance, social status, malefic planets, benefic planets, 4th house, Lot of the Father, Lot of the Mother, medieval science</keywords>

--- Page 42 ---
<meta>This page continues the astrological analysis of a birth chart (nativitas), specifically focusing on the fourth house, which governs parents, inheritance, and the end of life. It transitions from general family fortunes to specific predictions regarding the death of parents.</meta>

**W**hen the Lord of the 4th house is in a fallen house, combust <note>original: "combustus"; a planet so close to the Sun that its influence is weakened or "burned up"</note>, or retrograde: the native <note>the person whose birth chart is being read</note> shall not purchase lands. When a misfortune <note>a malefic planet like Saturn or Mars</note> is in the 7th house, is "feral" <note>wild or without dignity</note>, and regards the Sun or Moon with a bad aspect, and the Part of Fathers <note>a calculated mathematical point in the chart</note> and its Lord are in a bad place, and the Fortunes <note>benefic planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> are retrograde or wandering in falling houses, the parents of the native will suffer evil and hindrance on his account. When the Sun is in the Western angle, peregrine <note>having no essential dignity</note>, and in a bad aspect to a misfortune, and the Lord of the 4th house is combust, and the Lord of the Part of Fathers is unfortunate, the parents of the native shall incur evil, labor, and downfall. When the Sun and the Lord of its triplicity are in the 6th or 12th house, unfortunate and in aspect to a misfortune: chronic sickness and evils shall happen to the native. When the Moon is joined to Saturn, the native's mother will be weak. Certain others have said that when Mars and Saturn are in the 4th house, and said house is not the home of any Fortune, nor is any Fortune within it, the native shall be dragged from his grave.

### Chapter 151.
### On the Death of the Native's Parents.

**W**hen the Sun, Saturn, Mars, and Mercury are in the 6th house: the native's father will die quickly. When the Moon is situated in the same way as was said of the Sun: the native's mother will die quite soon after his birth. When the Lord of Enemies is in the 8th house <note>the house of death</note> and the misfortunes regard him with a bad aspect, the native's father shall live but a short time. When Mars is elevated above the Sun: the native's father shall die and be killed by the sword. When the Sun and the lords of its triplicity are unfortunate: the native's father shall be killed. When Mars is in the house of the Sun, the native's father will leave the world by a sudden death: especially if it be a day birth; if it be a night birth, he shall live but a little while. When Saturn is in opposition to the Sun, the native's father shall die a bad death. When the misfortunes regard the place of the conjunction or prevention <note>the New or Full Moon</note> preceding the birth without the radiation of a Fortune, they portend a bad death for the parents of the native; but the preceding conjunction specifically concerns the father, while the prevention concerns the mother. When Saturn and Mars are in the house of the fathers and regard the Lord of said house: the native's father shall die of a stroke <note>original: "apate," likely apoplexy</note> or another sickness bringing swift death. When the Lord of the Part of Fathers is in a bad place, and the Ascendant and the place of said Part regard it, and with this the said Lord is unfortunate: the native's father shall die in a place where he is not known. When the Lord of the Part of Fathers is in a bad place and in a bad aspect of the misfortunes, and similarly the Lady of Life <note>Venus</note> is at the boundary of the Part, it indicates death and loss of life for the father. When the Lord of the house of the father binds himself to the Lord of the 8th house in the 4th house, and both are unfortunate, and the Sun and Moon are in falling and bad places, it signifies the death of the parents. When the Moon and Saturn are joined in the same degree, and Mars regards them: the mother shall die in childbirth. When the Lord of the Part of Fathers is in the Midheaven and the Lord of the Part of Mothers is in the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house</note>: the mother shall die before the father. When the Sun and Saturn are joined in the same degree, the father shall die before the mother. When Mars is in the 4th sign from the Sun and Saturn is on the left side, the father shall die first. This you can also know through the *athazir* <note>a method of planetary progression or "distribution"</note> of the two figures to the conjunction or opposite aspect of the misfortunes: for whichever of them first binds to the misfortunes, that one shall die before the other. 

In the 23rd year, a certain man came to me having a leaf on which the birth of a certain native was written, and he asked me to judge something from that birth. Then I asked him whose birth it was; he replied that it was his son's. I took that leaf and placed it before me, considering the figure of the birth and the locations of the planets. Then Al-Andarzaghar <note>original: "Alambes," a corruption of the Persian astrologer's name</note>, seeing this, took the leaf from my hand and looked at it, and said that the son for whom this figure was made was procreated from adultery. And I asked how he knew this; he replied: "This old man believes that the native is his son; but it is now four years since the year the boy was born, and the father is dead." I asked again how he knew this; he replied: "In this figure I looked to the Part of Fathers and found it in opposition to Mars, and I found only one degree between them. And similarly I looked to the Lord of said Part and found him in the 11th house from the Ascendant, which is the 8th house from the 4th house, and between him and Saturn there was only one degree." And he said that the father of this native died in the year in which the boy was born. And that old man said: "You have spoken the truth that I am not the father of this native; for I took him and raised him, and his father died in the year named by you." Al-Qabisi <note>original: "Alkubit"</note> said that he saw Al-Tabari looking at the sign of the 4th house and its Lord, and the Part of Fathers and its Lord, as well as the Sun and Saturn, and taking the *almuten* <note>the planet with the most dignity/power</note> over these places; and similarly he looked to the Parts existing in the 4th house and made it the significator of the father. Afterward he looked at the significator so that through it he might know the length or shortness of the native's life, as I explained in the beginning of this treatise. Then from the part of the fathers, he looked at the Sun and the degree of the house of the father to see which of them was more fit to be the *hylegz* <note>the "giver of life" or primary vital planet</note>; then he made the *athazir* of the parents' life to the places of the Fortunes and judged what he saw. And similarly, to know the state of the parents, he made the *athazir* every year from the part of the father to the places of the Fortunes and misfortunes according to their quality and their places, and judged the state of the father for that year. And similarly in a day birth he made the "leadings" <note>original: "ductoria"</note> of the Sun and planets, and in a night birth the leadings of Saturn and the Moon. And when the native has a leading, his father will be great and shall have honor, riches, and good. And he shall also judge the state by the first and second lords of the triplicity of the Sun in a day birth; and in a night birth by the first and second lords of the triplicity of Saturn. 

Furthermore, he asserted that the native was born of adultery because his father doubted if he were his son. When the Sun in a day birth applies to Mars by conjunction, or the 4th or opposite aspect, or in a night birth from Saturn. Similarly, when in a day birth he found the Moon hindered by Mars, and in a night birth by Saturn: he judged that the native's mother was not honest, or at least she was doubted. But certain of the wise have said that when the Lord of the house of the father does not regard his own house, and similarly the Sun does not regard its house, and the Lord of the Part of Fathers does not regard its part: the father of the native shall be a "putative" father, because the father will believe him to be his son and he will not be.

### Chapter 152.
### On the State of the Mother.

**L**ook to the Moon and Venus, the Midheaven and its Lord, and also the planet which shall be the *almuten* over these places, and set that as the significator of life. Then look to the *athazir* of that part and see what it signifies in increase or decrease according to what I said in the years of the native. Then look to the state of the mother from the leading part of the triplicity of the Moon, and according to its quality, judge the state of the mother. Furthermore, look to the Part of Mothers and the Moon, and whichever of them shall be the *hylegz* of life, and perform the *athazir* with it to the Fortunes and misfortunes. For whichever misfortune binds there shall hinder the native's mother. And if the Moon or the Part of Mothers is not the *hylegz* and you wish to know the place from which to perform the *athazir* for the mother, do it from the degree of the Moon. And when it comes to a misfortune, a delay <note>or "fall"</note> happens to the mother. Similarly, you shall perform the *athazir* for the mother from the Midheaven to the misfortunes, giving one year to each sign, and when the misfortunes bind, judge as you shall see. And if you judge the haste of the death of the mother or father according to Dorotheus <note>original: "Dorochium"</note>, the luminary that is under the earth will judge; afterward you can weigh the life of the mother and her state in day and night births from the lords of the triplicity of the place of the Moon. For the first lord of the triplicity holds the beginning of life, the second the middle, and the last the end of life's term. Then you may inspect the heaviness or lightness of the mother's death from Venus and its Lord, from the Moon and its Lord, and from the Part of Mothers and its Lord. For when these are hindered by a misfortune, they have a bad death. 

Ptolemy, however, said that in the state of the father we ought first to look in a day birth to the Sun and its Lord, and the Midheaven and its Lord. And in a night birth to Saturn and its Lord, then to the Sun and its Lord, and afterward to the 4th house, and seek the *almuten* over these places: for that will be the significator of the father's life. Having found it, perform the *athazir* by looking at the Sun in a day birth if it be in the Ascendant or Midheaven, or in the 11th, 9th, or 2nd house, and if any of the planets regard it which have some dignity in that place. And if the Sun be not in any of the aforesaid places, look to Saturn by running through the said places, and if any planet having dignity there regards him, then perform the *athazir* from him to the rays of the Fortunes and misfortunes. And if Saturn be not in the said places, perform the *athazir* from the degree of the Midheaven, and afterward divide that which it holds in its years until it comes from the Midheaven to that in which the *alcocoden* <note>the planet that determines the length of life</note> comes. And when it is equal, the father shall die in that year. In a night birth, however, you shall begin from Saturn if he be in any of these seven places: namely in the 7th, 11th, 10th, 9th, 5th, 4th, or 3rd house. And if he be not in any of these seven places and the Sun be in the Ascendant, 3rd, 4th, 5th, or 7th, from the degree of the Ascendant to the same degree existing under the earth, then perform the *athazir* from the Sun. And if the Sun be not in any of the said places, then perform the *athazir* from the degree of the house, provided a planet having dignity there regards it or not. Afterward see what comes out of the years of the *athazir*, and if it be equal to or near the years of the *alcocoden*, the father shall die in that year. Dorotheus, however, said that in a day birth we ought to look to the Sun, and in the night—

It cites classical and Arabic authorities like Ptolemy, Dorotheus, and Al-Qabisi to explain complex calculations involving planetary "leadings" and mathematical parts.</summary>
