--- Page 43 ---
<meta>This page continues a technical discussion on astrological techniques for determining the lifespan of parents and the number of children a person will have. It relies heavily on the "Hyleg" and "Alcocoden" systems, which were standard in medieval and Renaissance astrology.</meta>

<page-num>81</page-num>

For as to Saturn and in both to the Part of the Father <note>The "Part of the Father" is a calculated mathematical point in a birth chart used to determine matters related to one's father.</note> and its lord. When the Sun is in a daytime birth, being in any of the angles <note>The 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses, considered the strongest positions.</note> or succedent houses, and has any of the aforementioned dignities in the place of the Sun—especially the house, exaltation, triplicity, or term—make it the **Hyleg** <term>Hyleg: The "giver of life," a planet or point used to determine the length of life.</term> and the planet aspecting it the **Alcocoden** <term>Alcocoden: The "giver of years," which determines the specific duration of life based on its strength.</term>. And if the Sun is not fit to be the Hyleg, look to Saturn; and if you find it in any of the angles or succedent houses, and any of the aforementioned having some dignity in its place aspects Saturn, it will be the Hyleg, and the planet aspecting it will be the Alcocoden. Then you should perform the **Athazir** <term>Athazir: A method of "directing" or moving a planet through the zodiac over time to predict events.</term> from that place of the killing point. And if it happens that Saturn is cadent <note>In a weak house (3rd, 6th, 9th, or 12th).</note>, take the Part of the Father; if it is in any angle or succedent house and some planet having dignity in its place aspects it, that said Part will be the Hyleg, and the planet aspecting it the Alcocoden. Therefore, perform the Athazir from it to the fortunes or misfortunes. And if the said Part is not fit to be the Hyleg, take the cusp of the 4th house. And it should be known that in a night birth, we ought to begin the Part from Saturn, then from the Sun, then from the Part of the Father, and finally from the cusp of the 4th house. For the mother, however, in a nocturnal birth, we must first begin from Venus, then from the Moon, then from Venus, then from the Part of the Mother, and finally from the cusp of the 10th house. However, Ptolemy, in both day and night births, begins from Venus and its lord, then from the Moon and its lord, and takes the **Almuten** <term>Almuten: The planet with the most essential dignity over a specific degree or house.</term> of the said places and sets it as the Alcocoden. And in a daytime birth, he performs the Athazir from Venus to the places of the killing point, and in a daytime birth from the Moon. If the Moon is the Hyleg, it signifies the life of the father and the life of the newborn, such that sometimes it happens that the father and the child die in the same hour. But if the newborn has a "bad life"—that is, if the Sun is the Hyleg and is weak and impeded—it will give its "minor years"; unless in the birth of the father it was strong, for then it brings its "middle years." And if it is retrograde and in an angle, free from misfortunes, and the retrogradation and opposition of the Sun draws out its minor years, it will give them. And if it is retrograde in a succedent house, it will diminish a third part of its minor years. And if it is retrograde in a cadent house, it will diminish two-thirds of them. And if it is retrograde and combust <note>Too close to the Sun, which "burns" its power.</note> or unfortunate, it will convert its minor years into months. And if it is retrograde, combust, unfortunate, and in its fall, the father will die at the hour of the birth. When Ubelides <note>Likely a reference to a specific Arabic or Hellenistic astrologer.</note> gave a general and abbreviated judgment, he said: Always look at the lord of the 4th house and the lord of the Part of the Father and its lord, Saturn, and the Sun; and find the Almuten over these places, for according to its strength and weakness will be the life of the father. For the mother, look at the 10th house and its lord, the Part of Mothers and its lord, the Moon and Venus, and take the Almuten over these places, and judge the life of the mother from its state. Al-Anduz <note>original: "Alabes"</note> said that he found the Almuten according to what Ubelides said by giving the lord of the house 5 dignities, the lord of exaltation 4, the lord of triplicity 3, the lord of the term 2, and the lord of the face 1; and the planet which has the most dignities in the said places will be the Almuten.

### Chapter on the 5th House.
It should be known that births sometimes return to the births of the fathers. And that the significance which the son had in the birth is firmer than his own birth which signifies the son. If there were misfortunes in the father's birth, the son will be laborious and his life likewise; not because it signifies that they are many, but because there was a sign in the father's birth. And if there is fortune in the births of any of the sons, it will elevate their fathers and he will be the greatest among them. That is to say, the births of parents return to the births of those of their faith. And the births of those of their faith return to that species which has greater power in the species of the newborn. And the wise look into the matters of children from the matters of the mother, namely from the 5th house and its lord; from the Part of Children and its lord; from Jupiter and the lords of its triplicity; from Mercury and Venus, as well as from any planet existing in the house of children. When, therefore, you wish to know the manner of the newborn regarding children, look at the planet that has power in the said places and which joins their testimonies. And if you find it linked with the lord of the Ascendant, or with a planet in the Ascendant having a great relationship, tell the newborn that he will have children; and their manner will be according to the strength, weakness, fall, choice, fortune, and misfortune of that part. When Jupiter, Venus, and Mercury are healthy and strong in the birth, the newborn will have many children and will see their multitude and riches. When Saturn looks at Jupiter with a trine aspect, the newborn will have children. When Saturn is direct in the Midheaven and Jupiter looks at it without an aspect of misfortunes, the newborn will have many children. When the first of the three lords of the triplicity is strong and free from combustion and misfortunes, the newborn will have children and will rejoice with them. When the Moon and Mercury are in the Midheaven, the newborn will have children as well as good and joy with them. And if the Moon and Mercury are in the 5th house, and their lord is strong and free from all impediment and misfortunes, the newborn will have many children. When Jupiter is in the Ascendant and Mars is in the West <note>The 7th house.</note>, the newborn will have children and will live with them in honor and good. When in the Midheaven there is a sign of many children and a planet in it has the significator over these, such as Jupiter, Venus, and Mercury, the newborn will have many children. When Jupiter is in the Ascendant and Mercury is in the West, the newborn will have many good and just children and joy with them. When the Part of Children is in any of the angles or in a good place from the Ascendant, the newborn will have many children. When the sign of the 5th house is fortunate and its lord is in an angle and a fortune aspects them, the newborn will have many children. When Mercury is in the Ascendant and the Moon is in the West and fortunes aspect them and misfortunes are cadent, the newborn will have children and will rejoice with them. When Mars is in a nocturnal birth in the Midheaven and fortunes aspect it, the newborn will see a multitude of children and will have joy with them. When the significators of children or the planet that joins testimonies is in the Ascendant or Midheaven or in the house of trust, the newborn will have children in his youth. And if they are in the 1st, 2nd, or 7th house, the newborn will have children in old age or near the end of life. When the planet of children is in a masculine sign, it will signify sons; in feminine signs, daughters.

### On Children Born to Parents, or Having Few Children. Chapter 163.
When Jupiter or the Sun is in the 7th house, the newborn will be without children or will have few. When the lord of the house of children is in a sterile sign or one of few offspring, the newborn will lack children or have few; especially if that lord is impeded or unfortunate. When Venus is with Saturn in the division of children, it follows that there will be many. When Jupiter does not aspect Saturn with any aspect and the other is a misfortune, the newborn will have few offspring. When a misfortune is in the 5th house and its lord is impeded by combustion or descent or conjunction or a bad aspect of a misfortune, the newborn will lack children or have few. When the Moon is in Aries or Libra, the newborn will lack children. When the Part of Children is unfortunate, the newborn will have few offspring or none. When Saturn aspects Venus with an opposite aspect, the newborn will lack children and his fame will be buried. When Jupiter, Venus, and Mercury are in bad places and misfortunes have lordship over them, the newborn will have few children or they will die and he will incur sadness and impediment. When the lord of the house of children is in the 3rd sign from an angle and misfortunes aspect it, the newborn will have few children and if he has any, they will die. When Saturn is in the Ascendant and Venus is in the West and Jupiter does not aspect Saturn, the newborn will have few offspring or none. When the lords of the triplicity of Jupiter are combust in the 3rd sign from the angles, the newborn will be without children. When the Part of Children is in the 6th or 12th house, the newborn will lack children. When Saturn is with the Part of Children, the newborn will have few or no children.

### On the Number of Children. Chapter 164.
Dorotheus <note>original: "Iridorochius"</note> in the 5th book: Look at the lords of the triplicity of Jupiter and if they are in one sign, count from them to the Ascendant and the number of signs existing between them will be the number of children. And other wise men look at the first lord of the triplicity of Jupiter from the stronger and more fortunate and existing in a better place; count how many signs are between it and the Ascendant, and the newborn will have that many children. And if there is any common sign <note>Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, or Pisces.</note> between those signs, give them two children. Al-Anduz said: Count from the place of the lord of the Part of Children to the said Part; and however many signs are between them, the newborn will have that many children. And if so, look at the planet that aspects the house of children and according to the number of signs existing between them will be the number of children, giving two children for each common sign if they are there. And whichever of the lords of children aspects from a masculine sign denotes sons; from a feminine sign, daughters.

### On the Hour or Time in which the Newborn will have Children. Chapter 165.
When the lord or the revolution reaches Jupiter or the Part of Children and the Ascendant or the house of children or any of the angles, or the linking of the Part of Children by its rays with Jupiter, or the linking of Jupiter with the Part of Children to the house, or to the Ascendant or the twelfth-part of Jupiter...

<meta>This page continues the astrological analysis of a person's life based on their birth chart, specifically focusing on the "House of Children" (the 5th House) and moving into the 6th and 7th Houses (servants and marriage). It follows the medieval tradition of using planetary "aspects" (angles between planets) to predict fortune or misfortune.</meta>

born at that hour, year, or time, he shall have children.

# Concerning the joy of the one born with children. Chapter 166.

**W**hen Saturn in a day-birth <note>original: "nativitate diurna"</note> beholds Venus with a trine aspect <note>An angle of 120 degrees, considered very harmonious</note> with reception: the one born shall rejoice in his children and shall have good and fortune because of them. When Jupiter beholds Venus with a trine aspect: the one born shall pursue good and joy from his children. If Jupiter is in the middle of the sky <note>The Midheaven or 10th House, representing career and public standing</note>, the one born shall have good and fortune by occasion of his children. When Venus is in the Ascendant <note>The 1st House, representing the self</note> and Jupiter is in the West <note>The 7th House</note>: the one born shall have children with whom he will rejoice and lead a joyful life. If the "Part of Children" <note>term: Part of Children (A calculated mathematical point in a horoscope used to predict matters regarding offspring)</note> is in the Ascendant, and its Lord is strong, fortunate, and free from misfortunes: the one born shall have children who honor him, and by their occasion, they shall provide fortune and elevation.

# Concerning the labor of the one born with a child or children. Chapter 167.

**W**hen Saturn is in his triplicity <note>A group of three zodiac signs belonging to the same element</note> and Mars beholds him with a trine aspect: the one born shall labor with his children, and sadness and worry shall multiply. When Saturn is opposite to Mars: the one born shall not rejoice with his children, but his worries shall multiply. When Saturn is in a nocturnal birth in the western angle: the one born shall labor with his children. When Saturn is in the house of Venus: the one born shall incur worries and falls by occasion of his children. When Saturn is in the Ascendant and the Moon is in the West, and the "Misfortunes" <note>term: Misfortunes (The planets Saturn and Mars, traditionally viewed as bringing challenges)</note> behold him without the aspect of the "Fortunes" <note>term: Fortunes (The planets Jupiter and Venus, viewed as bringing luck)</note>: the one born shall have little joy with his wife and children and shall be laborious with them through his whole life. When Venus is in the Ascendant and Saturn is in the West: the one born shall grieve by occasion of his children. When no planet beholds the Part of Children, nor touches it: he shall be sad with his children, and his first child shall die quickly or perhaps be miscarried; evil and sadness shall multiply for the one born with them. When a Misfortune is joined to the Part of Children and stands in a bad aspect: the one born shall have few children and modest joy with them. When the Part of Children is in the 7th House: the children shall hate their parents and do them evil. When the Lord of the 5th House is in the 12th House, and the Lord of the 12th is in the 5th: the one born shall have an impediment by occasion of his children and shall see things concerning them that will not please him. When the Lords of the 5th and 11th Houses and the Part of Fortune are in the 2nd House: the one born shall nourish the children of others; judge the same if the Lord of the 5th House is in the 2nd House. When Venus and Mercury are in the 4th House, and the sign of the 4th House is the exaltation or house of Jupiter: the one born shall nourish the children of great men or of certain princes.

# Concerning the Fortune of the Children of the one born. Chapter 168.

**W**hen Jupiter is in the Ascendant and in any of his dignities: the one born shall have high and famous children, so that few in the town shall be more noble. If the Part of Children is in the Ascendant: the children of the one born shall have good and honor, and the one born shall be honored by their occasion. If the Part of Children is strong in the 2nd House and has some dignity in it: the children shall have acquaintance with kings and high men. And there shall be great fame of him in his regions, especially if the Lord of the said Part is a Fortune and free from Misfortunes. When the Part of Children is strong and fortunate in the 3rd House: the life of the children shall be beautiful. When the Part of Children is in the 4th House and the Fortunes behold it, and the Misfortunes are falling away from it: the children shall possess money from their grandfathers and ancestors, and from there pursue fame and height. When the Part of Children is in the 5th House, and Jupiter and Venus behold it or are joined with it: the children shall have fortune and fame, and shall be named among their lineage. When the Part of Children is in the 6th House and in aspect of the Fortunes: the children shall obtain servants from their handmaids or beasts, or they themselves shall be doctors of men or beasts. When the Part of Children is fortunate in the 8th House: the children shall inherit money. When the Part of Children is in the 9th House and its Lord is free from Misfortunes: the one born shall have good, simple, and religious children. When the Part of Children is in any of its dignities, strong and fortunate, and the Misfortunes are falling away: the children shall be famous and rich. When the Significator <note>The planet representing a specific person or topic</note> of children is in a good place and the Lord of the Ascendant is unfortunate in the 10th House: the children shall be more noble than the one born himself, yet they shall always obey him. Look also to the Part of Males and Females, and according to their strength and weakness, judge the elevation and strength of the children.

# Concerning the labor of the Children of the one born. Chapter 169.

**W**hen Mars and Venus are with the Moon or behold her: the children shall incur labor, foolishness, and baseness. If Mars is in the Ascendant and the Moon is in the West: the one born shall have base and sad children. If Jupiter and Saturn are in the Ascendant and Mars, Venus, and Mercury are in the middle of the sky: the one born shall have base children. If the Part of Children is in the 4th House (which is also the house of enmity of children): the children shall be in labor and have many enemies. And if the Lord of the Part of Children is unfortunate by Mars: the children shall have many worries and incur prisons and things they would not want. When the Lord of the Part of Children is unfortunate in the 6th House: the one born shall have children suffering from chronic sickness and who live in poverty and want. When the Part of Children is unfortunate in the 10th House: the one born shall have the worst children and shall nourish them basely, and they shall act fornicatingly and do evil things, and he shall speak ill of them. When the Lord of the Part of Children is unfortunate: the children shall do his works <note>likely meaning menial labor</note>. When the Part of Children is in the 11th House: the children shall be enemies to one another and of bad character. And if the said Part is unfortunate and hindered: they shall be fornicators. When the Lord of the 5th House and the Lord of the Part of Children are under the rays of the Sun <note>term: Under the rays (A planet so close to the sun that its influence is weakened or "hidden")</note>: the one born shall have hidden children or those from adultery. If Saturn and Mars behold the Moon with a trine aspect: the one born shall buy his children <note>possibly referring to ransoming or adoption</note> nor will he confess that they are his children. If the Almuten <note>term: Almuten (The planet with the most dignity or power over a specific point in the chart)</note> or Significator of children is unfortunate and in a bad place, wandering and in its descent: it portends the fall, labor, and poverty of the children.

# Concerning the death of Children. Chapter 170.

**W**hen Saturn and Mars are in the house of children or behold it with a bad aspect: the children shall die. When the Lord of the 11th House is in the 5th House, and the Lord of the Part of Fortune is in the 5th: all the children shall die. When the Sun is joined to any Misfortune: the children shall die before him and none shall remain to him. When the Sun, Saturn, and Mercury behold the Moon from the middle of the sky: the children shall die. When Mars beholds Jupiter with an opposite aspect: the children shall be killed or perish by an evil death. When Saturn is in opposite aspect to Jupiter: the greater part of the children shall die. When Mercury is in the Ascendant and Saturn in the West: the children shall live little. When the Lord of the house of children and the Part of Children are in the 8th House <note>The House of Death</note>: the children shall die before him. When Mars and Mercury are in the West or middle of the sky: the one born shall see the death of his children. When Jupiter is in the 4th or 7th House joined to any Misfortune or applies a bad aspect, and is burnt <note>combust</note> or descending: it portends the death of children. When Misfortunes are joined to the Lord of the house of children or behold him with a bad aspect: they signify the death of children and the cutting off of their fame. When the Lord of the house of children is bound to the Lord of the 8th House: the children shall die and their fame shall be cut off. When the Lords of the triplicity of Jupiter are burnt or descending or in conjunction or bad aspect of Misfortunes: they indicate the death of the children. When Venus is falling from the Fortunes and the Misfortunes behold her: the children shall die. When the Part of Children is in the house of Saturn and the Misfortunes behold it: it prenounces the death of a great part of the children.

# Chapter on the 6th House.

**L**ook to the 6th House and its Lord, and also the Lord of the triplicity of the 2nd House, and Mercury, and the Part of Servants and its Lord: and see which planet is the Almuten over these places. If this Almuten is also the Almuten of the one born, he shall have his own servants, otherwise not: and according to the quality of these Almutens, you shall judge their state toward the one born and the one born toward them. And what we have determined in the preceding sections regarding the significations of this house shall suffice.

# Chapter on the 7th House.

**L**ook for marriage and its state from the 7th House and its Lord: from Venus and the Moon: from the Part of Marriage and its Lord: and also from the planets existing in the 7th House. For when a planet which has many testimonies in the said places is in an angle or succeeding house, free from Misfortunes, retrogradation, and combustion, and applies to the Lord of the Ascendant: the one born shall attain a good and laudable marriage. And if the said planet or the Lords of the triplicities of the said places are weak, hindered, and unfortunate: they portend an impediment of marriage and its imperfection, as well as heaviness and sadness. If Venus is made unfortunate by Mars: it signifies fornication. And if Venus is in a masculine sign: the one born shall be a public fornicator or a sodomite. And if in the birth of women Venus is made unfortunate by Mars and is in a masculine sign: the woman born shall be a harlot. And if she is in a masculine sign: she shall be a sodomite. When Venus is in Saturn: the one born shall be of bad intercourse and of children...

### On those born lacking a wife. Chapter 171.

**W**hen the primary testimonies of the planets which signify marriage are present, but they provide many testimonies and the Part [of Marriage] in the nativity is not linked, the native shall not enter into a union. When the Part of Marriage is in a bad place, and Venus is in the sign of Taurus <note>The symbol .τ. represents Taurus</note> and in masculine signs, the native will lack a wife. When the lords of the triplicity of Venus do not behold Venus nor the Midheaven <note>term: Midheaven (Medium Coeli) - the highest point in the sky at birth, relating to public life</note>, the native shall have no wife. When the first of the lords of the triplicity of Venus and Mars is in the term of the malefics <note>term: Malefics - "bad" planets, usually Saturn and Mars</note>, or in the 4th house as a pilgrim <note>term: Pilgrim - a planet in a position where it has no essential dignity or strength</note> and unfortunate, the native will not desire women nor take a wife. When Venus is unfortunate and beholds the Moon, and the Ascendant and Saturn are pilgrims falling from the aspect of Jupiter, the native will lack a wife. Where the Part of Marriage is in the 12th house and the malefics behold it without the aspect of the benefics <note>term: Benefics - "good" planets, usually Jupiter and Venus</note>, and its lord beholds it but is combust <note>term: Combust - so close to the Sun that its power is "burned up"</note> or hindered by the Ascendant or some malefic, the native shall be without a wife and his seed shall be hindered.

### On the marriage of beauty and good women. Chapter 172.

**W**hen the lord of the 7th house is in the 7th house, the native will take an honest and simple wife. When Jupiter, Venus, and Mercury are in the 7th house, the native will have a beautiful and notable wife, as well as goodness and joy on her account. When the lord of the 7th house is in the 9th, the native will take a foreign wife, yet one who is good and religious. And if they have any of their own dignities and the benefics behold them, and the malefics are falling away from them, the native will be good and famous and will take a noble wife. When Venus is in the Midheaven and the benefics behold her without the aspect of a malefic, the native will take noble wives. When the lord of the house of the Sun <note>The Sun's house is Leo</note> is not combust and is in the 7th house and in his own house, and the benefics from the angles or succedent houses behold him, the native will have wives who are beautiful and great of body. And if Jupiter beholds them there, they will be noble and honest. When the Part of Marriage is strong and fortunate in the 3rd house, and its lord is free from malefics, the native will take a foreign wife and will have good and perfect success through her. When the Part of Marriage is in the 10th house and free from malefics, the native will take a good, famous, and honest wife. When the said Part is fortunate in the 10th house, the wife of the native will be the daughter of great and sublime men. When the Part of Marriage is in the 5th house, free from malefics and fortunate, the native will take a wife from among his own relatives; she will have a good and beautiful manner and wealth in her lineage, and she will be younger than the native and delicate in her life. When Jupiter beholds the Moon in a trine or quartile aspect and Venus is in the 8th or 12th, the native will take a wife of good lineage and will see her [good] character. When Jupiter, being the lord of the Part of Marriage, is in the 7th house, the native will take a wife with joy and will be a drunkard. And if Mercury is in the place of Jupiter, the marriage will be characterized by laughter and cunning.

### On the marriage of wicked women and harlots. Chapter 173.

**W**hen Mercury is with Venus in the 4th angle <note>The Imum Coeli or bottom of the chart</note> and Mars beholds them, the native will have a wicked and sorcerous wife. And if Venus is in an angle in Cancer or Capricorn, the native will take a wife who is a harlot and wicked. When Venus is in the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house</note> and in Aquarius, and the Moon beholds her with a bad aspect, the native will take a harlot for a wife. When Venus is in the 6th house, the native will lie with a harlot and will have evil and sadness because of women. When Venus is in the 12th house, the native will take a wife who is wicked and inferior to himself. When Mercury is in the 7th house, the native will have a sorcerous wife. When the Sun is in the 7th house, the wife of the native will be disobedient to him, an adulteress not caring whether she is spoken well of, and she will frequent many places. When the lord of the 7th house beholds the lord of the 8th, the native will take a wife without roots <note>meaning without family or stable background</note>, especially if the sign of the 7th house is Gemini, and he will commonly have two wives, and if noble, many more. When the Head of the Dragon <note>term: Head of the Dragon - the North Node of the Moon</note> is with Saturn and Mercury in the 7th house, the native will take a wife who is a widow or one who has had many men. When the Part of Marriage is in a shameful place, the native will take a wife who is infamous, or stained, or having an inseparable sickness.

### On the marriage of the native with the aged, the ugly, and the sterile. Chapter 174.

**W**hen Capricorn is the sign of the 7th house, and Saturn and Mars are in it or behold it, the native will take a wife who is aged, yet beautiful and wealthy. When the lords of the triplicity of Venus are unfortunate before the angles, or combust, or hindered, they signify the burden of marriage and sadness, and that the native will take an old woman, or a handmaid, or a sinner, and there will be little love for her. When Venus is with Saturn in her own term, or Saturn beholds her, the native will take an old woman, corrupt and having some visible deformity. When Jupiter or Venus is under the rays of the Sun in a sterile sign, the native will take an old wife. When Saturn, being the lord of the Part of Marriage, is in the 7th house, the native will take an aged wife. When Saturn is the lord of the 7th house, the native will take a wife from his own lineage. When either of the two superior planets <note>Saturn or Jupiter</note> is in the western angle <note>the 7th house</note> and has testimony in the marriage, the native will take the aged and sterile; especially if the said planet is western in the 7th house. When Saturn joins the testimonies of the planets of marriage and is western, it signifies a marriage of the aged and sterile. **On the marriage of the native with handmaids. Chapter 175.**

**W**hen Venus is in the 12th house and Saturn and Mars behold her, the native will take wives who are handmaids or harlots. When the lord of the Ascendant is in the 12th house, the native will have a wife who is a handmaid or a harlot. When the lord of the 8th house is in the west, the native will take a handmaid for a wife. When the Part of Marriage is in the 2nd house and its lord is free from malefics and has some dignity in its place, the native will lie with the handmaids of nobles and will have riches therefrom. When the Part of Marriage is unfortunate in the 6th house, the native will lie with handmaids and captives. When the lord of the 7th house is in the 12th house, and the lord of the 12th is in the 7th, the native will have and take a handmaid as a wife. When Saturn is with Venus in her house and in the Ascendant, the native will be joined with a woman who is a handmaid and wicked. And if Venus, being in her own house, is with Saturn in the Ascendant, the native will be joined with many handmaids or with foul and vile women. When Saturn is in the western angle or the Midheaven, and also in Leo, Capricorn, or Aquarius, and Jupiter or the Moon does not behold him, the native will take a handmaid or a harlot as a wife. When Mars beholds him there, the native will incur detriment because of this. When Venus is in the house of Mercury, he will take a wife or a handmaid even beyond his lineage. When Venus is in the house of the Moon <note>Cancer</note>, the native will lie with harlots and handmaids.

### On those born marrying against the law: or lying with prohibited women. Chapter 176.

**W**hen Venus and Mercury are in the Ascendant and Saturn or Venus are the lords of the Ascendant, he will lie with sisters or daughters. Judge the same if Venus and Saturn are in the Ascendant and Venus is their lord of the Ascendant. When the sign of the 3rd house is Aries or Scorpio and Venus and Mars behold them, the native will lie with the wives of his brothers or his friends. When the Part of Marriage is in the 5th house, the native will sometimes take one of his blood relatives as a wife, or lie with the wives or sisters of his brothers, or with those prohibited by law. And if the said Part is fortunate there, the native will lie with his own daughters. When the Part of Marriage is unfortunate in the 4th house, the native will lie with his mother or paternal aunt, or with his father's mistress. When the Part of Marriage is joined with a malefic and the Moon is in the 7th house or beholds it, the native will lie with his maternal aunt <note>original: "matertera .i. cum sorore matris sue"</note>. When the Part of Marriage is unfortunate in the 5th house and the benefics are falling from it, the native will lie with his daughters or granddaughters. And similarly, when the Part of Children is in the 7th house, the native will lie with someone who is like a daughter to him. When the Moon beholds the Part of Marriage and the lord of the said Part is with her, the native will lie with maternal aunts or relatives of the mother. When Mars beholds this without the aspect of Jupiter, his deed shall be revealed, and he shall be defamed for it. When the Sun is in the house of Jupiter, the native will fornicate with the wife of his son or with the sisters of his brothers or relatives. When Venus is in the house of Saturn, and especially in Capricorn, he will fornicate with the mistresses of his father or brothers, or with his own mother. When the Moon is in the house of Venus with Mars and the Part of Marriage is in the 4th or in opposite aspect to them, the native will lie with the mistresses of his father or brothers. When the Sun is in the term of Venus, the native will take a woman who is a handmaid or prohibited. When Venus is in the house or exaltation of the Moon, the native will take his blood relative as a wife. And say the same if Venus is in her own house or exaltation. When the Moon is in the 7th house and in a fixed sign, and Venus and the Sun behold her with a bad aspect, the native will have two wives.

### On the fortune of the native concerning women. Chapter 177.

**W**hen Mercury is in the Ascendant and in a fixed sign, especially...

If Venus is in her own house and free from the **malefics** <note>original: "infortuniis" - planets like Saturn or Mars that were thought to bring bad luck</note>, the person born will pursue goodness and fortune from women and will find joy with them. When Venus is in the house of Jupiter and free from the malefics, the honor of the person born will come by occasion of women, and he will be fortunate with them.

### On the Misfortune of the Person Born Concerning Women. <margin>Chapter 178.</margin>

**W**hen Venus is in the 12th house, the person born will have a bad wife and will suffer sadness, loss, and much evil on her account. When Venus is in the 2nd house during a daytime birth and the malefics aspect her, the person born will pursue evil, sadness, and loss by occasion of women. When Venus is in any of her dignities during a nighttime birth and is in the 3rd house, and Saturn and Mars are joined with her or aspect her with a bad aspect, the person born will not be able to stay with a single woman, and will thus have evil, sadness, and loss—especially if Venus is in a **mutable sign** <note>Signs like Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, or Pisces, associated with changeability</note>. When Venus is in the **angle of the earth** <note>The 4th house, representing the lowest point of the sky</note> and the malefics aspect her, the person born will be malicious and will have little joy with women. When the Moon is full of light and joined to Mars in the house of Venus or in the 6th house, the person born will incur evil, sadness, and labor by occasion of women. When Venus is in the 9th house and in any of her dignities and the malefics aspect her, the person born will pursue evil, sadness, and pain by occasion of women and will take an inferior wife. When the **Part of Marriage** <note>original: "pars proventui" - a calculated mathematical point in a horoscope related to marriage</note> is hindered and unfortunate in the 7th house, the person born will suffer evil and loss on the part of women. When the Part of Marriage is unfortunate in the 9th house, women will not seek the person born, but will hold him in hatred, and he will have evil and pain from them. When the **Part of Fortune** of marriage is unfortunate in the 12th house, the person born will labor with women or wives and will have evil and sadness therefrom. When the Part of Fortune is unfortunate, the person born will incur anxiety and sadness because of women.

### On Those Born Who Secretly Commit Fornication with Women. <margin>Chapter 179.</margin>

**W**hen the lord of the 7th house is in the angle of the earth or **under the rays of the sun** <note>Too close to the sun to be seen, signifying secrecy</note>, the person born will secretly commit fornication with women. When the lord of weddings is in the house of weddings, the person born will hide his fornication. When Mars is the lord of the Part of Weddings and is in the 7th house, the person born will secretly commit fornication with someone of his own lineage. When Saturn and Mars are in the 6th house, the person born will hide his marriage or fornication; especially if he aspects neither the Part of Marriage nor the 7th house and its lord, the fornication of the person born will be hidden. But if he aspects the said places, it will be made manifest. When Venus is in the 3rd house and is **occidental** <note>Setting after the sun</note>, being in her own **term** <note>A specific degree-based subdivision of a zodiac sign</note> or in the term of Jupiter, the person born will take a wife secretly; he will nevertheless have good from her, as well as riches and fortune.

### On Husbands Whose Wives Die First. <margin>Chapter 180.</margin>

**W**hen the Moon is in the 4th house, the wives of the person born will die and he will be grieving therefrom. When Venus is in the angle of the earth and Saturn and Mars aspect her, the wives of the person born will die, and their death will be hidden. And if that sign is mutable, many wives of the person born will die before him. When the lord of the 7th house is in the 8th house, the wives of the person born will die before him and he will remain alone. When the Moon, separated from Mars, applies to Venus while she is in the west, the person born will have many wives, but they will die before him. When Saturn is in the 7th house during a nighttime birth and Venus and the Moon are joined to him, it signifies the death of the wife of the person born; it also indicates a hindrance to marriage and the destruction of the house in which he was born. When Venus is in the 7th house and the malefics aspect her without the aspect of the **benefics** <note>Planets like Jupiter or Venus that bring good luck</note>, the person born will see the death of his wives. When the lord of the 7th house is **combust** <note>Burnt up by the sun's proximity</note> or descending or unfortunate in the 2nd house, the wives of the person born will die before him, because the 2nd house is the 8th from the 7th <note>In astrology, the 8th house from any house represents the death of that house's subjects</note>. When one of the malefics is in the 4th or 7th house, the person born will see the death of his wife. When Venus is occidental and joined to a malefic, it portends the swift death of the wife. When the lord of the 7th house is made unfortunate by the aspect of Mars and Mercury and Jupiter does not aspect, the person born will kill his wife out of jealousy. When the Part of Marriage and Mars are in the same sign and an unfortunate Mercury aspects them, the person born will suffocate his wives or kill them treacherously and deceive them. When Mars and Venus are in the 12th house, the person born will kill his wife. When Venus is in the house of Mars, the person born will have evil from women and will kill them because of fornication and jealousy.

### On the Number of Wives of the Person Born. <margin>Chapter 181.</margin>

**A**ccording to **Dorotheus** <note>Dorotheus of Sidon, a 1st-century astrological poet</note>, the number of planets existing between the **Midheaven** <note>The highest point of the chart</note> and Venus signifies the number of wives of the person born. And if it is the birth of a woman, count from the Midheaven to Mars. But if Mars is in the Midheaven, count from Mars to Jupiter; and according to the number of planets existing between them, such will be the number of husbands of the woman born.

### On the Time When the Person Born Shall Marry. <margin>Chapter 182.</margin>

**W**hen Jupiter applies to the degree of the Part of Marriage by **profection** <note>original: "asthazir" - a method of advancing chart points by time</note>, or the Part of Marriage to the degree of Jupiter, or the lord of the 7th house to the degree of the lord of the Ascendant, or when Jupiter applies to the degree of Venus, giving one year for each degree: all these signify that marriage will occur at that time. When the year of the revolution reaches from the Ascendant to the 2nd sign, the person born will marry in that year. And if that sign is mutable, the person born will make many marriages. And if it is **common** <note>Another term for mutable</note>, he will have two wives. And if **fixed**, only one.

### Chapter on the 8th House, which is called the House of Death. <note>original: "Azortis" - likely a corruption of the Arabic term for the 8th house</note>

**I**t should be known that the deaths of men are of many kinds. There are some who want death and yet fear it. And some who love death and do not fear it. And some are fortunate in death, that is, having a beautiful death. And some have death through pain, evil, and labor. There are also those who kill themselves and some who die a bad death. And some finish their life elegantly in their own bed. And some die in a foreign land. And others in their own: and we shall see concerning these particularly in what follows. When Aries is ascending, Mars, which is its lord, will be the significator of death, because in Scorpio it will be in the 8th house, of which Mars is the lord. The person born will die because of Mars in his own house, and if he is similarly litigious because of Mars, he will have great strength as the cause of his death; likewise, the disputes, wars, and robberies of those born will bring them to death. Similarly, when Libra is ascending, Taurus will be the sign of the house of death and Venus the mistress; because she is the mistress of death and life, it signifies that the person born will bring death upon himself. Similarly, women in whose births Libra is ascending will bring death upon themselves through fornication, adultery, and other troubles; and perhaps sexual intercourse will be the cause of their death, because those with Libra ascending desire this more. When Sagittarius is ascending, whose lord is Jupiter, the sign of his exaltation—namely Cancer—will be in the 8th house; and because Jupiter signifies the religious, the faithful, clerics, hermits, and men praying to God, it is a sign that the death of the person born will be good and honorable, and also that his soul, by the will of God, will be placed in the **bosom of Abraham** <note>A traditional Christian expression for paradise</note>.

### On Those Born Fearing Death. <margin>Chapter 183.</margin>

**T**hese are the Mercurial types, because the house of their death is of Saturn or Mars, which signify fear and laborious and bad deaths. The places of the significator of death are these: the Ascendant and its lord; the sign of the 8th house and its lord and the malefics existing therein; the Part of Death and its lord; the lord of the first **triplicity** <note>A group of three zodiac signs of the same element</note> of the angle of the earth; the eighth sign from the Sun; the eighth sign from the Moon; the lord of the term; the 7th house, the Moon, and the lord of its house. Know these places and look at the planets that join the testimonies of these places; and thus you will see what kind of death and nature they have concerning the death of the person born.

judge. **Concerning those born who will die from some sickness. Chapter 184.**
When Mars or the Sun, being the lord of the 8th house <note>The 8th house is traditionally associated with death and inheritance.</note>, is in a humid sign: the native will die from a hot and humid sickness, that is, from a flux of the belly <note>likely dysentery or diarrhea</note> and similar things. When Mars, being under the rays <note>meaning too close to the sun to be seen</note>, is the lord of the 7th house: the native will die from pain in the belly or in some other member of his body. When the Sun is eclipsed in the 7th house and the lord of that angle is in his station or retrograde: the death of the native will be from a chronic sickness. When the Moon is unfortunate in the western angle <note>the 7th house</note>: the native will die of a hot and humid sickness, or from foul and festering cancers. When the Sun is in the Ascendant and the Moon is in the West, and Mars is in the Midheaven or in the angle of the earth <note>the 4th house</note>, and Jupiter has no testimony there nor regards Mars: the native's death will be from burnt humors. When the Moon is with Mars in the 7th house: the native will die from a flux of blood through the anus or another place.

### Concerning those born who will die an evil death. Chapter 185.

When the Moon is in the 7th house and the misfortunes <note>the "malefic" planets, Saturn and Mars</note> regard her: the native will die an evil death. When the Moon is in the 12th house and the misfortunes regard her: the native will die an evil death; and this indication will be stronger if one of the said misfortunes is the lord of the 7th house. When Saturn and Mars and Mercury are with Jupiter and Venus in the 4th house: the native will die an evil and foul death. When Mars is in the 7th house and the Moon regards him: the native will die an evil death. When the misfortunes are descending after the 7th house and the Moon regards them from any angle: it signifies an evil death for the native. When the lords of the triplicities of the 4th house are in bad places and the misfortunes regard them: the native will be burdened at the departure of his soul. When Saturn is in a nocturnal nativity in the 8th house and the fortunes <note>the "benefic" planets, Jupiter and Venus</note> do not regard him: the native will die an evil death.

### Concerning those born who will die a sudden death. Chapter 186.

The native will incur a sudden death when the Moon is in a nocturnal nativity in the 8th house and the misfortunes regard her: the native will die a sudden death. When the first lord of the triplicity of the angle of the earth is outside his own house and the 8th house is unfortunate and the fortunes are falling away from it: the death of the native will be sudden. When Mars is in the house of the Moon <note>Cancer</note>: the native will die suddenly by iron <note>a blade</note> or by the shedding of blood.

### Concerning those born who will die in water. Chapter 187.

When Saturn is in the 4th house, setting, and in a moveable sign, and Mars regards him: the native will die in water. When the sign of the 4th house, the part of death, and its lord, as well as Saturn and the first lord of the triplicity are in watery signs, or the greater part of them: the native will die in water. Unless the aforementioned things which provide testimony of the said times are in a watery sign: the death of the native will be in water. When the lord of the 4th house is in the 7th house and in a watery sign: the native will die in water or because of too much drinking of water. When Saturn, Mars, and the Moon are in the Midheaven and in a watery sign: the death of the native will be in water or from the clouds of Heaven.

### Concerning those born who will die by fire. Chapter 188.

When the lord of the 4th house is in the 7th house and in a fiery sign, and the fortunes regard him from fiery signs: the native will die by fire. When the Moon is with Mars in the 8th house: the native will be burned. When all the parts signifying death are in a fiery sign: the native will die by fire. When the aforementioned things which provide testimony of the aforementioned signs of death are in a fiery sign: the native will be burned. When the Moon is the mistress of the 7th house and Mars and the Sun regard her from the 7th and 10th houses: the native will be burned. When the Moon is in the sign of the angles or the succeeding houses and in a fiery sign, and the misfortunes regard her from fiery signs without the aspect of Jupiter: the native will die by fire.

### Concerning those born who will die by falling from heights. Chapter 189.

When the Sun is peregrine <note>in a sign where it has no essential dignity</note> and unfortunate in the 8th house, and the misfortunes regard the lord of the 8th house: the native will fall from a high place or die. When the Moon is in any of the angles or succeeding houses, and Jupiter is peregrine in the 2nd or 12th house and in an earthy sign: the native will die by falling from a height. When Saturn is the lord of the 8th house: he will fall and die. When Jupiter and Mars are in the West and do not regard the angle of the earth: the native will die by falling from a height. When the Moon is in the Midheaven and the misfortunes are in opposition to her: a house or a wall or a tree will fall upon the native and he will die.

### Concerning those born who will die by poison or toxic substances. Chapter 190.

When the lord of the 8th house is peregrine and made unfortunate by Mars or Saturn, and is in Cancer, Scorpio, or Pisces: the native will be struck by a serpent or die by poison or toxic substance. When Venus, being the mistress of the 8th house, is impeded: the native will die by poison given by women, or will take a medicine that causes him to die. When Venus, being the mistress of the 8th house, is unfortunate and likewise the sign of the 8th house is unfortunate: the native will die from drunkenness or too much drinking of wine or the taking of poison. When Venus is the mistress of death or of the aforementioned places signifying death, and is free from all harm: the native will die of pain in the belly. And if she is impeded: the native will perish because of an excess of medicine or commonly. When the Tail <note>the South Node of the Moon, or Cauda Draconis</note> is in the 4th house with Mercury: the native dies without medicine, or will end his life through necromancy. When the Moon is in the 8th house with the Tail of the Dragon: the native will die by poisonous purgatives or a death-bearing draught.

### Concerning those born who will be eaten or killed by beasts. Chapter 191.

When the lord of the 12th house is with Saturn in the Ascendant: beasts will eat the native, yet he himself will love hunting and dogs. When the Part of Fortune is in the 2nd house and a misfortune is ascending and regards the said part: the native will be eaten by wild beasts or dogs. When Mars, being the lord of the 8th house, is joined with the Moon in the sign of a beast: animals will eat the native and kill him. When the Moon is in any angle or succeeding house and Jupiter is peregrine in an angle and in a quadruped sign, especially in Leo: the native will be eaten or killed by wild beasts. When the Moon is joined with Saturn and Mars in the Midheaven and in a quadruped sign or in Virgo: beasts will eat and kill the native.

### Concerning those born who will die by the sword or be hanged. Chapter 192.

When the lord of the house of life is in the 7th house and Mars is the lord of the 7th house: the neck of the native will be cut off. When Mars is in the 10th house and the Moon is in the 7th, and the misfortunes do not regard them: the native will be killed by enemies or thieves. When the Moon, being the mistress of the 7th house, is in some way unfortunate or impeded by combustion, and the Sun is unfortunate in the 6th house: the native will be killed by order of the king. When one of the misfortunes is in the Midheaven and another in the angle of the earth: the native will be killed or hanged. When the Moon is in the Ascendant and Saturn in the Midheaven and Mars in the angle of the earth without the aspect of any fortune: the native will be killed or beheaded and afterwards hanged. When Mars is with the Head of the Dragon <note>the North Node, or Caput Draconis</note> in the 8th house and hinders the lord of the 8th: the native will die in prison. When Mars is in the 8th house and hinders the lord of the 8th: the death of the native will be by iron. When any of the misfortunes in a diurnal nativity is in the 8th sign from the Sun, or in a nocturnal nativity in the 8th sign from the Moon: it signifies the same as what was signified in the 8th house from the Ascendant. When the degree of the 7th house is impeded, and the lord likewise: it signifies a most wretched death. When the luminaries <note>the Sun and Moon</note> are with the Head of Algol <note>a fixed star in Perseus associated with decapitation</note> and the native there regards it with a bad aspect: the native will be hanged with hands and feet cut off. When the Sun is eclipsed in the 7th house after the conjunction of the Moon: the native will be killed by robbers or plunderers. When Mars is in the Midheaven in a diurnal nativity and the Moon is in Scorpio, and none of the fortunes regard it from an angle nor are bound there: the native will be killed by enemies or thieves. When the Tail of the Dragon, Jupiter, Venus, and Saturn are in the 8th house: the native will be killed and perhaps beheaded or hanged without cause. When Saturn is in the Ascendant and the Moon in the West, and Mars regards them from the 4th house: the native will be killed or hanged. When Saturn with Mars is in the West or the angle of the earth: the native will be killed by the sword. When Saturn, Jupiter, and Mercury are in the West: the native will be killed in war. When the misfortunes regard the part of death without the aspect of the fortunes: the native will be killed. When the lord of the part of death is in opposition to the lord of the 8th house and in a sign inimical to him: the native will be killed. When Saturn and the Moon are in the West and Mars is in the angle of the earth: the native will be killed or hanged. When Mars is in the 8th house or hinders the eighth house: the native will die by iron.

### Concerning those born who will kill themselves. Chapter 193.

[When they] arise and Saturn and Mars regard them, provided that one of them is the lord of the 2nd house: the native will hang himself. When the 7th house and its lord are made unfortunate and the said lord is in the Ascendant: the native will kill himself. When the lord or the Ascendant or the 7th house does not regard the Moon: the native will kill himself. When the lord of the Ascendant applies to the lord of the house of death, or the lord of the house of death or the lord of the 7th house is in the Ascendant and does not regard the Moon: the native will kill himself. When Saturn and Mars are in the 10th house, and the lord of the Ascendant is unfortunate and the Moon is separated from the lord of the Ascendant [and] the Ascendant...

When Mars is in the Ascendant and the Moon is in the West <note>The 7th House</note>, and the fortunes <note>Benefic planets like Jupiter or Venus.</note> do not behold them: the native shall kill himself.

### Concerning those born who will die on account of women. Chapter 194.

When the Part of Marriage is in the 8th House <note>The house of death.</note> and its lord is unfortunate: the native shall die on account of women. When Venus, the lady of marriage, being in the 6th house, is unfortunate: the death of the native shall be with a maidservant. When Venus and the Moon are peregrine <note>A planet with no essential dignity or "standing" in its current sign.</note> in the 7th house and the misfortunes <note>Malefic planets like Saturn or Mars.</note> behold her: the native shall die on account of women. And if Jupiter is in the place of Venus, the death of the native shall be with his own children. And if Mars is in the place of Venus, the native shall die on account of his brothers. And if Saturn is in the place of Venus, the death of the native shall be on account of his father. And if the Moon is in the place of Venus: the native shall die on account of his mother.

### Concerning those born whose death shall be secret. Chapter 195.

When the lord of the triplicity of the 4th house is in the 4th or 7th house: the native shall die in a secret place and nothing of him shall be found. When the lord of the eighth house is unfortunate or the 10th sign is unfortunate: the native shall die secretly on account of friends and relatives. When the lord of the 8th house and the lord of the Part of Death and the significators of death meet together, and the parts that join their testimonies are in the 4th house under the rays of the Sun <note>Combust, or hidden by the Sun's light.</note>, or the greater part of them: the native shall die secretly. When the lord of the Ascendant joins with a misfortune existing in the angle of the earth <note>The 4th house/Imum Coeli.</note>, no one shall have news of the native's death.

### Concerning those born who will die in their own land or country. Chapter 196.

When the lord of the 8th house is in the 8th house and in another of its own houses: the native shall die in his own house or country. When the sign of the 8th house and its lord are strengthened and not hindered: the native shall end his life in his own house or country. When Jupiter and Venus, without the aspect of Saturn, behold the death by a third aspect <note>A trine aspect (120 degrees).</note>: the native shall die in his own land or country. When Mars is in the Ascendant and Jupiter beholds him by a third aspect: the native shall die in his own land or country. When the Part of Death in any of the angles is safe from the misfortunes: the native shall end his days by a natural death in his own land or country.

### Concerning those born who will die outside their country or in a foreign place. Chapter 197.

When the lord of the 8th house is in the 12th house: the native shall die on a journey and shall have hindrance on account of the dead. When the lord of the 4th house is naturally an enemy to the sign of the day-house and possesses the 6th or 12th house: the native shall die in a foreign land. When the Part of Death is in the 4th and the lord is in another: the native shall die outside his own land. When the Part of Death is in the 3rd or 9th house: the native shall die outside his own land. When the Part of Death and its lord and the lord of the 7th house are in the Ascendant, or in signs in which the lord of the Ascendant has no dignity: the native shall die outside his own land. When the lord of the angle of the earth is in the 8th house: the native shall die outside his own land. When the lord of the 8th house is in the 3rd house: the native shall die outside his own land. When the Moon of a daytime birth is in the 10th house and is bound to a misfortune: the native shall die outside his own land. When Saturn is in the 4th house and Mars, being peregrine, beholds him: the native shall die outside his own land. When Jupiter is the lord of the 8th house and is also peregrine and unfortunate, and the 8th house is unfortunate: the native shall die in a foreign place because of kings or princes. When the lord of the 8th house is in the 5th house: the native shall die in a foreign place. When Venus, Saturn, Mars, and the Moon are in the 9th house: the native shall die in a foreign land from thirst or the bite of a beast. When the lord of the 8th house is peregrine: the native shall die outside his own land. When the Sun is in the middle of the sky <note>The 10th house/Midheaven.</note> and Mars beholds him with a bad aspect: the native shall die in a foreign land.

### Chapter on the 9th house, which is the significator of journeys.

In the journeys of the native, we ought to look to the sign of the 9th house and its lord, and to the sign of the 3rd house and its lord. To the Part of Journeys and its lord, and to Mars and Mercury, as well as to the parts that are in the 9th house or those which collect the testimonies of the said places. And having found these, you shall judge. When the lord of the 9th house in the Ascendant is strong and fortunate, and the misfortunes do not have testimonies with him, then the journey will be profitable for the native, and he will be joyful, clever, and wise in it, and his status and body shall be directed in that journey. When the Moon in the 3rd house is Oriental <note>Rising before the Sun.</note> and appears from under the rays of the Sun: the native shall love journeys and shall have profit from them with joy. When the Moon is in the 3rd house and Jupiter and Mars behold it with a good aspect: the native shall have joy, fortune, and utility in the journey, and shall not complete it without joy: especially if the lord of the 9th house is in a good place and

<column-break/>

in any of its dignities and the misfortunes have fallen away from it. When the Sun, Venus, and Mercury are in the 9th house: the native shall follow joy and profit in the journey; that which he loves he shall receive in it. When the Moon in the birth is in the 9th house and in a feminine sign: the native shall make many journeys, and on account of them shall pursue riches and honors, and shall be fortunate in them. When the Moon and its lord are in the place of journeys: the native shall love journeys and rejoice in them and have profit from them: especially if the Moon and Venus are fortunate, for then from his journeys he shall pursue great good and honor. When the lord of the Ascendant is in the 9th house and the lord of the 9th house is in the Ascendant: the native shall love journeys or rejoice in them. When the Moon on the third day from birth is bound to a fortune and that fortune is shown outside the rays of the sun: the native shall be fortunate in travel and shall have good from his journeys. And if the said planet is unfortunate and shown, yet in the aspect or term <note>A specific degree-division of a sign ruled by a planet.</note> of a fortune: the native shall make long journeys, and shall be a wanderer from place to place with little gain. When the lord of the 9th house in any of the angles is strong and fortunate and the fortunes are falling from it: the native in his journeys shall be fortunate and find good and fortune from them; nor shall honor and utility be removed from him as long as he involves himself in journeys.

### Concerning those born who will die in their journeys or will never return from them. Chapter 198.

When Jupiter and Venus are in the 9th house: the native shall have sons who will never return from their journeys and voyages. When Mercury, appearing from under the rays of the sun, is in the middle of the sky with Mars: the native shall go out from his land and never return to it. When Saturn is in the house of the Moon <note>Cancer.</note>: the native shall undertake a long journey from which he shall hardly or never return to his land.

### Concerning those born who will return from their journeys. Chapter 199.

When Saturn is in his own house <note>Capricorn or Aquarius.</note>: the native shall undertake a long journey but shall return to his land. When Mars is in the Ascendant and has some dignity in the house of journeys: the native shall make long journeys and return from them to his land. When Venus is with Jupiter or Mercury and with Mars, and the Moon beholds them: the native shall undertake many journeys, by which he shall return to his land.

### Concerning those born who will incur losses and misfortunes on account of journeys. Chapter 200.

When a misfortune is in the 3rd house and its lord is hindered and in a bad place: the native shall have evil and detriment on account of a journey. When the Moon of a daytime birth is in the 3rd house and in a feminine sign and a misfortune beholds her: the native shall receive little profit and much [trouble] from his journeys. When Mars and Venus are in the 3rd house and the Moon beholds them with a good aspect: the native shall make many journeys, and from them shall have many anxieties and little good. When the Moon of a daytime birth is in the 7th house: the native shall fall ill in his journeys. When Saturn, Mars, Venus, and the Moon are in the house of journeys: the native shall follow much labor and little gain from his journeys, nor shall he see anything that pleases him in them. When Mars beholds the Ascendant with a trine aspect and the Moon is joined with him: the native shall make many journeys and follow evil rather than good from them. When Mars is in the house of Mercury <note>Gemini or Virgo.</note>: the native shall undertake many journeys and incur losses on account of them. When the lord of the house of journeys, existing in a watery sign, makes the lord of the Ascendant unfortunate: the native shall make a journey by sea, in which he shall suffer many evils and fears. When Mars is the lord of the house of journeys and beholds the lord of the Ascendant with a bad aspect without the fortunes: the native shall suffer many evils in his journeys, so that he will be near to death. When the lord of the triplicity of the Sun in a daytime birth, or the lords of the triplicity of the Moon in a nighttime birth, are falling from the angles and are unfortunate and do not behold the Ascendant: the native shall not be fortunate in his journeys, even if he journeys willingly. When a planet provides testimony, and the joining of the significators of journeys is in a bad and shameful place: the native shall be shamed by his journeys, and there shall be much evil and labor in them. When the lord of the house of journeys and the lord of the Part of Journeys are unfortunate in a watery sign: the native has his share of journeys in water, and from there shall follow evil and labor: especially if the misfortunes behold him, and the fortunes have fallen from them, or if Saturn is unfortunate. And if Mars is unfortunate, evil shall happen to the native and...

### Chapter 10. On the 10th House, which is the significator of the Nativity's Kingdom and Honor.

Look for the lords and honor of the newborn at the **Midheaven** <note>the highest point of the chart, relating to career</note> and its lord; likewise to the **Sun** in a daytime birth and **Saturn** in a nighttime birth. ¶ And know the **Almuten** <note>the planet with the most dignity/strength</note> over these places: for if there is a connection between the Almuten of these places and the Almuten of the Ascendant: the newborn will have a kingdom and dominion, otherwise not. ¶ Others, however, say to look at the indicator of the aforementioned places: for if the stronger among them is in its own house, and is applied to the lord of the Ascendant, or has some agreement with it: the newborn will arrive at a kingdom or dominion and will pursue honor and glory through it; but if there is no application or agreement between them: the newborn will lack a kingdom or dominion, and his possession will be modest. ¶ **Abelius** and **Albinus** <note>likely references to Hellenistic or Arabic astrologers like Albusmasar or Al-Biruni</note> said: ¶ Look at the Midheaven and its lords, as well as the Almuten of mastery and its **Part** <note>Arabic Part/Lot</note> and its lord. ¶ Then look at its nature and the places, whether there be one or many. ¶ Then look at its nature and mix it with the Almuten of the Ascendant and judge according to the ways and agreement. ¶ And if it is of the bound modes which we have noted: the newborn will have much mastery and dominion and it shall not be removed from him. ¶ Similarly, look at the Sun: for if it is in the high degree of the four western signs: the kingdom or dominion of the newborn will be at the end of his life. And if the lords of the **triplicity** <note>a group of three signs of the same element</note> are strong, his dominion or exaltation will not be removed. ¶ But if they are impeded: it will be hindered because the first lord of the triplicity of the Sun signifies dominion or kingdom around the beginning of life, the second in the middle, and the third in the final part. ¶ Certain others also said that when **Saturn** in a nighttime birth is in an **angle** <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>, and **Mars** likewise in a daytime birth is in an angle, and Mars is in the Ascendant or Midheaven: the newborn will remain in exaltation and dominion until the minor years of Saturn or Mars are completed; and after that time, he who had exaltation from Mars will incur persecutions and reproaches and perhaps be killed. ¶ But he who had exaltation from Saturn will, after that time, be imprisoned or made an exile, or die of hunger or misery.

### Chapter 11. On the 11th House, which is the significator of Friends.

Look for the friends and friendship of the newborn at the 11th house and its lord; at the **Part of Friends** and its lord; at **Venus**, **Jupiter**, and **Mars**; and at the planets existing in the 2nd house; likewise at the planets which join their testimonies to the said significators; and according to their state in their strength and weakness, or their direction and retrogradation, and so from many other states or modes, judge the state of the newborn regarding friendship.

### On the friendship of kings or illustrious men toward the newborn. Chapter 201.

When **Venus** is in the Ascendant and in any of her dignities: the newborn will have friendship with powerful men and noble women, and will be great and honored among them. ¶ When the lord of the 3rd house is in a good place from the Ascendant and in any of its dignities, and the **fortunes** <note>benefic planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> look upon it, while the **infortunes** <note>malefic planets like Mars or Saturn</note> fall away from it: the newborn will have friendship with kings and powerful men and will pursue profit and honor from them. ¶ When the sign of the 3rd house is **Leo** or **Cancer** and **Jupiter** is in it or looks upon it: the newborn will be loved by sublime men and will obtain good and fortune from them. ¶ When in a daytime birth **Sagittarius** or **Pisces** is in the 4th house and the fortunes look there, especially Jupiter, while the infortunes fall from said house: the newborn will have friendship with kings or powerful men and will be honored by them. ¶ When Jupiter is in the 4th house and in any of its dignities: the newborn, by occasion of his parents, will have friendship with kings or illustrious men and will pursue good and fortune therefrom. ¶ When Venus is fortunate in the 4th house: the newborn will have friendship with kings or powerful ones in old age, and will receive good and fortune from them at the end. ¶ When the **Sun** is in the 11th house and in its own **face** <note>a specific 10-degree division of a sign</note>: the newborn will acquire the friendship of kings and sublime ones, and they will prefer him in their affairs. ¶ When **Mars** is strong in the 11th house and free from infortunes, and a fortune—especially Jupiter—looks upon him: the newborn will be greatly honored by kings or illustrious men. ¶ When Venus is fortunate and strong in the 11th house, and free from infortunes: the newborn will be a friend of kings or princes, and will be loved and honored by powerful women.

### On the friendship of evil and base men and certain others toward the newborn. Chapter 202.

When the Part of Friends and its lord are in the 2nd, 6th, 8th, or 12th house and are unfortunate: the newborn will be a friend of evildoers or fornicators. ¶ If the Part of Friends is in a shameful place and unfortunate: the newborn will be a friend of evil and base men, and from this, infamy will arise for him. ¶ When the lord of the house of friends knocks <note>original: "pulsauerit", an astrological term for a planet applying an aspect</note> from the 3rd house to the lord of the Ascendant: the newborn will have friendship among brothers and relatives. ¶ If the said "knocker" is unfortunate: the newborn will incur evil and shame through that friendship. ¶ When the said knocker is in the 4th house: the newborn will have friendships with the elderly. ¶ If it is in the 5th house: he will be a friend of children and minors, and will follow infamy therefrom. ¶ And in the house of servants <note>the 6th house</note>: he will have friendship with servants and wretched people. ¶ And if it is in the house of journeys <note>the 9th house</note>: the newborn will acquire friends in a foreign land and will have profit therefrom. ¶ And if it is in the 12th house: his enemies will become his friends. ¶ And if the lord of the house of friends is in the 8th house: the newborn will see the death of brothers or friends.

### On the Faith and Good Friendship of the newborn. Chapter 203.

When **Aries** is the sign of the house of friends: the newborn will be of good friendship and a faithful friend, and will honor his brothers and help them in all things. ¶ When [the sign of] the house of friends [is favorable]: the newborn will be looking for friends from his companions and will love them, and there will be good profit with them. ¶ When a fortune is the lord of the Ascendant and exists in the 11th house: the newborn will profit from his friends and will have good and honor from them. ¶ When **Pisces** is the sign of friends: the newborn will have many friends and will profit them through his friendship. ¶ When the lord of the house of friends is bound to or applies to the lord of the Ascendant: the newborn will have many companions and friends who will procure his love, and he will be necessary in their business. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant applies to or is bound to the lord of the house of friends: the newborn will acquire the friendship of many because they will be necessary to him, and he will be cheerful and rejoicing in the business of his friends. ¶ When the lord of the 11th house is a fortune, and in a good place and state, and free from infortunes, and applies to the lord of the Ascendant with **reception** <note>when a planet is in a sign ruled by the planet it aspects</note>: the newborn will have love and agreement with his friends and companions, and will acquire utility from them, and they from him. ¶ When the Part of Friends is in any of the angles and in a **fixed sign** <note>Taurus, Leo, Scorpio, or Aquarius</note>, and the fortunes look upon it while the infortunes fall from it: the newborn will be of firm friendship and great profit; likewise rejoicing with friends and they with him. ¶ When the Part of Friends and its lord are good and in good places: the newborn will have many friends and companions. ¶ And if the Part and its lord are fortunate: the newborn will have utility from friends, and they from him. ¶ And if with this they are received by fortunes: the newborn will be beloved by them.

### On those born who will have discord with friends. Chapter 204.

When a planet which collects or joins the testimonies of the indicator of friendship looks upon the planet joining the testimonies with an evil aspect: the newborn will destroy the friendship of friends and will be hateful to them, and his friendship will change into enmity. ¶ When the Part of Friends is in a **mutable sign** <note>Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, or Pisces</note>: the newborn will be unfaithful and will destroy the friendship he previously had with companions or friends, and will turn himself to enmity and treachery. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant is **retrograde** in the house of friends and the lord of friends is likewise retrograde: the newborn will lose his friends, nor will he be firm in his friendship. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant hinders or unfortunates the lord of the house of friends: the friends of the newborn will receive evil and heaviness from him. ¶ And if the lord of the house of friends hinders the lord of the Ascendant: the newborn will receive evil and heaviness from his friends or companions. ¶ And if Venus fortunates another: the newborn will receive utility and help from companions and friends, and they likewise from him. ¶ When the lord of the Ascendant is separated from the lord of the 11th house: the newborn will hate friends and destroy their friendship and love to be alone. ¶ When the lord of the 11th house is separated from the lord of the Ascendant: the friends of the newborn will hold him in hatred and cut off friendship from him. ¶ When an infortune is in the 11th house: it is an indicator of evil for friends, for the newborn will have few friends. ¶ When the lord of the 11th house does not look upon the 11th house: the newborn will have neither society, friendship, nor comfort with anyone.
